**As built (BC5b, recorded):**
- **Producer registration needs a touch.** The four producers register from their static constructors, but nothing references the producer types, so the constructors never run and `BattleCoreTick.HasProducers` stays false (no BC5 slice owns `BattleCoreTick.cs` or `Plugin.cs`). The integrator/BC7 must run the class constructors once (e.g. `RuntimeHelpers.RunClassConstructor` for every `IBattleIntentProducer` type, or a reflection scan, in `BattleCoreTick`'s static constructor) before the first arbiter tick.
- **F6 (integration verify):** `OrderArbiter.Submit` drops `IntentSource.FeudReplay`, so no producer intent, claim or retry carries the Feud exemption. It is reachable only from #42: through `OrderExecutorRuntime.ExecuteInline` (with a snapshot), and through #42's no-snapshot `DirectGuarded` path (`TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite(Regiment, feudReplay: true)`; PlayerOwned and exposed player-owned descendants still refused).
- **Shared `OrderExecutorRuntime` edits:** `IssueMount` (Formation intents with `Limber`/`Unlimber` → `MountUnits(unit, 1/0)`, artillery only, formation 3 ⇔ mounted 1 per `ChangeRegimentFormation` 135440-135452), `MountUnits(unit, 1)` before a `Limber`-flagged Move, and `bombardstarttime = 0` after a 7 whose source is `ArtilleryDoctrine` (3904).
- **Group stance decision cache key.** `PopulateResolvedDecisionsForSide` keys by `Regiment.GetInstanceID()` (component); the snapshot keys by GameObject id, so `TryResolvedDecision` takes the cache key from the live unit (`BattleSnapshotBuilder.UnitAt`). If BC2b re-keys the cache to `UnitRecord.InstanceId`, switch the lookup to `own[i].InstanceId`. `PopulateResolvedDecisionsForSide` itself is unchanged (its stance mapping already yields `FinalStance`/`ChargeAction` as the producers need). Groups without a cached decision get no stance intent; vanilla's AdjustGroupAIStance decides them (coordinator decision 3). `PrePopulateResolvedDecisionsAtBattleStart` and `TacticalCacheValidator`'s resolved-cache probe are deleted.
- **Stance hold refresh (coordinator decision 1).** A Stance intent is never a Duplicate and a refresh of the already-ordered stance is exempt from the recent-order cooldown (`OrderExecutor.IsDuplicate` / `InCooldown`): `TacticalStanceWriter` decides (cheap skip while vanilla's 4264 stamp is live, claim + re-stamp when it lapsed). The group-stance rule sends an agreeing intent even for an unsettled group, as #45 re-stamped before its friction gate. Test `HeldStanceRefreshReachesTheWriter`.
- **Order friction.** #45's `TacticalOrderSettlementGate` becomes the snapshot rule "no paths and no courier order in flight" for the Scorer stance; the Safety demotion is not held back by a march (a demotion that waits for the march is too late for BC6b).
- **Artillery.** Bombard (9) stays vanilla's (its write carries `bombardposition`/`bombardrange`, 3898-3901). Retreat direction is away from the closest visible enemy (vanilla `retreatangle` is binned from true enemy slice strength, 122717/122830); the Move is immediate, line-checked, `Limber`, `ReliefOrSupport`, validated by `WaypointValidator`; vanilla's `SetMovementMode(4)` / `skipcover` from `CheckArtyFallback` 3525 are not reproduced. The point-blank watchdog does not delete vanilla's queued order: the immediate `SetWaypoint(newpath)` replaces the battery's paths and a later courier delivery only raises the status of the paths present (`SetOrderStatus` 125482), so it re-paths nothing. Limber intents are Safety (the micro doctrine limbers only to escape, so they must pass the 180 battle-s recent-order cooldown); unlimber, face and combat-behavior intents are Executor. The per-battery hold also delays a stationary battery's retreat (parity with the deleted audit); the moving-battery watchdog is not held. Quadrant threat (wheel test) = visible strength in fire range per 90° around the battery's facing; the wheel's Face heading points at the winning quadrant's strongest visible in-range enemy (vanilla micro facing turns toward one enemy unit's position, 4603), or the closest visible enemy when none is in range and it is closer than firerange × 1.5 (vanilla's turn reach, 4606) (verify r1 major; tests `ArtilleryWheelFacesTheWinningQuadrant`, `ArtilleryWheelFacesTheStrongestInTheQuadrant`, `ArtilleryFallbackWheelHonoursVanillaReach`). The deleted B7/audit inputs `TacticalArtilleryInputAdapter.Snapshot`, `IsEligible` and `ToSupportScreenInput` (test-only after the deletion) are gone; `ArtilleryAmmoRatio01` stays. Per-battery hold reads the executor's last outcome (`TryGetLastOutcome`) on the doctrine clock.
- **#44.** Only enemy reads moved to the snapshot (`SnapshotPressure` for sector and inferred strength; `SnapshotEnemyStrengthInRange` — perceived enemies within vanilla's line-of-sight reach of the group's own position (vanilla reads the group's own `unitrange.enemiesinrange`, 120558/120563), visible in full, remembered by confidence, reinforcements never — for the sector's in-range strength instead of `groupenemiesinrange`; the single implementation, BC5a's `PostureSense.EnemyStrengthInRange` calls it at its merge; and `PerceivedFlankRisk` — the group's own outflanked state over snapshot flank pressure, or BC2b's `ArmyEvidenceBuilder.MeanFlankThreatByGroup` > 0 — instead of `flanksthreated`/`outflanked`, 120566-120580); `reinforcementarrivalswithin24hrs` is the side's OWN arrivals (odds input `reinforcementStrength24h` is added to own strength; vanilla reads it for its own retreat test, 6414), so it stays.
- **#42.** Target = the snapshot's closest visible enemy inside `neededdistancefeudgroupmovement`; vanilla still rolls per eligible group first. Fails closed (coordinator decision 4): without a snapshot for the side the move goes out directly (vanilla's SetWaypoint arguments) only where `TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite(Regiment, feudReplay: true)` allows it (`BattleWriterPolicy.DecideFeudReplayPath`, test `FeudReplayFailsClosedWithoutSnapshot`); vanilla's unguarded body never runs while Commander Mode is Active. That direct `SetWaypoint` is the one Patches-side mover left in #42 — BC7's grep gate must list #42's fallback with #59/#56/#60/#44. `TacticalWlActionGuard` callers removed: #42, the deleted #45, and `TacticalObserverPatch`'s decision-matrix row (→ `TacticalWriteGuard.IsPlayerProtected`).
- **Flags for BC7:** `BattleGroupStanceMinIntervalSeconds` and `EnableArtilleryWaypointVeto` have no reader; `Plugin.TacticalOperationsLedgerAllowsWrites` is orphaned (its only reader was #45's enable gate); descriptions of `EnableArtilleryAllStanceAudit`, `ArtilleryAuditMinHoldSeconds`, `EnableTacticalArtilleryDoctrine`, `EnableWlTacticalChargeGuard` name deleted patches. First-fire ids removed: `group-stance`, `b7-bombardment`, `artillery-waypoint-veto`.

- `OrderExecutorRuntime.ExecuteInline` is now `TryPlanInline` (claim check + `OrderExecutor.Plan`, no writes) followed by `ExecutePlannedInline`; its signature and behaviour are unchanged for #42. #56 screens with `PlanCourierParity`, plans with `TryPlanInline`, and removes vanilla's path only when `RemoveVanillaPath` holds (the plan executes); any refusal (player-owned, no snapshot record, higher claim, validator, silent exit, dedupe, cooldown) leaves vanilla's own move.
- #59 reads no enemy state. The doctrine branch (B8's deny: it needed `order.Role == Reserve`, which the ledger-first resolution already reads) and its `groupenemiesinrange`/`outflanked` inputs are deleted. The gate's non-Reserve arms, odds inputs and already-engaged exemption were unreachable (the Prefix admits only Reserve-role groups; vanilla commits only unengaged units, 6129, and `inengagement` is set only by `FireBullet`, 119050) and are deleted. So are the now-uncalled `DoctrineConsumerDecisions.DecideReserve` and `OperationalReserveDoctrine` / `OperationalReserveInput` / `Decision` / `Mission`. This also removes the last copies of `SumEnemyStrengthWithinAngle` in BC5c's files, so no `SlicePressure` substitute is needed.
- The withdrawal Move is pre-checked in the producer for the silent exits (`SilentExitPrecheck`) and `WaypointValidator`; a move either refuses is not produced and neither is its CombatBehavior 2. A Move the executor skips later (dedupe, failed-write backoff) can still let behaviour 2 through, which is vanilla's default infantry combat behaviour (112945-112947). The parity intent also carries `ReliefOrSupport` (the target sits one regiment width behind the endangered sibling, inside its group's footprint).
- `OrderExecutorRuntime.IssueMove`: an `InContactWithdrawal` Move on an infantry/cavalry regiment ends in `SetMovementMode(2)`, as vanilla's fallbacks do (5178, 4898).
- `UnitRecord` gains own-side fallback inputs (builder field reads, roster signature unchanged): `CoverValue`, `CoverObject`, `FriendlyRoutedNear`, and `UnitFlags.PathInterrupted`. The withdrawal reads cover and friendly routs from them, and the stalled-fallback re-issue requires `PathInterrupted`. Enemy-derived inputs stay perceived: the retreat bearing is vanilla's `retreatangle` convention (GetAngle 151304 / 122833, walked by GetAngleTerrainPosStatic 26897-26906) from the closest visible enemy; outflanked is fog-honest flank pressure (vanilla's code 123145-123175 reads true positions); under fire is a visible first range-list enemy. B8's `lastfeudactiontime` stamp is dropped: its only reader is the feud icon (UpdateUnitIcon 121676, `ai_feudstance >= 0`), and units of a feuding group are never withdrawn.
- Producers register from their static constructors; nothing in `src` touches the types yet, so BC7 adds the touch.

PROVE-MODE: harness. Per-slice proof is `ReserveMigrationTests` (Reserve-role deny and release, courier-parity plan and refusal, withdrawal Safety move/behavior through `OrderExecutor.Plan`, fog-honest retreat bearing, release target and priority, stalled-fallback re-issue, vanilla early-exit parity, own-side snapshot fields). Runtime proof is the BC7 in-game smoke: a reserve commit converted to a courier order under order delays, a Reserve-role rollback (`[TacticalReserveDrift]`), and `[OrderArbiter]` rows with Withdrawal / ReserveRelease sources.

## BC6 — Infantry charge doctrine (spec §4b)

**Owned files:** create `Tactical/Battle/ChargeDoctrine.cs` (pure, BC6a) and `Tactical/Battle/Producers/ChargeIntentProducer.cs` (runtime, BC6b) plus its pure half `ChargeIntentProducer.Plan.cs` (BC6b, in the test csproj); test file `ChargeDoctrineTests.cs`. File a bug-fix note in `docs/bug-fixes/` for the dead vanilla `MicroAICheckForCharges` 4910 compare (BC7 docs). BC6b shared edits (integrator-assigned): `BattleCoreContracts.cs` (two `UnitRecord` fields, one `IntentFlags` value), `BattleSnapshotBuilder.Capture` (two field reads), `OrderExecutor.IsDuplicate` and `OrderExecutorRuntime.IssueOther` (the Disarm path), `PostureProducers.EnsureRegistered` (one registration line).

**Vanilla to read:** `UpdateChargeTarget` 122199-122310; `MicroAICheckForCharges` 4905-4940 (dead compare 4910); micro cycle 6 at 5617-5638; `ChangeCombatBehavior` 90944; `AdjustCavalryBehavior` 5036-5056; GamePrefs `maxchargeradius` 53232, `microaitriggerforcharge` 54776, `weightingfactorsformicroaicharge` 54777-54781.

**Interfaces — as built (BC6a + BC6b):**
```csharp
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Battle {                       // pure, ChargeDoctrine.cs
  public struct ChargeInput { public bool ConfigOn; public bool PlayerOwned; public bool IsInfantryRegiment; public int GroupAiStance;
    public bool TargetVisible; public bool TargetIsFirstInRange; public float DistanceToTarget; public float VanillaMaxChargeRadius;
    public float OwnMorale, TargetMorale, OwnFatigue; public bool TargetDisordered; public bool TargetFlanked; public bool LaneBlockedByFriendly;
    public bool Moving; public int MovementMode; public bool ChargeResolved;
    public bool WhiskeyArmed;               // BC6b: producer memory (replaces BC6a's CurrentBehaviorOrdered == 3, see As built)
    public bool IsLeading; public bool WhiskeyArmedPendingDisarm; public double SecondsSinceLastAiChargeRefusal;
    public double RefusalBackoffSeconds;    // BC6b: RefusalBackoffSeconds(timetorenewaichargecheck); 120 s floor
    public bool ExcludedByVanillaFilter;    // BC6b: permanentlydetached | isrouted | markedforrout | feud (4917/4922 filters)
    public bool OutflankedBarsCharge; }     // BC6b: nomicroaichargeifunitisoutflanked set and own outflanked or firefromflank != 0
  public enum ChargeAction { None, Charge, Revert, Disarm }
  public static class ChargeDoctrine {
    public const int AssaultStance = 4; public const int ChargeBehavior = 3; public const int RevertBehavior = 2; // vanilla cycle-6 value for stance ≥ 1
    public const float MoraleMargin = 0.15f; public const float OwnMoraleFloor = 0.5f; public const float OwnFatigueCeiling = 0.6f;
    public const int MaxLeadingRegimentsPerGroup = 2; public const double RearmAfterRefusalBattleSeconds = 120.0;
    public const int NormalMovementMode = 0, ArmedMovementMode = 3, AssaultMovementMode = 6;
    public static ChargeAction Decide(in ChargeInput input);
    public static double RefusalBackoffSeconds(float vanillaRenewHours);   // max(120, renew h × 3600)
    public static bool ChargeCycleEnded(int previousMovementMode, int movementMode);
    public static bool PendingDisarmAfter(bool pending, ChargeAction action, int movementMode);
    public static bool IsLeading(ReadOnlySpan<float> distancesToTarget, int index);
  }
  // BattleCoreContracts (additive): UnitRecord.LastAiChargeTime (lastaichargetime 110798), UnitRecord.Outflanked (outflanked 111488),
  //   UnitRecord.FireFromFlank (firefromflank 111492), UnitRecord.AiGroupId (groupaiobject 110820 instance id);
  // PlayerOwnership.ApplyFeudGroups(BattleSnapshot, ref bool[] scratch) (builder, after ApplyChainFlags), PlayerOwnership.DirectFeudFlags(own, aiGroup, snapshotFeud);
  //   IntentFlags.LeaveChargeMode = 512 (Disarm: the executor also calls SetMovementMode(0) on a regiment in mode 3).
}
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Battle.Producers {
  internal sealed partial class ChargeIntentProducer : IBattleIntentProducer {   // Name "Charge"; runtime half ChargeIntentProducer.cs
    internal static void Register();                                             // static-ctor touch from PostureProducers.EnsureRegistered
    // pure half ChargeIntentProducer.Plan.cs (test csproj):
    internal readonly struct Settings { Settings(bool configOn, float maxChargeRadius, float vanillaRenewHours, bool noChargeIfOutflanked = false); }
    internal struct RegimentMemory { Seen; PreviousMode; Armed; PendingDisarm; LastAiChargeTime; HasRefusal; RefusalAtHours; Submitted; SubmittedAtHours; SubmittedMode; }
    internal sealed class ChargeMemory { /* per side: ById, pooled scratch; Clear() */ }
    internal static int Plan(BattleSnapshot s, in Settings settings, ChargeMemory memory, OrderExecutor executor, IIntentSink sink); // returns confirmed charges
    internal static BattleOrderIntent Intent(int regimentId, ChargeAction action);  // CombatBehavior, PerRegiment, Executor, ChargeDoctrine

```
**Rule:** Charge when all of the following hold:
- config on; not PlayerOwned; infantry regiment; group stance 4; target visible and `enemyinrangereg[0]` (D10); distance < `GamePrefs.maxchargeradius`;
- (`TargetMorale + MoraleMargin < OwnMorale` OR disordered OR flanked); `OwnMorale ≥ 0.5`; `OwnFatigue ≤ 0.6`; lane not blocked;
- moving (`regimentpaths > 0`) with `movementmode == 0`.

The "leading" regiments are the `MaxLeadingRegimentsPerGroup` regiments closest to the target. Revert to 2 when the charge resolved (`movementmode` left 3/6), when the conditions fail, or when the group stance is no longer 4. Intents go to the `CombatBehavior` channel, `PerRegiment`, `Priority.Executor`, `IntentSource.ChargeDoctrine`. Charge denial (`ChargeDemotion`, Safety, Stance channel) demotes the group stance, which yields Revert next tick.

**Steps:**
- [ ] BC6a tests first:
  - `ChargeFiresOnWeakerVisibleTargetInRadius`
  - `NoChargeWhenTargetNotFirstInRange`
  - `NoChargeWhenHidden`
  - `NoChargeBeyondVanillaRadius`
  - `NoChargeWhenFatiguedOrShaken`
  - `NoChargeWhenLaneBlocked`
  - `NoChargeWhenStationary`
  - `NoChargeOnPlayerOwned`
  - `RevertAfterChargeResolves`
  - `RevertWhenStanceDemoted`
  - `DenialPrecedenceOverCharge` (arbiter fixture)
  - `OnlyTwoLeadingRegimentsCharge`
  - `ConfigOffNeverCharges`
- [x] BC6b: the producer reads vanilla GamePrefs once per battle into the snapshot scratch, and checks the lane through the snapshot's own positions on the segment (40 m corridor, reusing `WaypointValidator.FriendlyFireClearanceMeters`). Scope `tactical.producer.charge`. OnceLog `tactical-battle-core:charge-first-fire`.

**As built (BC6b):**
- **Vanilla correction (124770-124774).** `Regiment.SetMovementMode(3)` sets `combatbehavior = 0` and `combatbehaviorordered = 0` for infantry and cavalry. So the 122214 gate fires once per behavior-3 write, and an armed regiment reads behavior 0 in modes 3/6. BC6a's "armed = `combatbehaviorordered == 3`" was therefore false exactly when a revert matters (red-first test: `ProducerArmedFromMemoryNotOrderedBehavior` failed with "armed regiment gets an intent"). `ChargeInput.CurrentBehaviorOrdered` is replaced by `WhiskeyArmed`, the producer's memory. Two BC6a header claims were wrong and are corrected there: a refusal does not re-arm the gate on the next frame (behavior is already 0), and the volley-before-the-charge branch (122267, needs `combatbehavior == 3` in mode 3) is unreachable for a regiment armed through the gate. After a refusal vanilla leaves the regiment at behavior 0 (short range); the doctrine's Revert restores 2.
- **Armed = executor-confirmed.** A Charge counts as armed only when `OrderExecutor.TryGetLastOutcome(regiment, CombatBehavior)` reports `Issued`, source `ChargeDoctrine`, at or after the submission (read on the next tick, before the executor runs again). With no such outcome (guard, claim, cooldown) nothing is armed (fail closed). A confirmed Revert or Disarm releases the regiment. A behavior 3 seen on a non-player AI regiment re-arms the memory: nothing else on the AI side writes 3 (the player UI 203769 and `AssignChargeTarget(usechargeorder)` 167621 are player-side; `SetAIStance` writes 0-2).
- **Restored stance-drop cancel (decision point).** Under order delays (the default) the charge courier (behavior 3, queued at 90967) can land after the doctrine's Revert stamped ordered 2. The delivery (125401-125403 → `SetCombatBehavior`, 122175-122178) sets behavior and ordered back to 3, and the 122214 gate fires on the next slow tick, so the producer almost never sees the 3; the regiment would sit in charge mode 3 unarmed. `Decide` therefore restores vanilla's own intended cancel, `MicroAICheckForCharges` 4922-4924 (dead behind 4910): for every AI infantry regiment, armed by the doctrine or not, mode 3 while the group stance is not 4 (and ≥ 0) → Disarm. Mode 6 still waits for 6 → 3. Vanilla's 4917/4922 unit filters (`permanentlydetached`, `isrouted`, `markedforrout`, and a feuding AI group, `aigroup.ai_feudstance != -1` outside `isplayeraiorfeud == 2`) bar arming and the cancel (`ExcludedByVanillaFilter`): the feud instance owns feuding units. Feud is the group's state (verify r2): `ActivateFeud` (116613) sets it on the group, whose `AddUnitToPool(overwriteaigroups)` points every descendant's `groupaiobject` at it (7637-7648). The builder therefore propagates `UnitFlags.Feud` to every unit whose command ancestor, AI group (`UnitRecord.AiGroupId`) or that group's ancestor feuds (`PlayerOwnership.ApplyFeudGroups`, sources are only the units' own `ai_feudstance`, order-independent, zero-alloc), and `TacticalWriteGuard`'s Regiment overloads apply the same rule (`DirectFeudFlags`: own, live AI group, or the side's snapshot flag), so every per-regiment writer refuses them, not just the charge. Ancestors of a feuding group are not marked. Only a unit's own `ai_feudstance` is a source: a unit whose AI group G merely inherits a feud from G's own AI group H stays clear, as vanilla's micro AI tests only the unit's own `aigroup` (verify r3 N5; `FeudPropagatesFromAiGroupAndAncestors`). Known over-protection, accepted as the safe side: when a sub-brigade of a feuding group is separated in contact (`SeparateAIGroup`, 117388-117390 → `SeparateAIGroupFromParentGroup` 85358-85385) it is re-pooled in the main AI with its own `groupaiobject`, yet the feud instance still holds it in `allgroupsassigned` (`AddUnitToPool` 7642-7644; `RemoveUnitFromPool` 7617-7619 runs only when the feud ends, 116557) and `CheckForFeudGroupActions` (4940) keeps moving it. The ancestor rule still marks it Feud, so Whiskey refuses those units rather than fight the feud instance over them. Consequence (verify r1): vanilla's follow-up charge after a contact melee — 122302/122326 leave the regiment in mode 3, and in vanilla it stays armed for a further odds-checked charge — is now disarmed whenever its group is out of assault stance, as the dead 4924 intended; in assault stance it is left to run. BC6a's "a mode 3/6 the doctrine did not arm is vanilla's own" now holds in assault stance only, which matches vanilla's intent (4919 arms in stance 4, 4924 cancels outside it). Residuals, accepted: a Revert for a non-stance reason (lane, margins) in assault stance followed by a late courier leaves vanilla's own charge running, with vanilla's odds check still the gate (4919's intent); config off after arming can leave an assault-stance regiment in mode 3, which is vanilla's own post-melee state (OFF = vanilla).
- **Resolved** (Decide reverts) when the regiment leaves the cycle (`ChargeCycleEnded`), when the refusal stamp changed, or when it is back at mode 0 without behavior 3. The last case covers an arm and refusal both inside one tick, and vanilla's cycle-6 cascade: `SetAIStance` writes the group's own behavior (122099-122120), so cycle 6 re-orders the group to 2 and `ChangeCombatBehavior` cascades to every non-detached unit below it (5631-5634, 90944-90968, PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel 92572-92627). A Revert whose behavior 2 is already ordered writes nothing and releases the regiment.
- **Refusal.** A change of `lastaichargetime` since the last tick is a refusal seen now on the doctrine clock. The first value the producer sees is only the baseline, so a fresh unit's 0 (or a stamp from before the producer saw the unit) is never a refusal. Back-off = `ChargeDoctrine.RefusalBackoffSeconds(GamePrefs.timetorenewaichargecheck)` = max(120 battle-s, renew h × 3600). The shipped renew is 0.5 h (battleprefs: "a renewed charge order is not given if it was broken up before due to bad conditions"), so the effective back-off is 1800 battle-s; vanilla's own use (4917) is dead and compares `lastaichargetime < now + renew` (always true). **Integrator decision point:** the plan named both 120 s and the renew interval; this takes vanilla's intended interval with BC6a's constant as the floor.
- **Inputs from the snapshot.** The target is the regiment's own `FirstEnemyInRangeId` (`enemyinrangereg[0]`, 122228), only while it is a Visible observation; nothing is substituted. Target morale, `outflanked` (`TargetFlanked`) and disorder are read from that visible target's own `EnemyRecords` row, following BC5a's `TargetGarrisoned`/`EnemyAdvancing` precedent. `outflanked` is fog-honest in the main: UpdateFlanking (122959-123175) computes it from the target's facing and this side's own units in its flank sectors. Caveat (verify r1): a sector is also cleared when one of the target's own friendlies stands closer in it (owninrangeregs within `distancetoenemyforoutflank`, 123077-123110), and those friendlies may be hidden from this side, so the flag can carry that much unseen information. Disordered = `MarkedForRout`, `OnRetreat` or movement mode 2/4. Distance runs from `Own[ParentIndex]` (the transform parent, D5; vanilla measures from `transform.parent`, 122257), strictly `<`. With no parent there is no charge. Stance = the parent's: 4 only while `ai_stance` and `ai_stanceordered` are both 4. The demotion writes `ai_stanceordered` at once and `ai_stance` after the courier (ChangeStance 90787/90814), so a demotion reverts on the next tick and a pending assault order arms nothing. Lane: an own standing regiment (not a group) whose projection falls strictly between the regiment and the target, within 40 m of the segment. Leading: `IsLeading` over the parent's direct children's distances to their own visible first target.
- **Own flank (verify r1).** battleprefs "No micro ai charge if unit is outflanked or receiving flanking fire" parses to `GamePrefs.nomicroaichargeifunitisoutflanked` (54784; shipped true) and vanilla never reads it. The producer reads it once per battle with the other constants and, when set, arms no regiment whose own `outflanked` (111488) or `firefromflank` (111492: fire it received from a flank sector within `timetolastfireforflank`, 123208-123264; vanilla's flanking-fire morale effect reads it, 128329) is non-zero (`OutflankedBarsCharge`; holding or reverting an armed regiment is unchanged). Own `outflanked` comes from the regiment's own range list (unfiltered, as vanilla's own state); `firefromflank` is fire the regiment actually received. An unreadable pref fails closed (set).
- **Radius.** `GamePrefs.maxchargeradius` is 50 m in the shipped unitprefs ("calculated from next group commander"), measured from the brigade HQ, so charges are rare: the HQ must be within 50 m of the target. It is kept as vanilla's.
- **Disarm through the executor.** `IntentFlags.LeaveChargeMode` (512; 256 is BC5a's `AvoidCoveredCrossings`). `OrderExecutor.IsDuplicate` does not swallow it while the regiment is in mode 3, even with behavior 2 already ordered. `OrderExecutorRuntime.IssueOther` runs `TacticalCombatBehaviorWriter.EnsureBehavior`, then `Regiment.SetMovementMode(0)` on a regiment (unittyp ≤ 13) still in mode 3. That is vanilla's refusal (122290); the 4922-4924 disarm is unreachable. The write is behind the executor's per-target `TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite(Regiment)`. ChargeDoctrine has no executor recent-order window on CombatBehavior (BC5a, `RecentOrderBattleSecondsFor`).
- **W&L.** `PlayerOwned` regiments are never charged, reverted or disarmed (Decide), the executor refuses them (`OrderExecutor.Plan` step 1), and the runtime re-checks `TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite` on the live unit.
- **Config.** `Plugin.EnableInfantryChargeDoctrine` is read on every tick. Off still runs the plan, so what the doctrine armed is handed back (Revert/Disarm), and nothing new is armed. The master `Enabled` switch idles the producer.
- **Registration.** `ChargeIntentProducer.Register()` is called from `PostureProducers.EnsureRegistered` (BC5a's temporary touch, run by `ArmyPostureInputsRuntime.Refresh` on the coordinator tick). On this base nothing registers BC5b's `GroupStance`, `ChargeDemotion` and `Artillery*` producers or BC5c's `Withdrawal` and `ReserveRelease`; their static constructors never run. So the charge demotion (and with it `DenialPrecedenceOverCharge`) cannot happen in game until BC7's explicit registry. The doctrine still reverts on any stance change away from 4.
- First fire: `OnceLog.Info("tactical-battle-core:charge-first-fire", "[ChargeDoctrine] first infantry charge armed: combat behavior 3 issued to a leading regiment of an assault group")`, logged on the tick that confirms the executor issued the first Charge (one arbiter tick after the vanilla write).
- For BC7: `BattleWriteGateTests`' writer regex does not list `SetMovementMode(`; the executor's two calls are gated (file checks `TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite`), but the scanner would not see a new ungated one.

PROVE-MODE: harness for this slice; in-game at BC7. Per-slice proof is `ChargeDoctrineTests` (BC6a pure block plus the BC6b block, exit 0; a failing assertion throws, exit 134): the producer over a `BattleSnapshot` fixture (two leading regiments, intent shape, armed from memory, executor-confirmed arming, a late charge courier disarmed or re-armed, fresh stamp baseline, refusal back-off with and without vanilla's renew interval, parent-group distance strict `<`, stance from ordered and current, lane between only, target state only from a visible record, never PlayerOwned, deferred disarm after a denied assault, release after vanilla's re-order, config off releases, zero allocation after warm-up), the executor's `LeaveChargeMode` dedupe rule, and `DenialPrecedenceOverCharge` through the real `OrderArbiter` (tick 1: Safety demotion wins the Stance channel while the charges keep their CombatBehavior channel; tick 2: Revert for the courier-pending regiment, Disarm for the armed one). Red first: `ProducerArmedFromMemoryNotOrderedBehavior` (against BC6a's `CurrentBehaviorOrdered` contract), `LeaveChargeModeIsNotADuplicateInChargeMode` (before the executor flag), `VanillaStanceDropDisarmsUnarmedChargeMode` (before the restored cancel) and `ProducerLateChargeDeliveryIsDisarmed` (before the behavior-3 re-arm rule). Mutation evidence (each turned a named test red, exit 134): no renew interval in the back-off; a first-seen non-zero stamp taken as a refusal; group stance read from `ai_stance` alone; lane check without the strictly-between test; a confirmed Revert counted as arming; no re-arm on a seen behavior 3; (verify r1) `IssuedSince` without its source check or without its `AtHours` check; the lane counting inactive friendlies; `MarkedForRout` dropped from the unit filter; the outflanked pref ignored, or applied when clear. Red first (verify r1): `ProducerVanillaUnitFilter` (Feud missing from the filter). Verify r2: `FeudPropagatesFromAiGroupAndAncestors` (BattleSnapshotTests; red before the AI group's ancestors were walked), `DirectWriterFeudFollowsAiGroup` (PlayerOwnershipTests), `ProducerExcludesFeudingBrigadeRegiments`, and the flanking-fire half of `ProducerHonoursNoChargeIfOutflanked`; plants that turned them red: no propagation, no AI-group source, ancestors only, a propagated flag used as a source, `DirectFeudFlags` ignoring the AI group, `firefromflank` ignored. Verify r3: the N5 plant (a propagated flag used as a source inside `SourceOnChain`) turns the AI-group-of-an-AI-group case red ("X not marked through a propagated G"). Runtime proof (the `charge-first-fire` line, `[OrderArbiter]` rows with `ChargeDoctrine` issues, the SetMovementMode(0) disarm, no W&L player regiment touched) is the BC7 in-game smoke.

**As built (BC7):**
- **Producer registry.** The producers' static constructors never ran (nothing referenced the types), so only the four posture producers and the charge producer ran, through BC5a's temporary `PostureProducers.EnsureRegistered`. `BattleProducerRegistry` (pure, test csproj) walks `BattleProducerRegistry.Order` — Withdrawal, ArtilleryDoctrine, ArtilleryPointBlank, GroupStance, ChargeDemotion, Posture, FacingPulse, FireControl, DivisionPlay, ReserveRelease, Charge (11; no "Play": the geometric play is no mover) — and asks `BattleCoreTick.CreateProducer(name)` for each, once, in `BattleCoreTick`'s type initializer. A null, misnamed or throwing producer leaves its slot out and is logged once per battle (`tactical-battle-core:producers-missing`), so it cannot fail the type that also builds every snapshot. `RegisterProducer`, `Rank`, every self-registration, the `Instance`/`Register()` touches and `EnsureRegistered` are deleted; producer static constructors keep only their `BattleScope` registrations. Tests: `BattleCoreIntegrationTests` (order, build, missing/misnamed/throwing, and a source pin of the factory cases and producer names).
- **Plugin.cs.** Descriptions now state the battle-core behaviour of Enable Battle Fog-Honest Perception, Enable Infantry Charge Doctrine (BC6b, incl. the outflanked/flanking-fire pref and the restored stance-drop cancel), Enable Tactical Withdrawal Doctrine, Enable Tactical Orchestrator Reserve Commit Gate, Enable Tactical Reserve Order Delay Guard, Enable Artillery All-Stance Audit, Artillery Audit Min Hold Seconds, Enable Tactical Artillery Doctrine (either artillery flag runs the point-blank watchdog), Enable W&L Tactical Charge Guard, Enable Tactical Contact Facing Pulse and Tactical Commander Mode.
- **Dead code.** `TacticalDivisionPlayDecision.OrderFor`; the duplicated group range reach (now `BattlePerception.GroupRangeReach`); the Operations `CommandWaypointWritePolicy` forwarder; `UnitRecord.GroupStrengthDirect` and `UnitRecord.Experience` (written, never read; the sweep missed them on name collisions); `FormationSnapshot.GroupStrengthDirect` (strategic formation-directive ledger, written never read, not in the sidecar DTOs); `IntentSource.Play`; `B8MicroAICheckForRetreatsObserverPatch` (duplicated `tactical.patch.observer.retreats`); five tag routes whose emitters battle core deleted; `TestLog.ThrowOnWarning`. `OrderOutcomeKind.Superseded` is now produced (`OrderExecutor.RecordSuperseded`, the source's last outcome instead of a stale `Retried`). `UnitRecord.FirstEnemyInRangeId` is read (charge target, withdrawal under-fire, evidence contact). The test build suppresses CS0649 (fields the runtime halves assign); `./build.sh` still reports it.
- **Write-gate scanner** also matches `SetMovementMode(` and `BlockedMovingOrder(`.
- **Charge demotion telemetry.** #45's `[TacticalChargeDeny]` row went with #45. `ChargeDemotionTracker` (pure, test csproj) holds each side's submitted demotions and counts one on the next tick only when the executor's last Stance outcome for the group is Issued, source ChargeDemotion, at or after the submission; the producer logs the first per battle (`tactical-battle-core:charge-demotion-first-fire`), writes a `ChargeDemotion` Decision row per confirmed demotion (per group and stance signature, 30 s throttle) and a `ChargeDemotionCount` row per side at most every 30 s real time. Tests: `ChargeDemotionTrackerTests` (red first).
- **Review fixes (final branch verify, round 1).**
  - The `[OrderArbiter]` 30 s row is written to `LOG` through `LegacyInfoToMainLogIfAllowed`. `LegacyInfo` writes only to the sidecar, `[OrderArbiter]` has no sidecar route, and the row had been written nowhere since BC4. `BattleCoreIntegrationTests` pins the row's route and that every statement-level `LegacyInfo` call with a literal tag under `Tactical/` and `Patches/` names a sidecar-routed tag. Every other battle-core line without a `Tactical*` tag goes through `OnceLog` (which writes `LOG`) or `TelemetryRouter.Emit`.
  - The master `Enabled` switch, which can be turned off at runtime, stops the arbiter. `PostureExecutorBattleGate.ArbiterAction` checks `Enabled` and Commander Mode first, and the side stands down with its claims cleared. The army-retreat stance release runs only when writes are allowed. `TacticalWriteGuard.ModeAllowsWrites` also requires `Enabled`, so the executor's plan guard and its live `CanWrite` fail closed. #42, #56 and #59 already checked `Enabled` on their inline paths.
  - Cross-level Move claims: claims are per (unit, channel), so a regiment's Safety withdrawal and its brigade's Executor move both won. Vanilla `SetGroupFormation` then re-pathed every non-detached, non-routed child (92076-92108, `RegimentSetPath` 92108), undoing the withdrawal. Now:
    - `OrderExecutor.BeginTickClaims` takes the tick's Move winners before retries and winners execute.
    - `EndTickClaims` clears them, so inline plans see none.
    - A group Move whose descendant holds a higher-priority Move claim fans out around that subtree (`TacticalChainOrderPlanner` `shielded` / `shieldedBelow`, as for exposed player-owned subtrees).
    - If nothing else is left, the group Move yields with `ChainNoTargets`.
    - Tests: `OrderArbiterTests` `SafetyRegimentMoveSurvivesAnExecutorBrigadeMove`, `ClaimedBranchFansOutTheRestMovesWhole`, `EqualOrLowerDescendantClaimDoesNotFanOut` and `GroupMoveYieldsWhenEveryChildIsClaimed` (red first).
  - `ChargeDemotionTracker` keeps its counts private; the count row reads them through `TryTakeWindow`.
- **Review round 2 landing items.**
  - `OrderArbiterTests.UnrelatedSafetyClaimDoesNotShieldAnEqualDescendant` exercises the per-descendant priority comparison behind the tick-wide early return.
  - `BattleCoreIntegrationTests.RuntimeWiresEnabledAndTickClaims` pins three runtime wirings as source text:
    - `ArbiterAction(PluginEnabled(), …)` and `PluginEnabled` reading `Enabled`;
    - the `Enabled` leg of `ModeAllowsWrites`;
    - the order `BeginTickClaims` → outcomes, retries and winners → `EndTickClaims`.
  - Plants P1, P2, P7 and P8 now fail the harness.
  - `CIC.EmitCampaignInfo` writes through `LegacyInfoToMainLogIfAllowed`, so `[CIC:diag]` (no sidecar route) reaches `LogOutput.log`. It was written nowhere, on `main` too. The `[Plan:*]` rows stay sidecar-only.
  - `DiscardedLegacyRowsRouteToTheSidecar` now scans all of `src`. It covers statement-level `LegacyInfo` calls with a literal tag, and literal rows passed to one-line wrappers that hand their string parameter to such a call (`EmitCampaignInfo`, `EmitCampaignInfoOnce`, …). It skips `[once:…]` tags as `TelemetryTagPolicy.ExtractTag` does. It does not cover calls whose row is built elsewhere (a variable argument) or wrappers with braces in their body.
- **Plan corrections.** No `TacticalPlayerProtected`, `TacticalChargeDeny` or `TacticalChargePreserved` row exists any more (their emitters were #61 and #45): the re-attach check reads the decision matrix's `wlGuard=deny:owned-descendant-attached`. The telemetry pipeline has no percentiles: the perf thresholds below read `PerformanceAggregate` rows (count, meanMs, maxMs, slowCount per 5 s window).

PROVE-MODE: harness for the registry, range reach, superseded outcome, charge-demotion confirmation and write-gate scanner (`BattleCoreIntegrationTests`, `ChargeDemotionTrackerTests`, `OrderArbiterTests.SupersededRetryIsReportedToTheSource`, `BattleWriteGateTests`; red first: the runtime registry pin before `BattleCoreTick` used the registry, `GroupRangeReach` and `RecordSuperseded` before they existed, the two new scanner cases before the regex). Runtime proof of the whole battle core is the smoke checklist below.

### BC7 smoke checklist (consolidated, in order)

Paths: `LOG` = `<GTCW>/BepInEx/LogOutput.log` (truncated each launch); `SESSION` = the newest `<GTCW>/BepInEx/WhiskeyRealism/tuning-logs/<session-id>/` (JSONL sidecars, default `Logging Profile = TacticalTuning`). Lines tagged `[BattleCore]`, `[OrderArbiter]`, `[BattlePerception]`, `[ChargeDoctrine]`, `[ChargeDemotion]`, `[BattleSnapshot]` and `[first-fire]` go to `LOG`; `Tactical*` rows (and every `Tactical*` OnceLog line) go to `SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`; `PerformanceAggregate` rows to `SESSION/performance*.jsonl`. Every `tactical…` OnceLog key re-arms at battle end, so each `[once:…]` line below appears once per battle.

0. **Precondition.** The deployed `WhiskeyRealism.dll` hash equals `dist/WhiskeyRealism.dll` (AGENTS.md deploy check). An existing `dev.kyle.whiskey-realism.cfg` keeps its values: confirm `Tactical Commander Mode = Active` and every battle-core flag `true`; the three removed keys may linger in the cfg and are ignored.
1. **Session A — a campaign battle (load a campaign and fight one battle; `tools/smoke-check.py` assumes the campaign session described in `tools/smoke-situational.txt`, so a custom battle exits 1 on the `campaign.patch.*` ids), `ai_vs_ai` off, 20x, through one night.** With `ai_vs_ai` off only the enemy AI alliance is driven: the player's `AIBattle` instance stands down (`PostureExecutorBattleGate`, `player-instance`), so "per AI side" below means that one alliance.
   1. Launch, load, fight to the end of day 1 and into day 2, quit. Run `python3 -I tools/smoke-check.py "$LOG"`: exit 0. Every battle id in the list above shows `[first-fire]` (feud, reserve and #62 ids fire once their vanilla method runs, which `UpdateAITasks` 5857-5872 and the micro AI do every battle).
   2. Deployment (day 1, #60): `[first-fire] tactical.patch.deployment-terrain`; `TacDeployTerrain` rows in `SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`; **absent** from `LOG`: `[once:tactical-battle-core:snapshot-degraded]`, `[once:tactical-battle-core:snapshot-missing]`, `[once:tactical-battle-core:snapshot-failed]`, `[once:tactical-battle-core:ensure-snapshots]`.
   3. Perception: first `[once:tactical.battle-perception:mode:RangeOnly] [BattlePerception] alliance=<a> mode=RangeOnly reason=GridNotPainted visible=<n> remembered=<n>` (the #60 build precedes the painted grid), then `[once:tactical.battle-perception:mode:FogGrid] [BattlePerception] alliance=<a> mode=FogGrid reason=None visible=<n> remembered=<n>` within about 5 battle seconds of the AI side's first perception tick; `[once:tactical.battle-perception:intelligence-map] [BattlePerception] side grid UseIntelligenceMap=<true|false>`. With HQ in the saddle the side stays RangeOnly and logs no FogGrid line; the `LOG` line is once per mode (it may still read `reason=GridNotPainted`), so check the reason on the `BattlePerception` row in `SESSION/tactical*.jsonl` (`.fields.reason=="HqInTheSaddle"`). **Absent:** `tactical.battle-perception:probe-failed`, `fog-probe-failed`, `firstrun-field-missing`, `bunits-field-missing`.
   4. Producers and arbiter: `[once:tactical-battle-core:producers] [BattleCore] producers count=12 order=Withdrawal,ArtilleryDoctrine,ArtilleryPointBlank,GroupStance,ChargeDemotion,Posture,FacingPulse,FireControl,DivisionPlay,Recon,ReserveRelease,Charge` (count=11 without `Recon` before BC9); `[once:tactical-battle-core:arbiter-first-fire] [OrderArbiter] first vanilla order issued channel=<channel> source=<source>`. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:producers-missing`, `tactical-battle-core:producer-threw:<Name>`, `tactical-battle-core:arbiter-failed`, `tactical-battle-core:snapshot-unavailable`, `tactical-battle-core:inline-failed`, `tactical-battle-core:bunits-missing`, `tactical-battle-core:terrain-*`, `tactical-battle-core:bfs-missing`.
   5. `[OrderArbiter] alliance=<a> intents=<n> winners=<n> issued=<n> moved=<n> failed=<n> retried=<n> gaveUp=<n> skipped=<n> deduped=<n> superseded=<n> topSkip=<reason>` every 30 s real time per AI-driven side (written to `LOG` since the BC7 review fix; before it the row was written nowhere): `moved > 0` once the armies move, `failed / issued < 0.2`, `gaveUp ≤ 5` per row, no row where `superseded` dominates `retried`.
   6. Performance (`SESSION/performance*.jsonl`, `PerformanceAggregate`): `tactical.snapshot.build` meanMs ≤ 1.5 and slowCount (≥ 5 ms) near 0; `tactical.arbiter.resolve` meanMs ≤ 0.5 with slowCount/count ≤ 5 %; `tactical.patch.battle-core-arbiter` slowCount/count ≤ 1 % at its 5 ms threshold; note `tactical.arbiter.execute`, `tactical.perception` and each `tactical.producer.*`. E.g. `cat "$SESSION"/performance*.jsonl | jq -r 'select(.event=="PerformanceAggregate" and (.fields.scope|startswith("tactical.snapshot.build") or startswith("tactical.arbiter") or startswith("tactical.patch.battle-core-arbiter"))) | [.fields.scope,.fields.count,.fields.meanMs,.fields.maxMs,.fields.slowCount] | @tsv'`.
   7. Posture (`SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`): `TacticalCommandPosture` rows with `writeResult=submitted` for the AI-driven alliance (both alliances only with `ai_vs_ai` on), `TacticalLeafCascade` rows, `TacticalFacingPulse` rows; `TacticalDecisionMatrix` battle rows with a known force balance (own / (visible + remembered)).
   8. Night: the doctrine clock is frozen across the end-of-day cycle; on day 2 the arbiter resumes (`[OrderArbiter]` rows again, `moved > 0`) with none of the warnings listed in 1.2-1.4.
2. **Watch items in Session A** (code-only proxies; note what you see, they decide tuning, not pass/fail):
   1. Close engagement = melee in the subtree or a visible enemy within 250 m (narrower than shipped #61): brigades in a firefight at 250-600 m — formation holds, approach moves, `FallBackToLine` courier vs immediate.
   2. Flank risk weights remembered enemies (0.5 × confidence) with no friendly term: a stale remembered enemy should not keep driving a local flank emergency, a refused flank or the division play's "in trouble".
   3. Leaf brigades: after the parent moves or wheels the leaf goes to its layout slot; after a Whiskey refuse wheel it holds the skewed slot; a leaf whose division moved as part of its corps is pulled toward the division's old anchor (stale `lastsetwaypointposition`); leaves on a terrain-shaped line more than 60 m off the straight slot are pulled onto it (`adjustbyterrainshape` is not applied).
   4. Refused flank: a refuse wheel on a threatened flank, its children skewed back, no thrash between refuse and plain re-form.
   5. Covered ford/bridge: an AI attack over a crossing the enemy covers goes around or through via the obstacle path; `[OrderArbiter]` shows it `moved`.
   6. Artillery point-blank watchdog: a battery that vanilla's delayed group move keeps sending forward is pulled back once, not back and forth every tick (watch the battery; `[OrderArbiter] issued` does not climb steadily for an idle front).
   7. Withdrawal: an AI regiment under pressure falls back by vanilla's distance and turns to fire (behavior 2), never toward a hidden enemy. When its brigade gets a posture move on the same tick, the regiment keeps falling back and the brigade's other regiments move (the brigade's move fans out around the higher Move claim); a falling-back regiment yanked back into its brigade's line is a failure. Residual: a group formation or facing write on the same tick (`SetFormation`, the wheel) still re-lays every child; the wheel runs only with no enemy in range.
   8. Charge: `[once:tactical-battle-core:charge-first-fire] [ChargeDoctrine] first infantry charge armed: combat behavior 3 issued to a leading regiment of an assault group` in an infantry assault. Rare by design (brigade HQ within 50 m of the visible `enemyinrangereg[0]`); a whole battle without it is not a failure. Charge demotion (an assault brigade the doctrine holds in reserve or falling back drops from stance 4): `[once:tactical-battle-core:charge-demotion-first-fire] [ChargeDemotion] first vanilla assault demoted: stance <2|3> issued to a group vanilla had escalated to assault (ai_stanceordered 4)` in `LOG`, counted only once the executor issued it; `ChargeDemotion` Decision rows in `SESSION/tactical*.jsonl` (`decision=DemoteToHold`, `reason=charge-demotion`, `fromStance=4`, `toStance=<2|3>`, `submittedAtHours`, the unit; one per group and stance per 30 s) and a `ChargeDemotionCount` row per side at most every 30 s real time while demotions happen (`demoted=<n in the window>`, `battleTotal=<n>`, `windowSeconds=30`). The demotion followed by its regiments leaving charge mode is the BC6b precedence check; like the charge it can be absent in a battle where the doctrine never holds an assault group back. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:charge-prefs`, `charge-enabled`, `charge-config`, `charge-demotion-telemetry`.
   9. Reserves: `[TacticalReserveOrderDelayGuard] converted direct reserve path to delayed order unit=…` (with order delays on), `[TacticalReserveDrift] surface=CheckUseOfReserves …` for a Reserve-role rollback, `[OrderArbiter]` activity from a released reserve; **absent:** `tactical-battle-core:reserve-release-enabled`, `withdrawal-enabled`.
   10. Later-tick group moves over a withdrawal (review R2-4): the same-tick fan-out holds only while the regiment's Safety claim exists, which is one tick. Withdrawal refuses a unit that already has paths or movement mode, so the claim lapses on the next tick. On later ticks a group Move can re-path a regiment that is still falling back. The posture producer holds off while a subordinate is moving (`PostureSense.ActiveMove` makes the group `alreadyCorrect`); the division play, reserve release and the stalled-fallback moves do not, as vanilla's own group moves do not. Watch for a falling-back regiment pulled back into line a few seconds after it started.
   11. W&L dispatch on a yielded ancestor move (review R2-2, Session B): when every AI child of the player's superior holds a higher Move claim (all of them withdrawing), the superior's move yields (`ChainNoTargets`). No dispatch goes out that tick, because a dispatch accompanies an executed move (`OrderExecutor.ShouldDispatch`). The claim lasts one tick, so the next executed move carries the dispatch. Watch for a dispatch panel that arrives a tick late, not for one that is missing for long.
   12. **BC8, army attack/hold** (`Enable Experience And Drill AI Weighting` on). `TacticalReinforcementOpportunity` rows in `SESSION/tactical*.jsonl` carry `qualityFactor` (vanilla's `(1 + Δmorale) × (1 + Δexperience)`, 84862) and `currentRatio` = headcount × that factor: `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalReinforcementOpportunity") | [.side,.fields.outcome,.fields.currentRatio,.fields.qualityFactor,.fields.attackThreshold] | @tsv'`. Expect `qualityFactor` 1.00 until an enemy regiment has been seen, then away from 1 when the armies differ (early-war armies near 1; a veteran army against green troops > 1). `attackThreshold` at a mid-initiative commander ≈ 0.74 for Meeting/Attacker (1 / (1 + 0.5 × 0.7)), still > 1 for a Defender. Watch: a veteran army attacks a slightly larger green one (`outcome=AttackNow` at a headcount below 1); a green early-war army facing veterans holds (`DelayingDefense` / no AttackNow at a headcount above 1); no flip-flopping beyond the opportunity hysteresis. Vanilla-matched rule: the attack/defend boundary (`attackThreshold`) is divided by initiative as vanilla divides its defend and attack triggers (6400, 6403); the withdrawal threshold is not, as vanilla's retreat trigger (4216, 6414) is not, so `outcome=WithdrawalToFightLater` appears below the same `currentRatio` (≈ 0.40 at mid aggression) whatever the commander's initiative. With a quality-weighted `currentRatio` between `attackThreshold` and 1 and no near own arrivals the row reads `outcome=DefensiveHold` (no macro opinion: `[TacticalMacroDecision]` shows no Whiskey write for that side) and vanilla's own macro AI decides; an attack there is vanilla's (≥ 0.80 / 1.35 ≈ 0.59), not a BC8 AttackNow. The `TacticalDecisionMatrix` known force balance moves with the same factor, and so do the army envelopment gates (`ArmyOrchestrator` reads `CurrentReinforcementOpportunity.CurrentRatio` against the envelopment minimum, :156, :825, :1022): a veteran army chooses envelopment / double-wing cascades at a lower headcount ratio than BC7, a green one later. The flag is read once per battle; toggling it mid-battle applies from the next battle. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:troop-quality-prefs`, `troop-quality-flag`.
   13. **BC8, withdrawal and charge.** Watch the regiments: a green AI regiment under fire pulls back by vanilla's fallback distance and turns to fire (the withdrawal producer's RearGuard move) around a third of its strength lost while a veteran regiment of the same brigade holds to about half (break point = vanilla's unbearable-losses trigger; 80 % of it under fire); no regiment is withdrawn with only light losses and steady morale. `[ChargeDoctrine]` first fire only where vanilla's own experience-weighted odds pass (own clearly stronger: strength, experience, morale, fresher); expect fewer arm → refusal cycles than BC7 (a refused charge stamps `lastaichargetime` and costs 0.5 h). **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:troop-quality-leadership`. Performance: `tactical.snapshot.build` stays within item 1.6 (the break point adds a cached leadership read per own infantry/cavalry regiment; refreshes are staggered by commander id).
   14. **BC8, campaign offensives** (before or after the battle, on the campaign map). `CoordinatedOps` rows in the campaign sidecar (`SESSION/campaign*.jsonl`) with `.fields.action=="commit-package"` carry `.fields.requiredAttackRatio` = 1.25 × (1 + (1 − lead commander initiative) × 0.25): between 1.25 (initiative 1) and 1.56 (initiative 0). A cautious commander's package commits with more support or waits where a bold one goes alone (`reason=lead-overmatch` needs `ratio ≥ requiredAttackRatio`). **Absent:** `coordinated-ops:offensive:initiative`, `campaign-troop-quality:initiative-pref`.
   15. **BC9, cavalry reconnaissance** (`Enable Battle Reconnaissance` on). `[once:tactical-battle-core:recon-first-fire] [BattleRecon] first reconnaissance: a cavalry regiment rides to look (target=<EntryPoint|StaleBand|Forward>)` in `LOG`, usually early on day 1 (target `Forward` before any contact, `EntryPoint` toward the enemy's map entry points); `TacticalRecon` Decision rows in `SESSION/tactical*.jsonl` (`MissionStarted` with the target kind and the scout, `MissionEnded` with `returned` / `recalled` / `not-issued` / `timeout` / `gave-up` / `scout-lost`): `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalRecon") | [.side,.fields.decision,.fields.reason,.unit] | @tsv'`. `[OrderArbiter]` rows show Recon activity (target `RememberedEnemy` after contact once confidence has fallen below 0.35). Watch: a single cavalry regiment leaves its brigade, rides out (march column on its own, vanilla `CheckFormationChange`), stops when it first sees an enemy (about 150 m short) or at the entry point's stand-off, waits about 3 battle minutes and rides back; never into a visible enemy force at least its size; at most 4 such rides per side per battle hour; after contact, a ride to a stale band or an unwatched entry point raises intel confidence (`TacticalReinforcementOpportunity` `intelConfidence`); before any contact confidence stays 0 until an enemy is actually seen (its contact-recency term), so a forward ride that sees nothing does not move it. Residuals: the scout's brigade re-formed by vanilla while it is out (`AdjustGroupFormations` once nothing moves) pulls it back early (`MissionEnded recalled`); a W&L player-owned cavalry regiment never rides. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:recon-settings`, `recon-telemetry`, `producer-threw:Recon`.
   16. **BC9, reconnaissance in force.** While an attacking or meeting side's army is in the Probe phase (`TacticalReplan`/plan rows `phase=Probe`), `[once:tactical-battle-core:probe-first-fire] [BattleRecon] first reconnaissance in force: a group probes its front band in the Probe phase` and a `TacticalRecon` `ProbeNominated` row; `TacticalCommandPosture` rows with `task=Probe` and `writeResult=submitted` for that group. Only unlinked AI groups can probe: vanilla links every reserve, screening and wing group (`LinkReservesToLineGroup` 6729-6742, `AssignReserveToOperationalGroup` 7153-7229) and its link would re-lay them, so in practice the line's centre group and other unlinked groups probe; a battle without `probe-first-fire` where every group is linked is expected, not a failure. Watch: one AI group per front band with no enemy in sight advances about 200 m in screen stance and halts when it first sees the enemy (a Stop from the recon producer: screen stance walks on into contact in vanilla, 117253); a defending side (vanilla battle type Defender) never probes; a band is probed at most once per battle hour. After a probe that met no enemy at least its size the group stays where the probe took it (`[once:tactical-battle-core:probe-hold-first-fire] [BattleRecon] first probe hold: …`, a `TacticalRecon` `ProbeHeld` row; `TacticalCommandPosture` rows for the group with `reason=probe-hold` while its ledger task would walk it back): it usually stands in the probe's screen stance (expected, not a bug), and it does not walk back to its form-up position until its ledger task changes; forward bounds still go. After a probe that met an enemy at least its size the group's ledger task resumes (it may fall back to its form-up position).
   17. **BC9, no-contact scouting.** On day 1 before any sighting the `[TacticalCommandAssignment]` rows (`SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`) read `shape=FixAndFlank phase=Scouting` instead of a `Committed` operation on zero enemy strength (the director's reason `no-contact-scout` is not logged); Main-effort groups get `doctrineTask=Probe` and screens `Screen` and advance by bounds of about 200 m toward the objective (no longer to a 75 m objective stand-off); once the enemy is seen the side commits as before. A Defender side (vanilla battle type 2) reads `shape=DefensiveNetwork` (`phase=Scouting`, or `Forming` where a Meeting/Attacker side would commit) and never shows `doctrineTask` / `task` `Probe`, `AttackObjective`, `SupportAttack` or `FixEnemy` in `TacticalCommandAssignment` or `TacticalCommandPosture` rows (its main effort forms up or holds, its supporting and fixing brigades hold, its screens screen), and no #45 group-stance decision for it applies stance 3 (neither `ledger-…` nor `scorer-…`; the default scorer reports `scorer-defender-weak-point-hold` / `scorer-defender-counterstroke-hold` where a Meeting/Attacker side would attack), and its cavalry still rides out (item 15): `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalCommandPosture") | [.side,.fields.task] | @tsv' | sort | uniq -c` for the defending side. Screen and Probe brigades with nothing in sight turn to face the stalest unwatched enemy entry point: `TacticalFacingPulse` rows with `scope=Brigade`, `task=Screen` (or `Probe`), `reason=facing` **and `writeResult=submitted`** while no enemy is in sight (a `writeResult=none` row alone is not the feature), never urgent; regiment rows with the same bearing only under a brigade with a detached or player-owned descendant. HoldObjective / ReserveWait / Consolidate / FixEnemy / Delay / HoldChoke / FallBackToLine brigades with nothing in sight show no such row (`reason=no-threat-bearing`).
   18. **BC9, skirmisher screen (`SkirmisherScreenDistancePatch`).** `[first-fire] tactical.patch.skirmisher-screen`; `[once:tactical-battle-core:skirmisher-screen-first-fire] [BattleRecon] first skirmisher screen pushed out: …` once an AI brigade in screen stance has detached skirmisher companies. Watch: the companies stand about 100 m ahead of their regiments (vanilla 30 m) while nothing is in their range, and come back to 30 m (vanilla) as soon as an enemy is in range or seen within 250 m; W&L player companies are never moved. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:skirmisher-screen-failed`, `skirmisher-screen-fields`.
   19. **BC9, no Whiskey attack stance for a Defender.** In a battle whose only AI side is the Defender (vanilla battle type 2; for example the player attacks), `LOG` has no `[TacticalStanceWrite] … stance=3` line: `grep '\[TacticalStanceWrite\]' LOG | grep 'stance=3 '` must print no line whose source (the word after the tag, `TacticalStanceOwnership.StanceWriteSource`) is `Scorer` (#45) or `Executor` (posture, play, leaf); a `ChargeDemotion` line steps a vanilla-escalated assault down and is vanilla's own escalation (decision 12e). These lines are first-occurrence OnceLogs keyed by source, reason and stance and carry no side, which is why the check needs a battle with one AI side; with two AI sides use the per-side rows instead: `TacticalDecisionMatrix` group rows for the defending `side` never show `whiskeyStance=3`, and its `TacticalCommandPosture` / `TacticalCommandAssignment` rows never show an attack task (item 17). Vanilla's own stance cascade may still put the Defender's groups in attack stance (`aiStance=3` in the matrix rows with `whiskeyStance` ≤ 2): that is vanilla's counterattack, not a failure. The runtime call sites that feed the stance into the guards are pinned by `defender: every runtime call site passes the army's stance into the guards`.
   20. **BC10, terrain grid, covered crossings and the path probe.** `[once:tactical-battle-core:terrain-grid] [BattleTerrain] grid <sizeX>x<sizeZ> m, crossings=<n> (fords=<n> bridges=<n>)` once per battle at deployment. An AI attack over a ford or bridge that a **visible** enemy covers goes through the obstacle path (`[OrderArbiter]` moved), and one the enemy covers unseen (in woods, beyond sight) is crossed directly: `[once:tactical-battle-core:covered-crossings] [OrderExecutor] perceived covered crossings: alliance=<a> crossings=<n> coverPoints=<n>` appears once the side sees a covering enemy. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:terrain-grid-missing`, `tactical-producer-posture:nav-filter-failed`, and the deleted `tactical-battle-core:blocked-crossings-missing` / `blocked-crossings-failed` and `tactical-producer-posture:area-cost-failed`. `PerformanceAggregate` `tactical.covered-crossings` meanMs ≤ 0.5.
   21. **BC10 step A, arrival sizing (before step B is built).** In a battle where a multi-unit reinforcement group arrives while the AI already moves: `[first-fire] tactical.patch.arrival-guard.prefix` and `TacticalArrivalRepath` Decision rows in `SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`: `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalArrivalRepath") | [.side,.unit,.fields.arrived,.fields.queued,.fields.ordered,.fields.repathed,.fields.repathedOrdered] | @tsv'` (step A rows carry no `shielded`; step B would add it). Record the rows in the step-B commit: `repathedOrdered > 0` in at least one row → step B ships; three multi-unit arrivals all at 0 → step B is dropped (decision 10). `[once:tactical-battle-core:arrival-repath-first] [BattleArrival] an entry re-path overwrote an executor-ordered member: ordered=<n> repathedOrdered=<n>` appears the first time it happens. Fewer than three multi-unit arrivals observed, all at 0, when the rest of BC10 is ready → step B waits and BC10 merges without it. The integrator deploys step A. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:arrival-guard-failed`. (As built, step B is not built: it needs this smoke first; see As built (BC10).)
   22. **BC10, fog-honest deployment.** Day 1, AI side: `[once:tactical-battle-core:deployment-fog-first-fire] [Deployment] fog-honest re-placement: alliance=<a> source=<EntryPoint|MapCentre|Visible> candidates=<n> replaced=<n> day1=True` (day-1 perception is `RangeOnly`, so no observation is used at deployment and `source` reads `EntryPoint` or `MapCentre`; `Visible` appears only in a later-day deployment under `FogGrid`) and `TacticalDeploymentPlan` Decision rows: `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalDeploymentPlan") | [.side,.decision.kind,.fields.day1,.fields.candidates,.fields.replaced,.fields.issued,.fields.refused,.fields.reserveGroups,.fields.reserveChildren,.fields.clampReverted] | @tsv'` (the first fire line is absent when nothing was re-placed: every group kept vanilla's spot is a valid outcome). Watch: AI brigades face the enemy-owned entry points, not the player's hidden deployment; with `ai_vs_ai` (Session B) both sides' day-1 rows read `EntryPoint|MapCentre`, so the second-placed side's placement does not depend on where the first side's groups stand. `refused` should be 0 or small (a refused placement is a regiment target of a fanned-out group, or a skipped write); `issued` equals `replaced + reserveGroups`. #60's terrain corrections still appear as `TacDeployTerrainAdvice` rows, now only for groups vanilla moved in that call. `[first-fire] tactical.patch.deployment-terrain.prefix` and `.postfix`. **Absent:** `tactical-deployment-terrain:prefix-failed`, `tactical-deployment-terrain:failed`, `tactical-deployment-terrain:correction`, `tactical-deployment-terrain:entry-points`, `tactical-deployment-terrain:wl-group`, `tactical-battle-core:inline-failed`. `PerformanceAggregate` `tactical.patch.deployment-terrain.postfix` slowCount (≥ 5 ms) near 0 (two to three calls per battle).
   23. **BC10, deployment reserve echelon.** A Meeting or Attacker side with at least three eligible direct children: `[once:tactical-battle-core:deployment-reserve-first-fire] [Deployment] reserve echelon: alliance=<a> children=<n> groups=<n> clampReverted=<n>`; the reserve children stand about 425 m behind the line at battle start (not 350-575 m back after a march), and their `[TacticalDirectChildIntent]` rows read `role=Reserve` on the first allocation after deployment (the allocator reads the executor's last Move outcome of the child's groups: `Source=DeploymentReserve`, Issued or Moved). Watch: a reserve child's groups keep their spacing (no two groups on one spot); no reserve march backwards in the first minutes; a reserve released later (crisis, Exploit, odds, BC11 flank) moves forward from there; **the role stays `Reserve` for the first minutes** (see As built: the flag clears when another source moves the child's groups, after which the evidence path decides, which also gives Reserve to an uncommitted strong child). A Defender on authored fortifications places no reserve (expected).
   24. **BC10, road march.** A move of at least 400 m whose straight line runs at least 30 % through woods or swamp, by a group not in contact, where a road goes around: first a group formation write to March Column (vanilla group formation 4; the group's regiments go to march column, formation 3), then `[once:tactical-battle-core:road-route-first-fire] [OrderExecutor] first road march: via=<1|2> costRatio=<0.00> column=true`. Watch: the regiments march in column along the road, abreast within the brigade's column frontage (vanilla lays a brigade's regiments side by side in every group formation, so this is not single file); they leave the road for the last 250 m (vanilla's own road skip near the target) and re-form to the task's formation on arrival (the posture producer's formation write; vanilla's regiments also leave march column near the end of their path and on contact). A via point can sit up to 80 m off the road where vanilla snaps a leg end to cover or a crest (`RepositionWaypoints`; residual). A group that halts at a via point re-plans from there (its next posture move goes to the final target), not a stall longer than one courier interval (0.25 battle-h); a group still not in March Column 0.1 battle-h after the formation write moves unrouted. `PerformanceAggregate` `tactical.producer.posture.road-route` meanMs ≤ 1.5, slowCount (≥ 2 ms) ≤ 5 % of count. **Route failures to watch (verify r3 landing item):** every NavMesh query target is built at terrain height (`PostureTargets.AtTerrain`, `HeightAt`); a target at y = 0 misses the mesh on a map with relief and reads as "no path". The road-route first-fire line above must appear on a hilly map; absent: `tactical-producer-posture:road-route-failed`, `tactical-producer-posture:nav-plan-failed`, `play-executor-runtime:march-route-exception`; `TacticalEnvelopment` `reason=no-route` should be rare (a handful at most per battle, never on most enveloping children).
   25. **BC10, perceived outflanking.** `[ChargeDoctrine]` charges and `TacticalCommandPosture` local flank emergencies still occur; watch that an AI regiment is no longer barred from charging (or a brigade put on GuardFlank) by an enemy hidden in woods 40-60 m off its flank (vanilla's own `outflanked` still sees it: the vanilla morale effect is unchanged).
   26. **BC11, refused flank.** A settled AI brigade with a perceived threat on one flank: `[once:tactical-battle-core:refuse-first-fire] [OrderExecutor] first refused flank: mode=<Wheel|InPlace> flank=<Left|Right> regiments=<n> skipped=<n>`. Out of combat (the main path) the line wheels with the threatened wing bent back (vanilla skewed slots). In combat only the wing regiments that vanilla's micro turn will leave alone turn in place (`skipped` counts the others): those not under fire with no visible enemy within 40 m, and those whose closest visible enemy already lies within 25° of the refused heading; a wing regiment whose closest enemy is in front keeps facing it. Watch: a regiment Whiskey turned is not turned back within the next battle-minute (if it is, vanilla's own range list held an enemy the snapshot did not see: record the unit and time); no thrash between refuse and plain re-form (formation cooldown 0.05 / 0.1 battle-h).
   27. **BC11, pressure facing.** `TacticalFacingPulse` and `TacticalCommandPosture` rows carry `facingSource=pressure` when a holding brigade turns toward the most-pressured side rather than its closest visible enemy. Watch: a brigade facing a small screen in front and a large force on its flank turns to the flank; it does not swing back and forth between two similar threats (25 % hysteresis).
   28. **BC11, envelopment.** Under a MobileFlankMarch, WideFlankApproach, DoubleEnvelopment, ManeuverAndFix or CavalryRaid plan (`[TacticalPlan]` / `TacticalPlayExecution` rows): `[once:tactical-battle-core:envelopment-first-fire] [Envelopment] first flank march: plan=<plan> rect=<FlankMarch|LeftWing|RightWing|DeepRaid> child=<id>` and `TacticalEnvelopment` Decision rows with `phase` Hold → March → Turn → Attack (or `Abandoned` with `reason=river-on-leg|no-route|anchored-high-ground|lost-strength|timeout|plan-changed`, `mirrored=true` when the other side was taken; `proceed=center-contact|offset|hold-ceiling` on the March row). Watch: the flank corps waits, marches wide (BC10 routing applies), turns inward and attacks the enemy line end; a Defender side shows no `TacticalEnvelopment` rows. **Negative control:** under GenericAggressive, MeetingEngagement, FrontalShockAssault, ContinuousAttrition or GenericDesperate no `TacticalEnvelopment` row appears; under HesitatedCommit or GenericMethodical the wings show only `phase=Hold` then `phase=Released` (a start delay of 600 / 300 battle-s, then the allocator's role; no March, Turn or Attack). `TacticalDirectChildIntent` rows show the zone roles on direct children only.
   29. **BC11, vanilla flank tally (#113) and reserve to a flank.** `[first-fire] tactical.patch.flank-threat-tally`; `[once:tactical-flank-threat:first-correction] [FlankThreatTally] group flanks threatened re-tallied per subordinate: vanilla=<v> corrected=<c>`. A Reserve child released to a threatened wing: `TacticalReserveRelease` Decision row `reason=flank-threat` and its `[TacticalDirectChildIntent]` role `RefuseLeft|RefuseRight`. **Absent:** `tactical-flank-threat:failed`. `PerformanceAggregate` `tactical.patch.flank-threat-tally` slowCount ≈ 0.
   30. **BC12, terrain picture.** `[once:tactical-battle-core:terrain-picture] [BattleTerrain] picture ready: cells=<n> crests=<n> coverLines=<n> waterCells=<n> crossings=<n> towns=<n> junctions=<n> buildMs=<ms> steps=<n> maxStepMs=<ms>` within the first seconds of the battle; `buildMs` is the summed build time (estimate 10-15 ms on a 4 km map; report the measured value), every `tactical.terrain-picture.build` step ≤ 5 ms, the collider stage included (`PerformanceAggregate` maxMs; each step stops at its 4 ms deadline plus at most one unit of work). **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:terrain-picture-failed`, `terrain-heights-missing`.
   31. **BC12, objectives and terrain lines.** `[TacticalCommandAssignment]` / `TacticalOpsLedger` rows show objective types `Bridge`, `Ford`, `Ridge`, `Town` or `RoadJunction` where the map has them (map-dependent), and defensive planner reasons with `terrain-line:<crest|wall|sunken|fence|embankment|river>` and `:anchored-left|:anchored-right`; the first one logs `[once:tactical-battle-core:terrain-line] [BattleTerrain] first terrain-line anchor: <reason>`, and `PerformanceAggregate` `tactical.terrain-line` should stay under 1 ms. With `Enable Battle Terrain Picture` off none of this appears (objective types stay Unknown / VictoryPoint, no terrain-line reasons). Watch: a defending AI forms on a crest, wall or sunken road near its objective instead of on the objective point, and does not post a flank guard on a river-anchored flank.
   32. **BC12, crest playbooks and intent.** `ReverseSlopeDefense` appears in `[TacticalPlan]` only on a map with a crest across the front, its hold line about 80 m behind the crest and its screen on it; `[TacticalIntent]` rows can read `Defend` with evidence `EnemyOnStrongGround` when the enemy halts on a crest or behind a wall; the telemetry playbook label reads `LineHold` unless half the held groups stand on crests.
   33. **BC11, the flank tally patch's effect on vanilla flank moves (measured).** Two runs of one Defender-only AI battle (the player attacks; load the same pre-battle save), one with `Enable Vanilla Flank Threat Tally Fix` true and one with it false. Count `TacticalVanillaFlankMove` Observation rows per battle-hour in each: `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalVanillaFlankMove") | [.side,.unit,.fields.direction,.fields.macroai,.fields.tallyFix] | @tsv' SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`. Expected direction: the rate with the fix on is at least the rate with it off (vanilla's own counter-flank responds to real outflanking, Defender sides included). Record both rates in BUG-TAC-022; this is a measurement, not a gate.
   34. **BC13, tiers and promotion.** `[once:tactical-battle-core:lod-first-fire] [BattleLod] first distant tier: alliance=<a> full=<n> distant=<n>` before contact, and `TacticalLod` Observation rows every 0.1 battle-h: `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalLod") | [.side,.fields.full,.fields.distant,.fields.due,.fields.carried,.fields.navDeferred,.fields.promotions] | @tsv' SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`. Expected: before contact most groups are `distant`; when the enemy is first seen, the groups within 3 km of the sighting show `promotions=observation` in that row's window (the same-tick rule itself is proven by the harness test `ContactEventNeverDelayedByTier`); `parent`, `target`, `bearing` and `corridor` promotions appear while lines shift. Watch: no AI group visibly reacts to a sighting, to its parent division's move or to a new objective later than its neighbours do; a group under fire never sits `distant`; after a replan `navDeferred` falls back to 0 within the bound of BC13 decision 7 (about 25 rows' worth of ticks for 100 groups at most); W&L (Session B): the player's command chain is never counted in `distant`. **Absent:** `tactical-battle-core:lod-failed`.
   35. **BC13, measured saving.** Two runs of one large battle from the same save, through first contact (≥ 10 min real time at 1x, then ≥ 5 min at 20x): `Enable Battle AI Level Of Detail` true, then false. Compare the `PerformanceAggregate` rows `tactical.producer.posture`, `tactical.producer.*` (the five tiered producers), `tactical.arbiter.execute` and `tactical.lod` (meanMs, maxMs, count per 5 s window) and the `FrameHitch` rows. Expected: the tiered producers' summed meanMs per arbiter tick is lower with the flag on (illustrative scale from BC13 decision 9: 0.75-2.1 ms per side per cycle during the approach, 0.3-1.2 ms when engaged, depending on the measured `Distant` share); `tactical.lod` meanMs ≤ 0.2; `tactical.arbiter.execute` unchanged within noise (carried claims are not executed). Record the measured numbers in `docs/handoff.md`. **Outcome rule:** a saving below 0.2 ms per side per cycle on a 500-regiment side is recorded as "no measurable saving; keep the flag on, revisit" (with the tier counts), and BC13 stays on the revisit list in `docs/handoff.md` until a later measurement settles it.
   36. **BC14, doctrinal formations.** `TacticalCommandPosture` and `TacticalFacingPulse` rows carry `targetFormation` and its `reason` (`table`, `assault`, `frontage-line`, `road-route`, `road-column-wait`, `fallback-vanilla`): `jq -r 'select(.event=="TacticalCommandPosture") | [.unit,.fields.task,.fields.targetFormation,.fields.reason] | @tsv' SESSION/tactical*.jsonl`. Watch: attacking and holding brigades with 4 or more regiments stand in two lines; an assaulting brigade closes into attack column on the approach, about 500 m out, and a brigade already in contact keeps its line; reserves wait in column; screens stay in line; a long road march goes in march column (BC10). **Absent:** `tactical-formation:target-failed`.

Watch, in a Commander Mode MonitorOnly run (vanilla owns every AI group there) and on groups outside Whiskey's posture state in Active: a screen-stance or small attack-stance group forms line, not attack column; an assaulting division forms successive lines at its first settled assault-stance cycle, not double line; an assaulting brigade's formation does not change (vanilla's own gate never orders it).

   40. **BC14, supporting lines.** `[once:tactical-formation:support-gap-first] [SupportLine] first support gap applied: group=<id> layout=<1|2> gapMeters=<100|200|300>`, and `TacticalSupportClamp` rows when a gap was shortened (`reason=river|impassable|map-edge|deployment-zone`, `gapMeters`).
