## BC10 — deployment and movement honesty

**Goal:** the AI deploys, arrives and marches on what it can know. Vanilla places AI groups from true enemy positions, puts every group in the front line, re-paths arriving columns over their own orders, and the battle core still has four omniscient or overclaimed movement inputs (covered crossings, charge outflanking, path probe, avenue flags).

**Owned files:**
- Create (pure, test csproj): `Tactical/Battle/BattleTerrainGrid.cs`, `Tactical/Battle/CoveredCrossings.cs`, `Tactical/Battle/PerceivedFlanking.cs`, `Tactical/Battle/PathTerrainScorer.cs`, `Tactical/Battle/RoadRoutePlanner.cs`, `Tactical/Battle/DeploymentDoctrine.cs`.
- Create (runtime): `Tactical/Battle/BattleTerrainGridRuntime.cs`, `Patches/ReinforcementArrivalGuardPatch.cs` (#112).
- Modify: `Tactical/Battle/BattleCoreContracts.cs`, `BattleSnapshotBuilder.cs`, `BattlePerception.cs`, `BattlePerceptionProbe.cs`, `OrderExecutor.cs`, `OrderExecutorRuntime.cs`, `SilentExitPrecheck.cs`, `WaypointValidator.cs`, `Producers/PostureIntentProducer.cs`, `Producers/ChargeIntentProducer.Plan.cs`, `Producers/ReserveReleaseIntentProducer.cs` (via points only), `Tactical/Operations/CommandPostureExecutor.cs` (`PostureIntentRequest`, `PostureIntentPlan.MoveIntent`), `Tactical/Orchestrator/TacticalVisionRuntimeAdapter.cs` (avenue flags only), `Tactical/Orchestrator/DirectChildContracts.cs` / `DirectChildEvidenceBuilder.cs` / `DirectChildAllocator.cs` (deployed-reserve evidence only), `Patches/TacticalDeploymentTerrainDisciplinePatch.cs` (#60), `Patches/TacticalDeploymentObserverPatch.cs` (#58: its `GetVisibleEnemyBearing` call, `:332`, reads the assumed enemy point instead), `Tactical/TacticalTerrainProbe.cs`, `Tactical/TacticalTerrainFacingDiscipline.cs`, `Plugin.cs` (one bind), `tools/smoke-situational.txt`, `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/BattleWriteGateTests.cs` (decision 12), the test csproj, `AGENTS.md` (decision 12); docs listed in the Steps.
- Test file: `BattleMovementHonestyTests.cs`.

### Vanilla findings (build 14703111)

1. **Placement is from true enemy state.** `DoPlacementAIUnitsWithinDeploymentzoneNew` (85524-85892) runs at battle-init frame 30 for the AI side, then for the player side only under `ai_vs_ai` (23988-23995; W&L forces `ai_vs_ai`, 23754-23770). Its objective path (`num3 == 2`: Meeting and Attacker sides on day 1, 85689-85702 and 85758-85761; later days only for a Defender whose fortification search failed, 85735-85738) reads three enemy facts: candidate objectives exclude any objective with an active unrouted enemy unit within `objectivesdetectionrange` (50 m, battleprefs 81), scanned over `completeunitlist` with no fog (85604-85618); day 1 picks the candidate objective nearest to any enemy battle-data group with `reinforcementdelay <= 0.1` (`GetSuitableObjectiveForInitialAIPlacement` 86260-86281, owner own or neutral); facing points at `unitrange.closestenemyunitfar` after `UpdateUnitRangeFast(calculatefull: true)` (85809-85815), which has no fog filter (122658-122663). At frame 30 the first-placed side's enemy still stands at its `DoUnitPositioning` entry-point slots (87765-87790), so the leak there is which entry points are occupied and how strongly; under `ai_vs_ai` the second-placed side reads the first side's placed positions. `CheckForSimilarPositions` (94132-94232) spaces groups by `brigadesinaigroup × 25 m`, up to 8 tries, around the same assumed enemy point.
2. **No reserve echelon at deployment.** Every objective-path group goes to an objective or the zone edge (85770-85860); the fortification path (`num3 == 1`) gives each group its own authored fortification position (85718-85751). Vanilla reserves exist only after state 4 in the objective chains: `AssignReserves` (7017) and `LinkReservesToLineGroup` (6642-6742) chain them `unitlinkdistance` (10 m, unitprefs 1069) plus half depths behind the line centre (124312-124350). The battle core's Reserve role sends them 350-425 m back (`PostureTargets.ReserveStandOff` 350 m, `TacticalBattleLinePlanner.ReserveDistance` 425 m) only from the first arbiter tick, and the arbiter gate refuses state < 5 (`PostureExecutorBattleGate`), so the side deploys everything forward and marches its reserve back after the battle starts.
3. **`SetGroupFormation`'s `placeentrenchments` argument is unused** (signature 91822; the body 91822-92202 never reads it), so a re-placement leaves no stray entrenchment behind.
4. **Arriving reinforcements are re-pathed.** `CheckReinforcementArrivals` (87554-87604) runs every `entrytimedistance` (0.015 game-h, battleprefs 33) once `frame >= 50`; `SetGroupArrival` (87606-87663) places one queued unit per check (all at once in the end-of-day cycle, 87611-87615) and then calls `SetInitialPathForEntryPoint(group, entry, preventstartingpositions: true, ruleoutmanuallymoved: true, …)` (87662). That method (87967-88003) sends the whole group in group formation 1 to the entry's `trooptargetposition` ± 20 m random, facing `entryangle + 180`, through `SetGroupFormation` (not immediate: `RegimentSetPath` per non-detached, non-routed child, 92076-92108; the top group's own `RegimentSetPath` has no detach test, 92058-92063). Its only shield is `ruleoutmanuallymoved`: members with `alreadymanuallymoved` are made `permanentlydetached` for the call and restored after (87991-88003). `alreadymanuallymoved` is written only by the player UI path (`SetManuallyMoved` 138854-138859, called at 166135/166166) and reset in `Regiment` initialisation (112517); nothing else reads it. So vanilla or Whiskey orders to already-arrived members are overwritten every 0.015 game-h until the queue empties; which members that reaches depends on the AI pool level (pooled groups are `permanentlydetached`, 85255-85291). The other call (87841, scheduling at load) is not an arrival.
5. **Covered crossings are omniscient.** `UpdateBlockedCrossings` (7917-7973, every `AIBattle.Update`, 3381) walks `completeunitlist` one unit per frame (no fog) and lists (a) every `bfs.rivercrossingpositions` point (24723; filled from ford 5 and bridge 6 cells de-duplicated at 20 m, 26628-26640) within `1.5 × firerange` of a non-artillery enemy regiment (`unittyp != 2 && <= 13`) whose facing is within 45° of the bearing to it, and (b) the point `firerange` ahead of every enemy regiment with `covervalue > 0.15`, `coverobject != 3`, not routed and without paths. `BlockedMovingOrder` (3054-3150) only needs a `List<Vector3>` (it reads the list in its constructor and keeps none of it), so any perceived list can be handed to it.
6. **Path costs.** Mesh terrain specs set global NavMesh area costs at load (`NavMesh.SetAreaCost(index, terrainspeccost)` 54522); texture specs (forest 14, swamp 15, field 16) get none (54537-54555), so the pathfinder treats woods as grass (cost 2) while units move at `1 / terrainspeccost` (forest 4, swamp 6; `GetTerrainSpeedAdjustment` 132888-132901). `AddPath` re-sets Rivers 500000 (2 frozen), Roads/Bridges 1 (2 when `skiproads`), FencesWalls 2 (500 for types 1/2/4/5 with `pathinterrupted`) before every battle `CalculatePath` (130359-130394). AI units use roads except in line/skirmish/attack column without `useroads`, routed, fallback/charge modes, interrupted, and within `maximumdistancetoobjectiveroadmovement` (250 m, battleprefs 846) of their target (131134-131166). The terrain-id grid `BattlefieldSetup.terrainspecs` (private static `int[,]`, 1 m cells, `[sizeX + 1, sizeZ + 1]`) is built once at load by `ReadInTerrainSpecs` (26550-26670): NavMesh area under the cell, overwritten by the dominant texture only where the area is 0 (so a road through a forest reads road); `GetCurrentTerrainOnPos` (26727-26771) indexes it as `[(int)x, (int)(z + sizeZ)]`, clamped.
7. **Courier moves append.** Under `useorderdelay` `SetWaypoint` forces `newpath = false` and queues the order (`AddToOrderQueue` 124917, only when no move order type is active, 91483-91510) while the path is added in the same call (91540/91559). A second `SetWaypoint(newpath: false)` in the same frame appends a second path and queues its own courier entry (ordertype 0 vs 1, processing time after the first, 124930-124965). Vanilla's own multi-leg AI move is `FlankMove` (117089-117090: `newpath: true` then `newpath: false`, `useorderdelay: false`).
8. **Outflanked is range-only.** `UpdateFlanking` (122959-123175) takes the nearest enemy in each sector of `unitrange.enemyinrangereg` (range-only for the AI side, 122650) within `distancetoenemyforoutflank` (60 m, unitprefs 1260), sectors from `GamePrefs.fireangles[0..3]` relative to the unit's facing (reversed in fallback mode 2), skipping skirmishers, scouts and enemies in melee; a sector is cleared when an own regiment stands closer in it (123094-123111). Codes: 1 left, 2 right, 3 both, 4 rear, 5 left+rear, 6 right+rear, 7 all (123149-123176). `firefromflank` (123206-123264) is fire the unit actually received.
9. **Two setup bugs, already fixed on `main`.** `CalculateAllUnitsOfAlliance` divides the AI side's summed `entrenchmentlevel` by the player side's group count (87397-87399): #98 `BattleEntrenchmentAverageFixPatch`, BUG-TAC-018. `AssignConditionToSubordinates` clamps the group HQ's horse and gun condition with the men value (85470-85471): #99 `BattleHqConditionFixPatch`, BUG-TAC-019. Both shipped on `main` (W&L economy merge, `3fc82a7`); BC10 does not touch them. The research's `list11` duplication (85775-85779) has no effect (both pickers are minimum searches) and is only recorded here.
10. **W&L.** `DoPlacement…New` temporarily re-attaches the W&L current command (`permanentlydetached = false` on day 1, or when not in cover within 150 m of its AI group, 85649-85674) so it is placed with its parent, excludes it when it is its own AI group (list8), and re-detaches it after placement (85884-85887); `IsApplicableForDeployment` (78293-78303) leaves the player's deployable groups to the player. None of the arrival methods has a W&L test (87554-88003): the re-path also reaches player-side columns, except units the player moved by hand.

### Decisions

1. **One per-battle terrain grid (`BattleTerrainGrid`, pure; `BattleTerrainGridRuntime`).** The runtime half reads, once per battle (BattleScope): the `terrainspecs` array by cached reflection (a reference, not a copy), the terrain size, `GamePrefs.terrainspeccost` (speed cost per id), `GameVars.isfrozen()`, and `bfs.rivercrossingpositions` (copied to pooled float arrays with each point's id, 5 ford or 6 bridge). The pure half indexes exactly as `GetCurrentTerrainOnPos` (parity test) and offers `IdAt(x, z)`, `SpeedCost(id)`, `SampleLine(x0, z0, x1, z1, stepMeters, Span<int> ids)`, `Crossings`. Cost: < 0.1 ms per battle (one reflection read and at most a few hundred crossing points). A missing field logs `tactical-battle-core:terrain-grid-missing` once and every consumer below keeps its pre-BC10 behaviour. This is the per-battle grid the battle-core spec's architecture note names (outside the snapshot); BC12 builds its picture on it.
2. **Fog-honest covered crossings.** `CoveredCrossings.Build(BattleSnapshot s, BattleTerrainGrid g, List<Vector3> into)` applies vanilla's two rules (finding 5) to **perceived** enemies only: Visible observations (their record row: position, `RotationY`, `UnitTyp`, `FireRange`, `CoverValue`, `CoverObject`, paths, routed) and Remembered observations with `Confidence01 >= 0.5` (seen within the last 20 battle-minutes) at their last-seen values. The list is pooled per side and built at most once per arbiter tick, only when a move carries `AvoidCoveredCrossings`. `OrderExecutorRuntime.BlockedCrossings` and its reflection of `AIBattle.blockedcrossings` are deleted with the OnceLog keys `tactical-battle-core:blocked-crossings-missing` / `-failed`. Vanilla's own line-group use of its list (6953-6956) stays vanilla. Contract (additive): `UnitRecord.FireRange` (captured for own and enemy rows); `EnemyObservation.Heading`, `FireRange`, `CoverValue`, `CoverObject`, `Standing` (last seen while Visible, BC8 precedent).
3. **Perceived outflanking.** `PerceivedFlanking.Code(cx, cz, facing, fallbackMode, threats, screens, in FlankGeometry g)` reproduces finding 8 with explicit inputs (`FlankGeometry`: `fireangles[0..3]`, `distancetoenemyforoutflank`, read once per battle). The assembler fills `UnitRecord.Outflanked` from it after the perception probe (the builder's raw `reg.outflanked` read, `BattleSnapshotBuilder.cs:301`, is deleted): own units take threats from Visible observations (record rows; skirmisher/scout and in-melee rows skipped) and screens from own regiments; Visible enemy rows take threats from own regiments and screens from the other Visible enemy rows. Every existing reader becomes fog-honest at once: the charge producer's own bar (`ChargeIntentProducer.Plan.cs:271`) and target flank (`:295`), `BattleWriterPolicy.PerceivedFlankRisk` and `ArmyEvidenceBuilder.MeanFlankThreatByGroup`. `UnitRecord.FireFromFlank` stays vanilla's (fire the unit received). Cost: threats and screens come from a per-snapshot bucket grid of 60 m cells (`RangeMeters`) over own and Visible enemy rows (pooled, built once per snapshot), so each unit reads only its own cell and the 8 around it; scope `tactical.perceived-flanking` (0.5 ms per snapshot build).
4. **Non-mutating path probe.** `PostureTargets.ApplyVanillaPathAreaCosts` (the global `NavMesh.SetAreaCost` loop, `PostureIntentProducer.cs:945-961`) is deleted. The probe builds one `NavMeshQueryFilter` per tick (`areaMask = NavMesh.AllAreas`, `agentTypeID = 0` as vanilla's `CalculatePath(…, -1, …)` overload; every area's cost copied from `NavMesh.GetAreaCost(a)`, `a` in 0-31, then the four `PostureNavAreaCosts` values) and calls `NavMesh.CalculatePath(from, to, filter, path)`. `PostureNavAreaCosts` and its R5 pin test stay; the OnceLog key `tactical-producer-posture:area-cost-failed` becomes `tactical-producer-posture:nav-filter-failed`. The BC7 writer list no longer needs a footnote for the producer's global write.
5. **Honest path scores (`PathTerrainScorer`, pure).** The probe samples each candidate path's polyline every 10 m on the grid instead of reading corner ids: `roadPreference` = road (7) plus bridge (6) share of the samples (was `corners <= 3 ? 0.65 : 0.25`, `PostureIntentProducer.cs:981`); `terrainRisk` 0.85 when any sample is river (4, not frozen) or fence/wall (8), never for a bridge or ford (was 4/8/6 at `:990-994`); `bridgeRisk` (a choke) 0.55 when the path uses a crossing cell (5 or 6) and 0.70 when it also passes within 2 m of river or fence (`NearCrossingTerrain` kept); `deadGround` = 0.70 when a `Map`-layer `Physics.Raycast` from the threat's eye (+4 m, `DrawVisibility` 101061-101063) to the candidate's eye (+4 m) hits terrain, else 0 (was a distance proxy, `:1002`), computed only with a threat. `TacticalMovementCostField` weights are unchanged (its pinned tests stay green); `TacticalPathQualitySample`'s field names now mean what they say.
6. **Road march routing (`RoadRoutePlanner`, pure).** For a planned Move (posture pipeline, division play, reserve release) of a group that is not close-engaged, with task AdvanceToAssembly, FormUp, AttackObjective, SupportAttack, FixEnemy, ReserveWait or ReleaseReserve, at least `MinRouteMeters` = 400 m long, whose straight line samples (10 m) are at least `WoodedShareTrigger` = 0.30 forest (14) or swamp (15): an A* over the grid on a `RouteCellMeters` = 25 m lattice inside a corridor `0.6 ×` the move length wide, narrowed until the lattice fits `MaxCells` = 6,000 cells (no route when that leaves less than `MinCorridorShare` = 0.2 × the move length; a 4 km move is 160 cells long and fits 37 cells, 925 m, across: 0.23 × its length), cost = cell length × `terrainspeccost[id]` (river impassable unless frozen; finding 6), compared with the time of the route vanilla's pathfinder would take (the filter path of decision 4, sampled with the same costs). When the A* route costs at most `RouteGainRatio` = 0.80 of vanilla's, the intent carries up to `MaxVia` = 2 via points: the A* route's first road (7/6) entry and last road exit, each at least 60 m (`OrderArbiter.DedupeMeters`) from start, target and each other, and none within the last 250 m (vanilla skips roads there, 131164). Budget: one route plan per side per arbiter tick (the posture `NavPlanBudget`), cached per (group, 50 m target bucket) for 0.25 doctrine-h. A move with `AvoidCoveredCrossings` and a non-empty perceived list takes `BlockedMovingOrder` and no via points (safety before speed). Contract (additive): `BattleOrderIntent.ViaCount`, `ViaX0`, `ViaZ0`, `ViaX1`, `ViaZ1`; the executor validates each via point like the target (`WaypointValidator`), issues `SetWaypoint(via0, newpath: true, useorderdelay as planned)`, then `SetWaypoint(via1)` / `SetWaypoint(target)` with `newpath: false` and the same `useorderdelay` in the same call (finding 7), and judges the outcome (D6) on the final target; dedupe compares the final target only. **Column march.** A routed Move is planned only for a group whose `groupformation` is 4 (March Column; the group formation names are at 5883). `SetGroupFormation` maps group formation 4 to regiment formation 3, march column (91909-91917), the narrowest layout vanilla has: every group formation lays a brigade's regiments abreast (`BuildGroupOffsetPositions` 92203, brigade layout `num2` 91920-91954), so only 4 keeps a brigade's frontage near a road's width. For a group in another formation the posture pipeline, which owns both the route and the group's formation target, takes group formation 4 as that group's formation target in place of `CommandFormationCorrection.TargetFormationForTask` while the route is planned, so the arbiter sees one Formation intent for the group (the executor's existing `IssueFormation` → vanilla `SetFormation` index 4, 90971-91050, courier-delayed like any formation order); the routed Move waits until the snapshot reports formation 4, at most `ColumnWaitHours` = 0.1 doctrine-h (which also bounds a tug-of-war with vanilla's own `AdjustGroupFormations` on a settled group); after that the Move goes unrouted. On arrival the posture pipeline's ordinary formation target applies again, whatever it is (BC10 does not change it), and vanilla's regiments leave march column by themselves near the end of their path or on contact (127669-127682). **Leg headings.** Each via leg carries `manualfinalrotation` = the bearing from that via point to the next point (the next via point or the target), so the leg's `SetGroupFormation` (91559) lays the column's frontage across the road; the final leg carries the intent's heading. The executor computes the leg headings from the points (no contract field). **Why via points.** AI regiments have `useroads = true` from initialisation (112853), so regiment formation does not gate their road use (the formation 0/1/2 skip at 131135-131138 applies only with `useroads` off, which vanilla clears only around its own micro advance and fallback paths, 4117-4147 and 4854-4859); each regiment's pathfinder already takes roads at area cost 1 against 2 for open ground (130359-130394), except within 250 m of its target (131163-131166), in movement modes 1/2/6, interrupted, routed or as skirmishers (131139-131162). Woods carry no area cost (finding 6), so that pathfinder cuts straight through a forest; the via points pull the group onto the road around it.
7. **Fog-honest deployment (#60, `DeploymentDoctrine`, pure).** Vanilla still runs its placement; #60's Postfix then re-plans the objective-path groups of this call with fog-honest inputs and re-places those whose choice differs:
   - *Assumed enemy point* (one rule for objective choice, facing and spacing), by the side's snapshot perception. Under `RangeOnly` (every day-1 deployment: `GridNotPainted`, BC2b) no observation is used, because vanilla's AI range lists admit enemy units within 150-450 m with no fog or line-of-sight test (122650; the reach is `BattlePerception.cs:169-172`), and under `ai_vs_ai` those include the first-placed side's placed groups; the point is the nearest **enemy-owned entry point** to the group (entry points and their owners are public scenario data; BC9 precedent), else the bearing to the map centre that `DoUnitPositioning` already used (87789-87790). Under `FogGrid` (a later-day deployment with a painted grid) the strength-weighted centroid of Visible observations comes first, then the entry point, then the map centre.
   - *Candidates*: the same objectives vanilla considers (own zone, not enemy zone, not already taken this pass, owner own or neutral; `frontline2.CheckIfWithinZone` matrix computed by the runtime half), with the 50 m enemy filter applied to Visible observations under `FogGrid` and to enemy-owned entry points under `RangeOnly`. Day 1 picks the candidate nearest the assumed enemy point; later days the nearest to the group (vanilla 86285-86301, which has no enemy input). Facing = bearing to the assumed enemy point. Spacing re-uses vanilla's `CheckForSimilarPositions` rule (radius `brigadesinaigroup × 25 m`, 8 tries, 45° steps) around the assumed point.
   - A group is re-placed only when its fog-honest objective differs from the one vanilla used (vanilla's placement within its own spacing radius of that objective) or its facing differs by more than `DeploymentFacingToleranceDegrees` = 20°; otherwise #60 leaves it.
   - *Rejected alternative:* snapshot/restore of `BattleUnits.completeunitlist` around vanilla's call (AGENTS #25 pattern). It would make the objective filter and the facing honest, but `GetSuitableObjectiveForInitialAIPlacement` reads the `grp` array (86267-86281), not the list, so day-1 choice would still be omniscient; and emptying a static list other code reads during the call needs user approval. Re-planning in the Postfix needs neither.
8. **Deployment reserve echelon (`DeploymentDoctrine.ProposeReserve` / `ApplyReserveClamp`).** In the same Postfix pass: the army's direct children (snapshot D5 tree, `DirectChildDiscovery` level) that own at least one objective-path group placed in this call, contain no `PlayerOwned` unit (and not the W&L current command), and are not cavalry-only or artillery-only. With `N >= 3` such children, the `floor(N / 3)` children whose fog-honest placements lie farthest from the assumed enemy point become the reserve. *Propose (pure, `ProposeReserve`):* each reserve child gets one translation vector, from its own placement centroid to a point `ReserveDepthMeters` = 425 m (the ledger's `ReserveDistance`) behind the centroid of the other children's placements, along the axis away from the assumed enemy point, reserve children spread on `±90 m` lanes (`TacticalBattleLinePlanner.ReserveLane`). Every group of that child moves by the same vector, so the child's groups keep their fog-honest relative offsets (and decision 7's spacing) instead of stacking on one point; the unclamped group targets go into the plan with `Kind = Reserve`. *Clamp (runtime, then pure `ApplyReserveClamp`):* #60's runtime clamps each proposed group target into the own deployment zone with vanilla's `frontline2.GetClosestPointInDeploymentZone` (102819) and passes the clamped points to `ApplyReserveClamp`, which keeps a child in reserve only when every one of its clamped targets still lies at least `MinReserveDepthMeters` = 150 m behind the line centroid along the axis; otherwise that child's groups revert to their fog-honest placements (`Kind` Kept or Replaced). Fortification placements (`num3 == 1`) stay vanilla's authored lines (BUG-TAC-013 boundary), so a Defender whose groups all take fortifications gets no deployment reserve (residual, recorded). *Allocator alignment (no patch-written state):* each reserve group's placement is an executor inline Move with `IntentSource.DeploymentReserve` (the other re-placements carry `IntentSource.Deployment`; both appended). `DirectChildEvidenceBuilder` sets `DirectChildEvidence.DeployedReserve` for a child when, at the side's first allocation after the deployment pass, the last Move outcome of any of its groups (`OrderExecutor.TryGetLastOutcome(groupId, OrderChannel.Move, out o)`, `OrderExecutor.cs:781`) has `o.Source == IntentSource.DeploymentReserve` and `o.Kind` Issued or Moved. The outcome table is executor bookkeeping written by the executor's own pipeline (battle-core spec D4), not by the patch. That allocation gives the child `DirectChildRole.Reserve` ahead of the main-effort pick and the SupportMain fall-through; afterwards the allocator's role memory keeps it and only `ReleaseReserves` (crisis, Exploit, odds; BC11 adds the threatened flank) changes it, as for any Reserve child.
9. **#60 writes through the executor.** #60's `SetGroupFormation(immediateplacement: true, …)` becomes an executor inline Move with `IntentFlags.ImmediatePlacement` (= 1024, additive), `IntentSource.Deployment` (appended), `Priority.Executor`, issued by `OrderExecutorRuntime.TryPlanInline` / `ExecutePlannedInline`; the executor issues exactly #60's call (immediate placement, `ignoredeplyomentzone: false`, `newpath: true`) and then vanilla's cleanup on the placed tree (`lastsetwaypointposition` and `immediateunitplacement` cleared, 85873-85883, as #60 does today). D6 for an immediate placement: `Moved` when the group stands within `DedupeMeters` of the target at the next observation, else `Failed` (no retry: the arbiter is not running at deployment). This removes #60 from the BC7 "other writers" list. #60 also (a) acts only on groups vanilla moved in this call (a non-skipping Prefix records positions; research C(5)); (b) skips any group that is the W&L current command's command ancestor while vanilla's temporary re-attach was in effect (finding 10), so the player's command and its parent stay together; (c) faces a terrain correction with the assumed enemy point of decision 7 instead of the range-only visible bearing (`TacticalTerrainProbe.GetVisibleEnemyBearing` is deleted; #58's observer row reads the same assumed point). An immediate placement skips the executor's friendly-footprint, dedupe and cooldown checks (`OrderExecutorRuntime.cs:231-262`): the snapshot is stale after the pass's first re-placement, and spacing among this pass's groups is decision 7's `CheckForSimilarPositions` rule and decision 8's kept offsets; `WaypointValidator`'s terrain checks and the write guard still apply.
10. **Arrival re-path guard (#112 `ReinforcementArrivalGuardPatch`), smoke-sized first.** Non-skipping Prefix + Finalizer on `BattleUnits.SetInitialPathForEntryPoint`, acting only when `ruleoutmanuallymoved == true` (the 87662 call). *Step A, sizing (lands first, deploys, smokes):* the Prefix records each arrived (active) member, its last waypoint and whether it or a command ancestor strictly below the arriving group holds a live executor Move (`OrderExecutor.TryGetLastOutcome` Issued or Moved, target not yet reached, issued after the group's first arrival); the Finalizer counts members whose `lastsetwaypointposition` moved more than 60 m and emits one `TacticalArrivalRepath` Decision row per call (`group`, `arrived`, `queued`, `repathed`, `repathedOrdered`, `shielded`). *Step B, guard:* the Prefix sets `alreadymanuallymoved = true` on each such ordered member that `TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite(Regiment)` allows (never a player-owned or feuding unit) and vanilla had not already marked; vanilla then detaches it for the call (87991-88000); the Finalizer restores every value it changed. The arriving top group itself is not shielded (vanilla re-paths it unconditionally): `UnitFlags.Arriving` (= 8388608, additive; the builder marks a group whose `scheduledarrival` entry still has queued units, read once per build) and `SilentExitPrecheck` → `SkipReason.Arriving` refuse a Move, Formation or Facing on that group, so Whiskey lets vanilla assemble it at its staging point and orders its arrived sub-groups instead. **Go/no-go for step B:** the sizing smoke (smoke item 2.21) must show at least one `TacticalArrivalRepath` row with `repathedOrdered > 0` in a battle with a multi-unit reinforcement group; if three arrivals of multi-unit groups all show `repathedOrdered = 0`, step B's shield is not built, the finding is recorded in `docs/bug-fixes/vanilla-tactical-battlefield.md` (BUG-TAC-022) as not reproduced, and the sizing row stays as telemetry under `Enable Tactical Observer`. If fewer than three multi-unit arrivals have been observed (all at 0) when the rest of BC10 is ready, step B waits and BC10 merges without it. The integrator deploys step A (implementer seats do not deploy).
11. **Avenue flags (`TacticalVisionRuntimeAdapter`).** `roadAnchored` (`:800`, today true for every entry point with a `trooptargetposition`) = the entry → target segment's 10 m samples are at least 0.5 road (7) or bridge (6), or the entry point lies within 20 m of a road cell. `crossingAnchored` (`IsCrossingAnchored` `:1161-1178`, today a river or fence within 3 m of three points) = the segment's samples hit a ford (5) or bridge (6) cell, or it passes within 50 m of a vanilla `rivercrossingpositions` point. Intentional behaviour change: fewer avenues read as road-anchored, so `TacticalDefensiveLineAnchorPlanner` uses its generic 300 m intercept and 760 m frontage more often than the road 360 m / 620 m.
12. **Write-rule bookkeeping.** `AGENTS.md` § Patch hygiene (battle write owners) changes in the slice's docs commit: #60's deployment `SetGroupFormation` leaves the list of other battle writers (it is an executor inline write after decision 9), and #112 is named as a snapshot/restore steer of vanilla's `Regiment.alreadymanuallymoved` (set only on units `TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite` allows, restored by its Finalizer, read by vanilla only in `SetInitialPathForEntryPoint`, 87996). `BattleWriteGateTests.VanillaBattleWrite` gains `|\.alreadymanuallymoved\s*=(?!=)` with a fixture assertion, so a file that writes the field without a commander-mode check fails the gate.
13. **Rollback.** `Tactical` / `Enable Deployment And March Doctrine` (default true): decisions 6, 7, 8 and 10B. Bug fixes outside the flag: 1-5, 9 (the executor route of #60's existing write, and its restriction to vanilla-moved groups and the W&L parent skip), 10A (telemetry), 11.

### Interfaces — Produces

```csharp

  }

    public static int BuildPoints(BattleSnapshot s, BattleTerrainGrid g, float[] xs, float[] zs); }   // pure; OrderExecutorRuntime copies the points into its pooled List<Vector3>
  public struct FlankGeometry { public float FrontFrom, FrontTo, LeftFrom, RightTo; public float RangeMeters; } // fireangles[0..3], distancetoenemyforoutflank

        ReadOnlySpan<float> screenX, ReadOnlySpan<float> screenZ, int screens, in FlankGeometry g);   // 0-7 as 123149-123176
    public static void Apply(BattleSnapshot s, in FlankGeometry g);                                     // fills UnitRecord.Outflanked, own and Visible enemy rows; 60 m bucket grid
  }

  public static class PathTerrainScorer { public const float SampleMeters = 10f;
    public static PathTerrainScore Score(BattleTerrainGrid g, ReadOnlySpan<float> cornerX, ReadOnlySpan<float> cornerZ, int corners); }
  public struct RoadRoute { public bool Use; public int ViaCount; public float ViaX0, ViaZ0, ViaX1, ViaZ1; public float RouteCost, VanillaCost; }
  public static class RoadRoutePlanner {
    public const float MinRouteMeters = 400f, WoodedShareTrigger = 0.30f, RouteCellMeters = 25f, RouteGainRatio = 0.80f, NoRoadNearTargetMeters = 250f, MinCorridorShare = 0.2f, ColumnWaitHours = 0.1f;
    public const int ColumnGroupFormation = 4;                             // March Column (5883); regiments formation 3 (91909-91917)
    public const int MaxCells = 6000, MaxVia = 2;

  }
  public sealed class RoadRouteScratch { /* pooled per side: binary-heap open set, closed bits, g-costs and parents, each sized MaxCells; grow-only */ }

    public bool PlayerOwned, CavalryOnly, ArtilleryOnly, WlCommandAncestor; }

    public const int SpacingTries = 8; public const float SpacingStepDegrees = 45f;
    public static bool AssumedEnemyPoint(BattleSnapshot s, float gx, float gz, ReadOnlySpan<float> enemyEntryX, ReadOnlySpan<float> enemyEntryZ, int entries,
        float mapCentreX, float mapCentreZ, out float x, out float z, out AssumedEnemySource source);   // RangeOnly: entry point, else centre; FogGrid: Visible first
    public static int PlanPlacements(BattleSnapshot s, DeploymentGroup[] groups, int groupCount, DeploymentObjective[] objectives, int objectiveCount,
        bool[,] objectiveInOwnZone, bool[,] objectiveInEnemyZone, int ownAlliance, bool firstDay, ReadOnlySpan<float> enemyEntryX, ReadOnlySpan<float> enemyEntryZ, int entries,
        float mapCentreX, float mapCentreZ, DeploymentPlan into);                       // zone matrices are [objective, group] as CheckIfWithinZone(objective, alliance, group) 85771-85774
    public static int ProposeReserve(DeploymentPlan plan, DeploymentGroup[] groups, int groupCount);   // unclamped targets, Kind = Reserve; one vector per child, offsets kept

Commits: `b8a1cdf` (#112 step A, record-only), `133206e` (terrain grid), `603c0f0` (covered crossings), `68cdb3c` (perceived outflanking), `4817780` (path probe), `5975928` (road march routing), the deployment commits (`DeploymentDoctrine`/executor immediate placement/deployed-reserve evidence, then #60 on the executor), the avenue-flag commit and the blockedcrossings pin; the last commit of the slice also carries the docs. Gates at the last commit: `./build.sh` 0 warnings; test build `0 Warning(s) 0 Error(s)`; harness exit 0, `^PASS` 2368 (base 2281, +87), 0 `^FAIL`; `config-audit` 0 findings over 135 entries; `orphan-sweep` 0, `--include-test-only` 29 (base 29); `tools/test_smoke_check.py` `OK` (7 tests); `tools/smoke-check.py` parses every `PatchFirstFire.Mark` id (the two #60 ids are expected, the two #112 ids are in the situational list). Nothing was deployed (the integrator deploys). Mutation evidence (17 plants in round 1, 11 in round 2 and 4 in round 3 (order-independent spacing, locked claim, allocation every 4th call and every call), in a scratch copy, restored from byte copies and checksum-verified, all killed with exit 134): the confidence bar of remembered covered crossings, the friend clearing of a flank sector, the bridge-as-road split of the scorer, the router's gain ratio, the visible-only objective filter, the RangeOnly entry-point rule, the column-formation gate, the reserve share (floor(N/3) read as N/2), two runtime-wiring source pins (the immediate-placement flag on #60's placement, the builder re-reading `reg.outflanked`), the main pick ignoring a deployed reserve, the executor's immediate placement being deduped, the direct-child walk, spacing counting kept groups, the avenue road-share bar, the Plugin's grid-provider bind and the reserve-source carry.

What changed against the plan (each is a finding or a deliberate deviation, with anchors):

- **`terrainspecs` is public.** `BattlefieldSetup.terrainspecs` is `public static int[,]` (26550-26670), not private as finding 6 says; the grid still reads it through cached reflection so a rename downgrades to a logged warning (`terrain-grid-missing`).
- **Finding 7 is wrong about the second leg.** A second same-frame `SetWaypoint(newpath: false)` queues **no** courier entry of its own: `AddToOrderQueue` (124917) is skipped while an order of that type is active (91483-91510), and `SetOrderStatus` (125488-125506) activates every path added in the call when the one order is delivered. The two-leg march relies on that (one courier order, all legs), which is what decision 6 wanted; only the plan's claim was off. Recorded in `docs/vanilla-vision-and-movement.md`.
- **The allocator has no role memory** (decision 8: "afterwards the allocator's role memory keeps it"). `DirectChildAllocator` is stateless per cycle apart from `MainRoleHysteresisState`. Built instead: `DirectChildEvidence.DeployedReserve` is derived every cycle from the executor's last Move outcome (`DeploymentDoctrine.IsDeployedReserve`: `Source == DeploymentReserve`, Issued or Moved, for the child or any unit under it) and is part of the evidence signature. It therefore lasts until another source issues a Move to the child's groups (the posture producer's own `ReserveWait` move clears it), after which the evidence path decides: an uncommitted strong child (own bucket at least 2, no contact) is `Reserve` there too, a weaker one falls through to SupportMain. Smoke item 2.23 watches for a child leaving `Reserve` in the first minutes; if it does, the fix is allocator-side memory (a follow-up, not BC10).
- **W&L parent skip is conservative** (decision 9b). The Prefix records `currentcommand.groupaiobjectreg`; that AI group is `Locked` (never re-placed, never terrain-corrected, still holds the objective vanilla gave it, as vanilla's `used` list does, 85766) whether or not vanilla's re-attach condition (`!(flag3 && flag4) || day1`, 85649-85674) held. Any group whose snapshot record is `PlayerOwned`, `PlayerCommand` or `AncestorOfPlayerCommand` is also locked (`DeploymentDoctrine.IsLockedGroup`, one rule for the planner and the terrain discipline loop), and the direct child that holds a player unit is never a reserve.
- **`CheckForSimilarPositions` substitution** (decision 7). Vanilla's ring base angle comes from `unitrange.flankposition[2]` (94132), which Whiskey cannot read fog-honestly; the doctrine starts the ring toward the group's old position instead. Neighbours are every group vanilla placed in the call (vanilla's `list5` holds each objective-path group it placed, 85870): Kept and Locked groups stand where they are whatever their place in grp order (a locked group claims its objective first), and a re-placed front is spaced from all of them and from the re-placed groups before it; compared in the line-front frame (vanilla compares HQ transforms: a depth + back offset). The fallback branch (`list11.Count == 0`, `GetClosestPositionToObjectiveInZone`, 85785-85790) has no candidate objective, so vanilla's placement stands for such a group.
- **Frames.** Every plan position is a line front (the `targetpos` frame); vanilla's HQ is converted with `BattleWriterPolicy.DeploymentLineFrontFromHq` and the executor's outcome check uses the new inverse `DeploymentHqFromLineFront` (test `DeploymentFramesRoundTripAtAnyFacing`). A placement's outcome is judged by the group's position within `DedupeMeters` of that HQ at the next observation, not by `lastsetwaypointposition`, which the cleanup clears.
- **Immediate placement** also sends no W&L dispatch and carries `Task = None` (no task hold); it refuses regiment targets (a fanned-out group's regiment children), counted `refused`.
- **Round-1 verify fixes (r2).** (F1) the terrain discipline loop skips locked groups; (F2) `DeployedReserve` outranks play zone roles in `AllocateWithZones` (the zone row for such a child is ignored) and follows the severe-overmatch Fallback check; (F4) the flag ends on a later Move of another source anywhere in the child's subtree (the release producer moves the child node, not the placed groups) and `DeployedReserveMemory` keeps a role-released child from returning to Reserve; (F3) spacing counts every vanilla-placed group, in any order; (F5) the patch runs while either `Enable Deployment And March Doctrine` or `Enable Tactical Deployment Terrain Discipline` is on, the terrain correction needs its own flag (the dependency of the doctrine on the terrain flag was accidental and is removed); (F6) the covered-point buffers grow to 1024 and a cut is logged once (`covered-crossings-truncated`); (F7) `RoutePlanCache` prunes expired routes and is bounded.
- **Allocation tests.** `PerceivedFlankingAllocatesNothingAfterWarmup` failed once in three runs with 1920 bytes of runtime bookkeeping on the calling thread; the four zero-allocation tests now use `AssertNoAllocation` (30 warm-up calls, then at least six of eight windows must allocate nothing; planted allocations on every call and on every 4th call both fail, 0 of 8 clean; the route test's window is eight calls).
- **A terrain correction keeps the reserve source.** The discipline's correction of a group the executor placed as a reserve is issued as `Deployment`, which would replace the reserve's outcome; `DeploymentDoctrine.PlacementSource` carries `DeploymentReserve` through when the group's last Move outcome is a reserve placement (Issued or Moved), so the child stays a deployed reserve (test `TerrainCorrectedReserveKeepsItsReserveSource`; the pure rule was added after a review of the transcript, with its proof a mutation, not a red-first run).
- **One reserve constant.** `DeploymentDoctrine.ReserveDepthMeters` and `ReserveLaneMeters` are `TacticalBattleLinePlanner.ReserveDistance` / `ReserveLaneMeters` (made `internal`; `TacticalReserveAssemblyPlanner.PreferredReserveDistance` reads the same constant), so the ledger's reserve stand-off, the assembly planner's and the deployment echelon's 425 m are one value.
- **Reserve clamp failure.** `GetClosestPointInDeploymentZone` returns the default vector when it finds no point (102819): for a reserve front that counts as depth 0 (the child reverts); for a re-placed front the unclamped front is used and `SetGroupFormation(ignoredeplyomentzone: false)` clamps it.
- **Vanilla-moved-only** (decision 9a): a group counts as placed by vanilla in this call when its position changed by more than 1 m since the Prefix. This is also a behaviour change for the terrain discipline, which used to look at every eligible group.
- **Contracts.** `CoveredCrossings.BuildPoints` gained `out int crossingPoints` (the log line splits crossings from cover points). `ArrivalShield.Select` takes `IBattleUnitView` and has no `arrivalStartHours` (step A only measures). `DeploymentGroup` gained `Locked`. `PerceptionProbeResult`/`EnemyObservation` carry the five last-seen fields, `UnitRecord.FireRange` the own and enemy fire range. `ChargeDoctrineTests` fixtures were made internal so `ChargeBarsOnPerceivedOutflankNotRaw` reuses them.
- **Avenue seam.** `TacticalVisionRuntimeAdapter` is compiled into the harness and cannot see runtime classes, so it reads the grid through `BattleTerrainGrid.Current()`, a provider the plugin binds in `Awake` (`BattleTerrainGridRuntime.Get`); null in the harness. A missing grid anchors nothing (the old `SearchTerrainInRangePos` 3 m probe and the always-true target flag are deleted).
- **One flag, two scopes renamed.** `Enable Deployment And March Doctrine` gates the road routing and the deployment re-placement/reserve; the terrain discipline keeps its own flag. #60's scope became `tactical.patch.deployment-terrain.prefix` / `.postfix` (AGENTS multi-method naming; the first-fire ids come from source, so no list change beyond the situational #112 ids).
- **`TacticalEnemyBearingEvidence`** keeps its type; `Visible` now means "a bearing exists" (the assumed point), `Strength` is 0. `GetVisibleEnemyBearing` and its helper `IsBearingEligibleEnemy` are deleted.
- **Not built (step B and its tests).** The `alreadymanuallymoved` shield, `UnitFlags.Arriving`, `SkipReason.Arriving`, `ArrivingGroupMoveSkipped`, `ArrivingSubGroupNotSkipped`, `ShieldRestoresExactPriorValues` and the `BattleWriteGateTests` "ungated alreadymanuallymoved write" case wait on smoke item 2.21 (decision 10). In their place: `DeploymentWritesOnlyThroughTheExecutor` (the executor runtime is the only `SetGroupFormation` caller in src; #60 leaves the BC7 writer list) and `VanillaBlockedCrossingsListIsNeverRead`.
- **Process.** `DeploymentDoctrine.cs` was written before its first tests (a context reset between the plan reading and the test file); its tests, and `DirectChildOf`/`Locked`/`SpacingCountsOnlyRedeployedGroups`, were green on first run or fixed to the vanilla rule; their proof is the mutation list above. The other steps were red first (`red-*.txt` evidence: compile errors or a named assertion).
- **No transpilers, no IL copies.** HarmonyX 2.10.2's `CodeInstruction.Clone()` hazard (hotfix `dce672e` on main) does not apply: BC10 adds no transpiler, so no `TranspilerLabelTests`-style test.
- **Ordinals.** `ReinforcementArrivalGuardPatch` is #112 and its bug row BUG-TAC-022, assigned on merging `main` `8099aaa` into `feat/battle-next` (main's highest patch is #111 and highest tactical bug id BUG-TAC-021). BC11 onward name their patches and bugs without numbers; they are assigned at their own merge.

---

## BC11 — flank response and envelopment execution

**Goal:** the AI answers a flank threat as a formation and executes the envelopments its plays choose. Today the only live turning is vanilla's per-regiment turn to the nearest enemy, Whiskey's refused flank never reaches vanilla, and FlankMarch / wing / DeepRaid plays only relabel roles.

**Owned files:**
- Create (pure): `Tactical/Battle/FlankFacing.cs`, `Tactical/Orchestrator/Plays/EnvelopmentManeuver.cs`.
- Create (runtime): `Patches/FlankThreatTallyFixPatch.cs` (the flank tally patch (#113)).
- Modify: `Tactical/Battle/BattleCoreContracts.cs` (`UnitRecord.ReceivedFire`, `UnitRecord.LastAiOrder`), `BattleSnapshotBuilder.cs`, `BattleCoreTick.cs` (`TacticalVanillaFlankMove` row), `Tactical/Orchestrator/Plays/PlayRect.cs` (`Envelops`), `Tactical/Operations/CommandPostureExecutor.cs` (`CommandFormationWritePolicy`, `PostureSense.RefuseFor`, `PostureIntentRequest`), `Tactical/Battle/OrderExecutor.cs`, `OrderExecutorRuntime.cs` (in-place refuse), `Producers/PostureIntentProducer.cs` (pressure facing, maneuver facing), `Tactical/Orchestrator/Plays/PlayAnchor.cs`, `PlayExecutorRuntime.cs`, `PlayTemplateCatalog.cs` (`Envelops` arguments, comments), `Tactical/Orchestrator/DirectChildContracts.cs`, `DirectChildAllocator.cs`, `TacticalBattleCoordinatorRuntime.cs` (zone-assignment call only), `ArmyOrchestrator.cs` (maneuver table accessor only), `TacticalOperationsLedgerRuntime.cs`, `Tactical/Operations/CommandDoctrineAssignment.cs`, `CommandDoctrineOrder.cs`, `Plugin.cs` (two binds), `docs/bug-fixes/vanilla-tactical-battlefield.md`, the test csproj.
- Test file: `FlankResponseTests.cs`.

### Vanilla findings (build 14703111)

1. **Turning.** Vanilla's live AI turning is per regiment toward its own closest enemy: `StopMicroUnitsCloseToEnemyAndRotateTowards` (4539-4613; called over the AI groups in turn at 4987 and for single operations at 5031) takes `closestenemyunitfar` within `distancetoturntowardsenemy` (40 m, battleprefs 739), or within the regiment's fire range once it has received fire (4560-4565), and rotates the regiment to it when the angle is at least `angletriggerforturningtowardsenemy` (25°, battleprefs 743; more in cover, 4602), the enemy is within 1.5 × fire range and the regiment has no path (4606-4608); its skip list (4566) holds no state that means "keep this facing". Contact halts 117271-117375. The group change of front (`UpdateDefensivePositions` 4615 → `CheckDefensiveFacing`, `MostEnemiesAreOutsideAngle` 4784) is dead: its gate `!PerformAIActionDLCWL(aigroup, aigroup)` (4617) is false whenever the call returns true (5101-5115, true outside W&L and for any AI-stance group inside it). Its prefs (battleprefs 851-854, 872-879) are inert, and `enemystrengthwithinangle` has no live vanilla decision reader.
2. **Refused flank.** `SetGroupFormation(refuseflank)` (92095-92097 → `SkewedPosition` 78109-78168) moves each child straight back from its slot by `pow(t | 1 − t, refuseflankskew) × groupwidth × refuseflankdepth` (unitprefs 4 and 0.5; `t = (x + W/2) / W`, direction 0 left, 1 right, 2 both) and turns it to the skewed line (78155-78167). Only the player UI refuses (`RefuseFlank` callers 203696, 203708). Vanilla re-forms an AI group only when it is settled and out of combat (`AdjustGroupFormations` 5886: `combatstatussubordinates <= 0`, not moving, not in cover).
3. **Why Whiskey's refuse never fires.** `PostureSense.RefuseFor` (`CommandPostureExecutor.cs:908-924`) needs `closeEngaged` (melee below, or a visible enemy within `CloseContactMeters` = 250 m, `:810`). `CommandFormationWritePolicy.Decide` (`:217-251`) then gives `RotateInPlace` (which drops the refuse, `OrderExecutorRuntime.cs:476-491`) or "combat-no-relay" in combat, and out of combat refuses the wheel whenever `GroupEnemiesInRange > 0`, which the executor computes over a reach of at least 250 m (`OrderExecutorRuntime.cs:556-569`). A visible enemy within 250 m is therefore always "in range" and `refuseflank` is -1. Tests cover `RefuseFor` and `Decide` apart (`PostureMigrationTests`), never together.
4. **Flank attack.** `CheckFlankMoves` (6247-6312) lets the outermost line group of a chain flank when its `GetGroupStrength` ≥ `strengthtriggerfororderflanking[macroai]` (battleprefs 672-678, strength ratios: assault 1.0, attack 1.3, defend 2.2) and the inner neighbour's is below 3 (strength ratio), idle (`lastaiorder == 0`), with at most a 0.25 share of its subordinates moving; it returns early for `macroai` < 0 or 3 (6253); `GetGroupStrength` returns 0 for a group not in combat (6032-6035). `FlankMove` (116929-117108) is a two-leg hook of `width × rangeofflankingmove` (0.4 group widths, battleprefs 682) at ±45°, ending 45° inward, `useorderdelay: false`; it aborts on river id 4 on either leg (`CheckTerrainLine(…, {4}, 2f)`, 117011-117036) or when the first enemy unit along leg 2 (within the leg plus `1.5 × firerange`) stands at least `heightdifferencetoenemyunitanchored` (5 m, battleprefs 692) above the turn point (117038-117050).
5. **`flanksthreated` double count.** `UpdateGroupStrength` (120135; it returns early when `bui != null`, the selected unit is another unit and `overridecalc` is false, 120140-120143) adds 1/2/3/4 weight points per counted subordinate from the **group's own** `outflanked` (bare field, 120566-120580), inside the loop over `allattachedunits` (120148, filter 120284). The intended per-subordinate weight is clear from the weight table and from the only reader, `GetGroupStrength(includeflanksthreated)` (6047-6050: strength ÷ `1 + flanksthreated / flankinginfluenceonaidecisions`, battleprefs 694 = 10 weight points, "the higher, the less own actions … are influenced by enemy flanking"), used by `CheckFlankMoves` (6269-6270), the flank-unit cancel (5962) and `CalculateFlankData` (6518, 6547), whose `flankstrength` drives `AssignReserves`' flank extension (7059-7087).
6. **Plays.** `PlayCorpsAssignment.RoleForRectKind` (`PlayCorpsAssignment.cs:195-211`) maps FlankMarch, LeftWing, RightWing and DeepRaid to `Main`; `RectAnchorX/Z` and `StartTimeOffsetSeconds` are carried to the allocator and dropped (`DirectChildAllocator.cs:128-131`). New finding: `PlayAnchor.CollectOwn` (`PlayAnchor.cs:69-89`) offers every active own unit at or above the command level as a corps (the army HQ and grandchild groups included), so a rect can go to a unit that is not a direct child, which `zoneByChildId` then silently never matches.
7. **Leave vanilla:** squares (no AI path forms one; unitprefs 1491-1510), the flank and rear morale values (`UpdateMorale` 128273-128351), and the AI cheat `debug_aicheat_nomorallosswhenregrouping` (64461, 128321-128324).
8. **W&L.** `CheckFlankMoves`, `FlankMove`, `AssignReserves` and `CalculateFlankData` have no `PerformAIActionDLCWL` test and run for the player's alliance under `ai_vs_ai` (5859); `SetWaypoint` has no `dlcw` filter, so a vanilla flank move of a non-detached ancestor may drag the player's units (inference, not observed).

### Decisions

1. **Flank-pressure facing (`FlankFacing`, pure).** `MostPressuredBearing(BattleSnapshot s, int i, float currentFacing, out float bearing, out float windowShare)`: over the unit's slice bins (bin `j` centred on `j × w + w / 2`, `w = SliceCount` degrees; with the shipped 20, bins 0-17), the 3-bin window with the most pressure (Visible full, Remembered × 0.5 × confidence, BC2a), bearing = the window's pressure-weighted circular mean. Hysteresis: keep `currentFacing` while the window centred on it holds at least `KeepShare` = 0.8 of the best window (a turn needs 1.25 × the pressure in front). No pressure within `aidefensivemaxrange` (250 m) → the closest visible enemy, as today. Readers: the posture producer's threat facing for holding tasks (`PostureTargets.TryThreat` keeps the closest visible enemy for fallback targets; facing reads `FlankFacing`), the facing pulse's brigade and division scopes, and `RefuseFor`'s side. The regiment pulse stays on the closest visible enemy (vanilla's per-regiment rule, finding 1). Vanilla's dead `UpdateDefensivePositions` is not revived (it reads omniscient slices).
2. **Refuse reaches vanilla (bug fix).** `RefuseFor` needs `flankRisk` and either close engagement or perceived flank pressure on one side (`FlankPressure` left or right > 0); the side is the flank with more pressure (ties: the bearing test of today); the 45-150° geometry test runs on that side's pressure bearing. `CommandFormationWritePolicy.Decide` with `NeedsRefuse`: out of combat (`CombatStatusSubordinates <= 0`, vanilla's own gate, finding 2) and not moving → `WheelGroup` with the refuse whatever the enemies in reach (a facing-only wheel keeps today's `enemies-in-range` refusal). The wheel is the refused flank's main path, and it holds: vanilla's micro turn leaves a regiment alone while it is not under fire and no enemy is within 40 m (finding 1). In combat → new `FormationWriteKind.RefuseInPlace`, limited to what vanilla's micro turn will leave in place. The executor rotates in place (no position change) a regiment of the group (`PostureSense.CollectRotatableRegiments`, per-unit `TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite`) whose `SkewedPosition` setback is at least `RefuseWingShare` = 0.1 of the refuse depth, to the skewed-line rotation (78155-78167) evaluated at its layout offset (`UnitRecord.SlotOffsetX` with `HasLayoutSlot`, else its lateral projection on the group axis), and only when finding 1's rule would not turn it back, judged on the snapshot: either it is not under fire (`UnitRecord.ReceivedFire`, new: `receivedfire.Count > 0`) and no Visible enemy stands within `distancetoturntowardsenemy` (40 m), or its closest Visible enemy within its turn reach (40 m, or its fire range when under fire) lies within `angletriggerforturningtowardsenemy` (25°, plus `additionalanglewhenincovermicro` when its cover value exceeds `maxcovervalueforgrouprotation`; all three read from `GamePrefs`) of the skewed rotation. A wing regiment whose closest enemy is in front keeps vanilla's facing to it, and one whose closest enemy is already on the flank is turned there by vanilla anyway, so the in-combat refuse adds only the regiments in between. At most `MaxRefuseRotationsPerTick` = 4 regiments per group per tick. Rejected: (a) a vanilla exemption flag: the skip list at 4566 (`aiexpandingline`, `reattachmentorder`, `WithinOrderProcess()`, a charge target, movement modes 2/4/5/6) has no state that means "hold this facing", and setting one would change its other readers; (b) re-asserting the turn on the facing cadence: vanilla turns the regiment back on its next pass over the group (4987) and Whiskey would turn it again after its 0.05 doctrine-h cooldown, an oscillation. Residual: vanilla's turn reads its own range list (`closestenemyunitfar`), so an enemy the snapshot does not see can still turn a refused regiment back; that is vanilla's rule acting and is recorded (smoke 2.26), not fought. `OrderExecutor.NoteChildLayout` records a refuse only for the wheel (BC5a's leaf slot rule is unchanged).
3. **A reserve answers a threatened flank.** `DirectChildAllocator.ReleaseReserves` gains a fourth release: a wing child (sector 0 or the last) with `ContactFlag` and `FlankExposureBucket >= 3` releases the nearest Reserve child to `RefuseLeft` / `RefuseRight` (→ `CommandNodeRole.Defender`, task GuardFlank) with that wing's sector as axis override. It is a role change, not a mover (the posture producer executes it). For a Defender this is a holding task (allowed).
4. **Envelopment execution (`EnvelopmentManeuver`, pure; state in `PlayExecutorRuntime`, BattleScope).** `PlayRect` gains `Envelops` (bool, constructor argument, default false), true on exactly six rects: DoubleEnvelopment's LeftWing and RightWing, MobileFlankMarch's FlankMarch, WideFlankApproach's FlankMarch, CavalryRaid's DeepRaid, and ManeuverAndFix's RightWing (that play's maneuvering wing: the centre fixes, the right wing goes round 300 battle-s later). For each zone assignment whose rect has `Envelops`, a per-child state machine on the doctrine clock:
   - **Hold** until `StartTimeOffsetSeconds` battle-seconds after the later of the play's activation and, when the template has a Center rect, that rect's child first having a visible enemy within 250 m. Task FormUp with the child's current position as target (no move), allowed idle.
   - **March** to the rect anchor (the projection's world anchor; `DeepRaid` first to an outflank point `OutflankMarginMeters` = 300 m beyond the perceived enemy line end on the less-pressured side at the anchor's depth): task AdvanceToAssembly, `PrimaryTarget` per node = the anchor offset on `180 m` lanes along the rect's lateral axis (the line planner's per-node spacing). The move gets BC10's covered-crossing and road routing.
   - **Turn** on arrival (the child's nodes within `ArrivalMeters` = 120 m of their targets): the doctrine order carries `ManeuverHeading` = bearing to the perceived enemy line end on the rect's side (front axis `EnemyLateralMin/Max` at the enemy depth; with no enemy extent, the play's enemy anchor); the posture producer submits a group Facing with it (the executor wheels out of combat or rotates in place).
   - **Attack** after `TurnHoldHours` = 0.05 doctrine-h or on first visible contact within 250 m: task AttackObjective, `PrimaryTarget` = the enemy line end.
   - **Abandon** (the child returns to its allocator role): the march target or the inward leg fails the FlankMove checks (below), the child loses contact strength (`OwnStrengthBucket` 0, the lowest bucket), `MaxManeuverHours` = 1.0 doctrine-h pass, or the plan changes.
   - *FlankMove checks, reused:* the inward leg (anchor → enemy line end) must not cross river id 4 at 2 m steps (`BattleTerrainGrid.SampleLine`, FlankMove's `{4}`, finding 4; fords and bridges are other ids); the march leg must have no river sample on the filter path of BC10 decision 4 unless frozen (the pathfinder would cross only at a ford or bridge); and no perceived enemy (Visible, or Remembered at confidence ≥ 0.5) within the inward leg's corridor (leg length plus 150 m, 100 m wide) may stand at least `GamePrefs.heightdifferencetoenemyunitanchored` (5 m) above the anchor (heights from `BattlefieldSetup.GetTerrainHeight`, sampled by the runtime half into the plan input; terrain is public, the enemy position is perceived). A failing single-flank template (FlankMarch, a lone wing) tries the mirrored side once; a failing wing of a double envelopment is abandoned.
   - Plumbing: `PlayAnchor.CollectOwn` takes the side's direct-child id set (finding 6 fix); `DirectChildIntent` gains `Maneuver` (kind, anchor, offset); `CommandDoctrineAssignment.Build` takes the side's maneuver table and overrides task, `PrimaryTarget` and `ManeuverHeading` for every node whose `CommandNodeIntent.SourceNodeId` is a maneuvering child; `CommandDoctrineOrder.ManeuverHeading` (-1 none) is additive. Roles stay `Main` (the order override carries the maneuver).
   - Defender: offensive playbooks are never selected for a Defender (`TacticalPlaybookCatalog.StanceAllowsPlaybook`), `EnvelopmentManeuver.Plan` returns no maneuver for a Defender, and every overridden task still passes `PostureStanceGuard`.
   - *Every other wing rect* (the wings of FrontalShockAssault, ContinuousAttrition, HesitatedCommit, MeetingEngagement, GenericAggressive, GenericMethodical and GenericDesperate) stays a plain role assignment, as today. Its `StartTimeOffsetSeconds` becomes a start delay only: with an offset above 0 (HesitatedCommit 600 battle-s, GenericMethodical 300 battle-s) the child holds (task FormUp at its own position) for that long after the play's activation and then returns to its allocator role (phase `Released`); it never marches to the rect, turns or attacks under the maneuver. A wing with offset 0 gets no maneuver state.
5. **Catalog comments** (`PlayTemplateCatalog.cs:74-76`, `:87-88`) are rewritten to say what executes: "the FlankMarch rect's child holds until 900 / 600 battle-seconds after the Center child's contact, marches to the rect, faces the enemy line end and attacks it (`EnvelopmentManeuver`)"; the WideFlankApproach note says the mirrored side is tried when the right is anchored or cut by a river. The HesitatedCommit and GenericMethodical notes say their wing offsets are start delays (hold, then the allocator's role, no maneuver), and the ManeuverAndFix note names its right wing as the maneuvering wing.
6. **`flanksthreated` fix (the flank tally patch (#113) `FlankThreatTallyFixPatch`, the `flanksthreated` bug (`BUG-TAC-023`)).** Decision: fix, default on (integrator decision 2), because the consumer intent is clear (finding 5). Postfix on `Regiment.UpdateGroupStrength`, battle only, groups only (`unittyp > 13`), and only when vanilla did not take its early return: the Postfix re-evaluates vanilla's own test and runs when `bui == null`, `bui.selectedunit` is this group, or `overridecalc` is true (120140). `flanksthreated` = the sum, over the subordinates vanilla's loop counted (its filter at 120284 with the call's `ignoreactivestate` / `ignoreactivestateonlyforthisunit` arguments; a source-pin test keeps the predicate in step with the decompile text), of `weight(sub.outflanked)` in weight points (outflanked code 1/2 → 1, 3 → 2, 4/5/6 → 3, 7 → 4). A result correction of a vanilla field (no Prefix, no Transpiler). **Effect.** `GetGroupStrength(includeflanksthreated: true)` divides by `1 + flanksthreated / 10` (6047-6050), and today the tally is usually 0 (subordinates × the HQ's own code, and the HQ stands behind its line). With the fix an inner line group whose regiments are outflanked reads weaker, so `CheckFlankMoves`' below-3 test on the inner neighbour (6269-6271) passes more often and **vanilla launches more `FlankMove`s** (`lastaiorder` 4/5, set at 117107); the flank-unit cancel (5962) and `CalculateFlankData` / `AssignReserves` (6518, 6547, 7059-7087) respond to real outflanking too. This includes **Defender sides**: `CheckFlankMoves` returns early only for `macroai` < 0 or 3 (6253), and the defend trigger is 2.2 (battleprefs 676). These are vanilla's own counter-flank moves; the AGENTS Defender rule (a Defender never gets attack doctrine) governs Whiskey-originated doctrine, and every Whiskey BC11 path keeps it. Vanilla's own AI stays omniscient here (it reads its own range lists); Whiskey's readers use BC10's perceived code. **Measurement.** The snapshot gains `UnitRecord.LastAiOrder` (`lastaiorder`, 110990, groups), and `BattleCoreTick` emits one `TacticalVanillaFlankMove` Observation row (side, group, `direction` = lastaiorder − 4, the side's `macroai`, stance, `tallyFix` = the flag's value) when an AI-side group's value changes from 0 to 4 or 5; smoke item 2.33 compares the rate with the fix on and off. Flag `Enable Vanilla Flank Threat Tally Fix` (default true).
7. **Rollback.** `Tactical` / `Enable Flank Response And Envelopment` (default true): decisions 1, 3, 4. Bug fixes outside it: 2 (refuse), 4's `CollectOwn` fix, 5 (comments). the flank tally patch (#113) behind its own flag. The `TacticalVanillaFlankMove` row is telemetry under `Enable Tactical Observer`.

### Interfaces — Produces

```csharp
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Battle {
  public struct MicroTurnRule { public float DistanceMeters, AngleDegrees, CoverExtraDegrees, MaxCoverValue, FireRangeFactor; }   // 40 m, 25°, GamePrefs cover pair, 1.5 (4560-4608)
  public static class FlankFacing {
    public const float KeepShare = 0.8f; public const int WindowBins = 3;
    public static bool MostPressuredBearing(BattleSnapshot s, int ownIndex, float currentFacing, out float bearing, out float windowShare);
    public static RefuseFlank PressuredSide(BattleSnapshot s, int ownIndex);                 // left/right FlankPressure, None when both 0
    public const float RefuseWingShare = 0.1f; public const int MaxRefuseRotationsPerTick = 4;
    public static int RefuseRotations(BattleSnapshot s, int groupIndex, RefuseFlank side, float groupWidth, float depthFactor, float skew,
        in MicroTurnRule turn, int[] queue, int[] regiments, float[] headings, out int skipped);   // SkewedPosition 78109-78168 rotations; skips what vanilla's micro turn would undo
  }
}
// WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Operations.FormationWriteKind.RefuseInPlace (appended member)
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Orchestrator.Plays {
  public enum ManeuverPhase { None, Hold, March, Turn, Attack, Released, Abandoned }   // Released: a start-delay wing back on its allocator role
  public struct ManeuverInput {
    public PlayRectKind Kind; public bool Envelops; public float AnchorX, AnchorZ, StartOffsetSeconds; public bool TemplateHasCenter;
    public double PlayActivatedHours, CenterContactHours;            // CenterContactHours < 0 until the Center child sees an enemy within ContactMeters
    public float ChildX, ChildZ; public int ChildStrengthBucket; public bool ChildArrived, ChildInContact;
    public bool HasEnemyExtent; public float EnemyEndX, EnemyEndZ, EnemyAnchorX, EnemyAnchorZ, OutflankX, OutflankZ;   // the rect side's perceived line end; DeepRaid's outflank point
    public bool InwardLegHitsRiver, MarchLegHitsRiver;               // BattleTerrainGrid samples (2 m inward, the filter path's 10 m samples for the march)
    public float AnchorHeight; public int CorridorEnemyCount;         // perceived enemies (Visible, or Remembered at >= 0.5) on the inward corridor
    public float MaxCorridorEnemyHeight; public float HeightDifferenceAnchored;   // GamePrefs.heightdifferencetoenemyunitanchored (5 m)
    public BattleStance Stance; public double NowHours; }
  public struct ManeuverState { public ManeuverPhase Phase; public double PhaseSinceHours; public bool Mirrored; public string AbandonReason; }
  public struct ManeuverOrder { public ManeuverPhase Phase; public CommandTaskType Task; public float TargetX, TargetZ, Heading; public string Reason; }
  public static class EnvelopmentManeuver {
    public const float ArrivalMeters = 120f, OutflankMarginMeters = 300f, LaneMeters = 180f, TurnHoldHours = 0.05f, MaxManeuverHours = 1.0f, ContactMeters = 250f;
    public static ManeuverOrder Step(ref ManeuverState state, in ManeuverInput input);
    public static bool InwardLegClear(ReadOnlySpan<int> riverSamples, int count);
    public static bool AnchoredHighGround(float anchorHeight, ReadOnlySpan<float> enemyHeights, int count, float heightDifference);
  }
}
// CommandDoctrineOrder.ManeuverHeading (-1 none); DirectChildIntent.Maneuver; PlayAnchor.CollectOwn(…, ISet<string> directChildIds, …);
// PlayRect.Envelops (constructor argument, default false); UnitRecord.ReceivedFire, UnitRecord.LastAiOrder (BattleCoreContracts.cs, additive).

- refuse, the combined red-first test: `RefuseReachesVanillaOutOfCombat` (fixture through `RefuseFor` → `PostureIntentPlan.Submit` → `CommandFormationWritePolicy.Decide` → `OrderExecutor.Plan`: a visible enemy at 200 m on the left flank, `combatstatussubordinates` 0 → `WheelGroup` with `refuseflank` 0; red today: the plan carries -1) and `RefuseReachesVanillaInCombat` (in combat → `RefuseInPlace` with the left-wing regiments at skewed rotations, right wing untouched); `RefuseRotationsMatchSkewedPosition` (fixture at t = 0, 0.5, 1 with skew 4 and depth 0.5), `FacingOnlyWheelStillRefusedWithEnemiesInRange`, `RefuseInPlaceNeverPlayerOwnedOrDetached`, `RefuseInPlaceSkipsRegimentWhoseClosestEnemyIsInFront`, `RefuseInPlaceKeepsRegimentWithClosestEnemyOnFlank`, `RefuseInPlaceSkipsUnderFireRegimentWithoutFlankEnemy`;
