**As built (BC11):**

Branch `feat/battle-next`, base `ce3f14f`. Commits: `48e8bdd` (red-first refuse tests), `38ebd8c` (refuse reaches vanilla, flank tally patch, `FlankFacing`), `e54e590` (pressure facing, reserve release, envelopment state machine and plumbing), `60960e1` (`FlankResponseTests`), `9092bf1` (smoke lines, abandon reasons), `7e6ba17` (envelopment arrival and lane fix after review), `9fa695e` (living docs). Gates at the last code commit: `./build.sh` 0 Warning(s) 0 Error(s); harness exit 0, 2559 PASS (base 2498); `config-audit` 0 finding(s) over 150 entries; `orphan-sweep` 0, `--include-test-only` 29; `tools/test_smoke_check.py` OK. Evidence: `evidence/bc11/` in the session scratchpad (`red1-run`, `red2-run`, `green1`-`green9-run`, `mutation.txt`: 31 plants, 31 killed). The patch took #113 and the bug `BUG-TAC-023` (main `ce3f14f` had not moved; next free is #114).

What changed against the plan (each is a finding or a deliberate deviation, with anchors):

- **Unit width on the record.** `UnitRecord.UnitWidth` (additive; `Regiment.GetWidth()` 139382) feeds the skewed-line chord (`SkewedPosition` 78109-78168 lays regiments by their own widths), so `FlankFacing.SkewedRotation` and `RefuseRotations` reproduce vanilla's layout rotation rather than guessing a spacing.
- **`TelemetryCategory.Observation` does not exist.** The `TacticalVanillaFlankMove` row is a `State` row (under `Enable Tactical Observer`).
- **Additive types beyond the interface block:** `FlankFacing.SideBearing`, `ManeuverSpec`, `ChildManeuver`, `ManeuverNodeTable`/`ManeuverNodeEntry`, `CommandDoctrineOrder.ManeuverHold`, `ManeuverState.ActiveSinceHours`/`OutflankReached`, `ManeuverInput.CanMirror`/`ArrivalSlackMeters`, `ReserveReleaseContext.FlankRelease`, `OrderExecutor.DecideFormation`/`DecideFacing`, `VanillaFlankMoveWatch`, `EnvelopmentManeuver.Arrived`/`CenterChildId`.
- **A due refuse overrides a NoWrite verdict.** `PostureSense.RefusePromotesNoWrite` plus a `LastIssuedRefuse` guard: without it `already-correct`/`already-valid` suppressed the refuse of a group that already stood on its objective. The facing intent keeps the refuse in force (not only a due one) because a facing wheel re-lays every child and would otherwise undo the skew.
- **Wheel out of combat whatever the enemies in range** (decision 2): `CommandPostureExecutor.Decide` returns `WheelGroup "refuse-wheel"` for a needed refuse; facing-only still waits for no enemies in range. Two `PostureStanceFixTests` assertions encoded the old policy (refuse with enemies in range → no write) and were flipped; the `BattleReconTests` source pin on `StoreDoctrineForCommandStates` was loosened to its call prefix because the call gained the maneuver argument.
- **A11, limited in-combat refuse.** `RefuseInPlace` rotates only settled non-artillery regiments, at most 4 per tick, skipping regiments within the 15° tolerance and those vanilla's micro turn (`StopMicroUnitsCloseToEnemyAndRotateTowards` 4539-4613, rule 4551-4608) would turn straight back (`FlankFacing.VanillaTurnsBack`); the group keeps `groupformation` (BC14 rule). The refused wing carries 10% of the depth (`RefuseWingShare`).
- **Envelopment arrival and lanes (post-review fix `7e6ba17`).** The first build lane-spread every node including the child's own, so a real corps' headquarters was sent 180 m off the anchor and the 120 m arrival test could never pass. Now the child node takes the anchor, its direct sub-nodes (`Depth == child depth + 1`) the 180 m lanes, deeper nodes the anchor; arrival is judged on the child's headquarters within 120 m plus `GroupDepth + groupunitdistancetoback` (`SetGroupFormation` 92044-92058 stands the group HQ that far behind the waypoint). The wings wait for the Center child's contact only on a Center child that acts (superseded by verify round 1 below): DoubleEnvelopment's wings (priority 10) outrank its Center (8), so with two corps nobody holds the Center and the wings would have held for good. A hold's drifting target is excluded from the ledger hash (`ManeuverHash`).
- **River leg check.** The inward leg is a straight 2 m line sample on the BC10 grid (FlankMove's CheckTerrainLine); the march leg is judged on the planned route (verify round 1, F3 below); a frozen map skips the march-leg check.
- **Abandon reasons** use the smoke item's names (`river-on-leg`, `anchored-high-ground`, `lost-strength`, `timeout`; `mirror-flank` for the one mirrored try).
- **Signature changes:** `PlayExecutorRuntime.ComputeZoneAssignments` and `PlayAnchor.CollectOwn` gained the direct child id set (decision 4: only direct children are offered a rect); `CommandDoctrineAssignment.Build` takes an optional `ManeuverNodeTable`.
- **`CurrentManeuvers` accessor dropped** (orphan-sweep): `ArmyOrchestrator` exposes `ManeuverHash` only.
- **Test order.** Only the two combined refuse tests were red-first (`48e8bdd`); the remaining tests were written after the code and the mutation plants supply the kill evidence (hysteresis share, wing share, micro-turn skip, `Envelops` gate, river leg, 5 m height rule, Defender gate, tolerance gate, lane and arrival rules, wait-for-center, hash). No CI definition exists (`.github/workflows` is empty): the gate list is the brief's six commands.
- **Tally correction is vanilla-visible.** The corrected `flanksthreated` makes vanilla launch more of its own `FlankMove`s, on a Defender side too (`CheckFlankMoves` 6253); measured by `UnitRecord.LastAiOrder` and the `TacticalVanillaFlankMove` rows.

**Verify round 1 (BC11, REVISE on `538e197`):**

- **F1, structural.** (a) A BC10 deployed reserve is kept out of the zone assignment (`TacticalBattleCoordinatorRuntime` offers `PlayAnchor.CollectOwn` an empty id for it), consistent with `DirectChildAllocator` keeping it off its zone role, so the Center rect goes to a child that will act. (b) The wings wait only on a Center child whose effective role came from its zone: `DirectChildIntent.ZoneRectKind` (additive) is the rect the role came from, `EnvelopmentManeuver.CenterChildId` reads it, never the raw assignment. (c) The Hold has a ceiling: `MaxManeuverHours` from the play's activation, after which the wings proceed on their own checks; `ManeuverState.ProceedReason` (`center-contact`, `offset`, `hold-ceiling`, `mirror-flank`) rides the `TacticalEnvelopment` row as `proceed`.
- **F2.** `RefuseTurnBackMemory` (pure, fixed 64 slots, no allocation): the rotation the executor ordered per regiment; a regiment later found settled away from it (vanilla's micro turn sees enemies the snapshot does not, or the player turned it) is skipped for 0.1 doctrine-h or until the wanted rotation moves by the facing tolerance. Wired through `FlankFacing.RefuseRotations(..., memory, nowHours)` and `OrderExecutorRuntime` (cleared with the battle).
- **F3.** The march leg is checked on the planned route: `PlayExecutorRuntime.MarchLegHitsRiver` plans a NavMesh path from the child to the anchor with the posture probe's filter (`PostureTargets.CreateNavFilter`, shared builder) and `EnvelopmentManeuver.RouteHitsRiver` samples each segment every 10 m on the BC10 grid, so a river crossed by a bridge or ford (ids 6, 5) is not a river. It re-runs at the leg start, when the anchor moves more than 50 m (the mirrored side) and every 0.05 doctrine-h, at most one route plan per side and step (`RoutePlansLeft`); with no budget the previous result stands (round 2 below replaces the straight-line fallback and the mirror tick).
- **F4.** The `TacticalRefuseInPlace` row repeat test is numeric (`RefuseRowThrottle`, a dictionary on a long key) and runs before any string is built: allocation-free on the throttled path (`TurnBackMemoryIsBoundedAndAllocatesNothing`, `RefuseRowThrottleBuildsNoStringOnRepeat`).
- **Known interaction, BC14's:** vanilla's `AdjustGroupFormations` re-lay can undo a refuse wheel; BC14 owns that (its decision 2 is the only refuse path and keeps `groupformation`).
- **Tests and proof.** `FlankResponseTests.Review.cs` (10 tests) plus edits to the Maneuver tests (the mirror-tick order, the Hold ceiling, `CenterChildId` with intents); written after the code, the mutation plants (48 in all, 48 killed) supply the kill evidence.

**Verify round 2 (BC11, REVISE on `9d571f3`):**

- **Never launch or abandon a wing on a straight line.** `ManeuverInput.MarchRouteKnown` is false until the child has a route result for its current anchor (`MarchRouteCheck`, `CheckForAnchor`: a result for a leg whose anchor moved 50 m is no result); the Hold then reports `await-route` and neither launches nor abandons (past the Hold ceiling too), and a launched child keeps marching until the new result exists. The straight-line sample is deleted, not kept as telemetry. No complete NavMesh path (or a failed query) is a result: `NoPath`, abandoned as `no-route`. A frozen map or a missing grid has nothing to wait for.
- **Fair route plans, bound N steps.** One route plan per side and step, picked by `EnvelopmentManeuver.PickRoutePlan` before the children step (`PlayExecutorRuntime.PickRoutePlanChild`): a child with no result for its anchor goes before any re-check, then the stalest result, ties to the lower index. A step serves one child without a result and a served child stays served, so N enveloping children each have their first route within N steps (DoubleEnvelopment's two wings, offset 0, no Center: the second wing holds exactly one step, then marches on its bridge route). Holding children are planned too, so a result usually exists before the offset ends.
- **Mirror deviates from round 1:** a failing flank mirrors once by returning to Hold on the mirrored anchor (`MirrorHold`); the March to the mirrored anchor is ordered at the first step that leg has a route result. `ManeuverInput.MirrorAnchorX/Z` is removed.
- **`RefuseTurnBackMemory` overflow:** `NoteRotated(id, rotation, nowHours)` evicts an expired back-off first, then a plain remembered rotation, and only with all 64 backed off the one closest to expiry; an active back-off is never overwritten by newer rotations.
- **Tests and proof.** Four new tests (`NoRouteResultKeepsTheChildInItsPhase`, `SecondWingWaitsOneStepForItsRouteAndIsNotAbandoned`, `RoutePlanOrderIsFairAndBounded`, `TurnBackMemoryKeepsAnActiveBackOffOnOverflow`) plus edits to the mirror and route-wiring tests; the mutation plants (57 in all) supply the kill evidence.

**Landing items after verify round 3 (PASS on `a40f981`):**

- **NavMesh targets at terrain height.** `NavMesh.CalculatePath` snaps each end to the mesh within a small extent, so a target built with y = 0 misses it on a map with relief and reads as "no path". Vanilla's anchor: `AddPath` (130259) paths from `GetPosition() + (0, 1.5, 0)` to waypoint positions, which carry the terrain height (`GetTerrainHeight` 27002). Fixed at all three Whiskey queries: the envelopment route (`PlayExecutorRuntime`, `HeightAt`), BC10's road-route plan (`TryPlan`) and the posture probe (`Sample`), through `PostureTargets.AtTerrain` (no allocation). `NavMeshQueriesSitAtTerrainHeight` pins the set of three call sites (a fourth must be added to the test with its target at terrain height) and fails any `new Vector3(..., 0, ...)` argument. Smoke items 24 and 28 now name the route-failure lines to watch (`reason=no-route`, `road-route-failed`, `nav-plan-failed`, `march-route-exception`).
- **Plan order test.** `PlansBefore`'s `a.Valid != b.Valid` rule is not redundant with "stalest first": a result checked at doctrine hour 0 is as old as an empty one by the clock, and only the rule puts the empty one first whatever the index order (`RoutePlanOrderIsFairAndBounded`, plant "no-result-first rule removed").
- **Ordinals.** `main` had not moved since `ce3f14f`; the flank tally patch is #113 (next free #114) and the bug `BUG-TAC-023`.

## BC12 — terrain picture for planning

**Goal:** the AI picks ground. A fog-free picture of the public map (crests, wall/fence/sunken-road/embankment lines, river lines, fords, bridges, road junctions, towns), built once per battle on BC10's grid, feeds the objective types, terrain strength and defensive lines the planners already have branches for (dead today), the enemy-intent inference and the crest-based playbooks.

**Owned files:**
- Create (pure): `Tactical/Battle/TerrainPicture.cs` (layers and queries), `Tactical/Battle/TerrainPictureBuilder.cs` (stepwise build from grid, heights, cover segments, building points), `Tactical/Operations/TacticalTerrainLinePlanner.cs`.
- Create (runtime): `Tactical/Battle/TerrainPictureRuntime.cs` (heights, cover colliders, buildings, stepping; BattleScope).
- Modify: `Tactical/Orchestrator/TacticalVisionRuntimeAdapter.cs` (objective classification), `Tactical/Operations/TacticalObjectiveContracts.cs` / `TacticalObjectiveSourceModel.cs` (terrain fields on the observation), `Tactical/Orchestrator/TacticalOperationsLedgerRuntime.cs` (`:198-199`), `Tactical/Operations/TacticalOperationDirector.cs` (`ObjectiveSelectionScore`), `TacticalDefensiveLineAnchorPlanner.cs`, `TacticalBattleLinePlanner.cs`, `CommandDoctrineAssignment.cs` (anchored-side slots), `Tactical/Orchestrator/ArmyIntentInference.cs`, `TacticalBattleCoordinator.cs` (`:162`) and `ArmyTickCycle.cs` (`:126`) (the two `Build` callers that pass `ownEvidence`), `EnemyVisibleState.cs`, `ArmyEvidenceBuilder.cs` / `.Pure.cs`, `ArmyOrchestrator.cs` (`ArmyEvidence`), `TacticalPlaybook.cs`, `TacticalPlaybookCatalog.cs`, `Playbooks/ReverseSlopeDefensePlaybook.cs`, `Plays/PlayProjection.cs`, `Tactical/TacticalPlaybookLedger.cs`, `Patches/BattleCommanderIntentObserverPatch.cs` (its playbook input only), `WhiskeyRealism.csproj` (TerrainModule reference), `README.md` (refs loop), `Plugin.cs` (one bind), the test csproj.
- Test file: `TerrainPictureTests.cs`.

### Vanilla findings (build 14703111)

1. **Sources.** The 1 m id grid (BC10 finding 6); heights `Terrain.SampleHeight` (used by `GetTerrainHeight` 27002-27031, which also probes NavMesh for bridges and rivers and so is too slow to call per cell); crests: `CheckTerrainPeak` (27068-27108) takes the highest sample along a line and rejects it unless the line's relief is at least `maxterrainshapeheightdifference` (3 m, unitprefs 918) and the peak stands at least `maxsteepnessofterrainforshape` (3 m, unitprefs 920) above both ends; cover: `GetCoverCollider` (138392-138430) raycasts tagged colliders, `TrenchFinished`, `StoneWall`, `LogWallFinished`, `Fence`, `Railroad`, `UnfinishedRailroad`, `SmallRiver`, `SunkenRoad`, and `Road` only with `roadsascover`, mapped to cover ids by `GetCoverIDFromName` (138482-138502: Fence 1, StoneWall 2, Forest 3, Railroad 4, SmallRiver 5, Road 6, trenches 7-10, LogWall 11, SunkenRoad 15, UnfinishedRailroad 16) and values by `coverobjectvalues`; crossings `rivercrossingpositions` (24723, 26628-26640); occupiable buildings `Building` (94256). `Objectives.objectivetype` (105210+) is a hold/timer rule (105627-105798), not a terrain kind.
2. **Vanilla's own terrain use.** `RepositionWaypoints` (138531-138830) snaps the end of every AI move to cover within 15 m in front / 10 m behind or, with `usehills` (true for AI units, 112854-112861), to a crest within 80 m in front / 40 m behind; Whiskey moves already get it. `CheckIfFlanksAreAnchored` (6599-6640): a flank is anchored by river id 4 within `distancetocheckforanchoredflanksriver` (80 m, battleprefs 644) sideways or a crest within `distancetocheckforanchoredflankhills` (50 m, battleprefs 646). Nothing scores positions by terrain at the planning level; defensive lines come from authored fortifications only (`Fortification.GetGroupPosition` 24528-24660).
3. **Whiskey today.** The terrain branches of `TacticalBattleLinePlanner` (`IsTerrainAnchor` / `IsChokeObjective`, `:454-464`, used at `:426-449`), `TacticalDefensiveLineAnchorPlanner` (`:156-170`), `CommandDoctrineAssignment.ShouldAdvanceCommittedMapObjective` (`:276-281`), `TacticalDecisionDoctrine.IsKnownMapObjective` (`:174-181`) and `TacticalObjectiveSourceModel` (`:27-32`) never see a terrain type: the vision adapter emits only EnemyLine, StagingArea, UnknownVanillaObjective and VictoryPoint (`TacticalVisionRuntimeAdapter.cs:166, 206, 287, 381, 537`), `TacticalObjectiveSourceModel.Normalize` would demote a specific type without `TypeAnchorVerified` anyway, and the ledger passes `terrainStrength: 0f, approachDifficulty: 0f` (`TacticalOperationsLedgerRuntime.cs:198-199`). `ChokePoint` alone is produced (a corridor anchor's line objective, `TacticalDefensiveLineAnchorPlanner.AsLineObjective`). `TacticalOperationDirector.ObjectiveSelectionScore` (`:449-454`) subtracts `TerrainStrength` and `ApproachDifficulty` for every side. `ArmyIntentInference.Build(ownEvidence, enemy)` never reads `ownEvidence` (`:20-115`), so the `TerrainKind.Open` at `:150` is a dead input, not an assumption. `ReverseSlopeDefense` is fixed rects (`PlayTemplateCatalog.cs:174-185`); `TacticalPlaybookKind.HighGroundDefense` is the line-hold label of the telemetry-only `TacticalPlaybookLedger` (`:262`, `:329`; sole caller `BattleCommanderIntentObserverPatch.cs:79`).

### Decisions

1. **One picture per battle (`TerrainPicture`).** Lattice `PictureCellMeters` = 20 m over the map. Layers (pooled arrays, built once, immutable after `Ready`):
   - `Id` (dominant id of the 20 × 20 m cell from BC10's grid, sampled at 5 m: 16 reads per cell; water and crossing ids win over land),
   - `Height` (`Terrain.activeTerrain.terrainData.GetHeights`, one sampled heightmap strip per unit of work, through the `UnityEngine.TerrainModule` reference of integrator decision 1, `<Private>false</Private>`, with its `refs/` symlink in README's loop). There is no fallback height source: when `Terrain.activeTerrain` or its `TerrainData` is missing, the runtime logs `tactical-battle-core:terrain-heights-missing` once and the picture never becomes `Ready` (every consumer keeps today's no-terrain behaviour),
   - `CrestProminence` / `CrestAxis` (per cell over four axes, 0/45/90/135°: the cell's height minus the higher of the cells `k = 2` (40 m) and `k = 4` (80 m) away on both sides, kept when ≥ 3 m and the 160 m window's relief ≥ 3 m, the two `CheckTerrainPeak` tests; the crest runs perpendicular to the axis with the largest prominence),
   - `Cover` (cover id per cell, rasterised from the tagged colliders found once with `GameObject.FindGameObjectsWithTag` for StoneWall, Fence, SunkenRoad, Railroad, UnfinishedRailroad and SmallRiver; the colliders of each tagged object are its `GetComponentsInChildren<Collider>()` (vanilla accepts the tag on the hit collider or on its parent, 138471, 138479); each collider's segment = the pair of opposite horizontal AABB corners closer to its surface by `Collider.ClosestPoint`; trenches and log walls are built during the battle and are left out),
   - `Water` (river 4, creek 3), crossings (BC10's list with ford/bridge ids), `RoadJunction` (a road cell whose 60 m ring is crossed by at least 3 separate road runs), `Town` (at least 3 `Building` components within 150 m).
   Fog: map data only; no unit is read.
2. **Build cost and time slicing.** Every part of the build, the collider pass included, runs inside the measured scope `tactical.terrain-picture.build` (5.0 ms threshold), one step per frame from `BattleCoreTick.EnsureSnapshot` (#60's deployment build and every per-frame build) until `Ready`. `Step(…, long deadlineTicks, Func<long> clock)` takes a real-time CPU deadline and an injected clock (`Stopwatch.GetTimestamp` at runtime, a scripted counter in tests, so `BuilderHonoursDeadlineAcrossCoverAndCells` is deterministic; `BuildStepBudgetMs` = 4 ms after the step starts; real time is allowed for CPU budgets, AGENTS § Clocks) and stops after the first unit of work that ends past it. Stages, each resumable: (0) cover collection: one tag's `FindGameObjectsWithTag` per unit of work (six tags), then `CoverChunkObjects` = 32 tagged objects per unit (their colliders and two `Collider.ClosestPoint` calls per collider), fed through `ICoverSource` so the Unity calls run inside the step; (1) ids, one lattice row per unit; (2) heights, one heightmap strip per unit; (3) crests, one lattice row per unit; (4) cover rasterising, one segment per unit; (5) buildings (`FindObjectsOfType<Building>` once, then `BuildingChunk` = 64 per unit, through `IBuildingSource`), junctions and towns. Size estimate for a 4,096 m map (estimates, not measured): a 205 × 205 cell lattice = 42,025 cells; ids 672,400 array reads (≈ 3 ms in Mono at an estimated 4-5 ns per bounds-checked read); heights 205 strips of 2,049 floats (≈ 2 ms, ≈ 1.6 MB transient); crests 42,025 cells × 4 axes × 4 reads (≈ 2 ms); colliders up to a few thousand (≈ 2-5 ms); total ≈ 10-15 ms, about 3-5 steps of 4 ms. Every consumer treats "not ready" as "no terrain" (today's behaviour). The `[BattleTerrain] picture ready` line reports the summed build time and the step count, and smoke item 2.30 checks every step's `maxMs`.
3. **Objective classification (vision adapter).** For every vanilla objective record (`objectivechain`, `currentsetobjective`, scene `Objectives`): within `ClassifyRadiusMeters` = 60 m, Bridge (a bridge crossing) > Ford (a ford crossing) > Town > RoadJunction > Ridge (a crest cell with prominence ≥ 3 m) > unchanged, with `TypeAnchorVerified = true` (the picture verified it), so `TacticalObjectiveSourceModel.Normalize` keeps it. `ChokePoint` keeps its one producer (corridor anchors). The observation gains `TerrainStrength` and `ApproachDifficulty`, which the ledger passes at `:198-199`.
4. **Terrain strength and approach difficulty (Whiskey doctrine, pinned by tests).** `TerrainStrength = 1 − (1 − c)(1 − h)(1 − w)(1 − b)` for a point and the threat axis: `c` = the best cover value within 60 m (vanilla's loss reduction factor: wall 0.35, sunken road 0.30, embankment 0.20, fence 0.10, creek 0.08; `coverobjectvalues`), `h` = `min(1, prominence / 15 m) × 0.5` (height raises fire range by `Δh / range`, 118336-118340, and cuts the enemy's sight), `w` = 0.4 when water (river or creek) covers at least 60 % of a 100-300 m strip in front across the line ("in front" = the ground the own side has to cross to reach the point: the axis runs from the objective toward the own centroid, one axis per objective, so a river between us and an enemy-held ridge counts and a river behind the ridge does not), `b` = 0.7 for a Town (building cover 0.70). The planners' threshold `TerrainStrength >= 0.55` then means e.g. a wall on a 10 m crest (0.57: c 0.35, h 0.33), a 10 m crest behind a river (0.60: h 0.33, w 0.4) or a town (0.70); a wall alone (0.35), a wall on a 3 m crest (0.42: h 0.10) or a crest alone (at most 0.5) is not an anchor. `ApproachDifficulty` = `clamp01((mean speed cost of the 300 m approach on the own side of the objective, ending at it, − grass cost) / (swamp cost − grass cost))` with `terrainspeccost` (speed-cost units: grass 2, swamp 6).
5. **Director sign by stance.** `ObjectiveSelectionScore(objective, stance)`: an enemy-held objective (enemy strength > 0) subtracts `TerrainStrength` and `ApproachDifficulty` (today's sign); an own or neutral one adds `TerrainStrength` (seize strong ground), and for a Defender also adds `0.5 × ApproachDifficulty`. Tests pin both stances. Both directional terms are read on the own side of every objective (decision 4), which is exact for an enemy-held one (the ground we must cross); for an own or neutral objective they describe the ground our side crosses to it, an approximation the smoke can tune.
6. **Defensive terrain lines (`TacticalTerrainLinePlanner`, pure).** `BestLine(picture, objective, threatAxis, frontage)` scores candidate lines within `LineSearchMeters` = 400 m of the objective, on the own side or at most 100 m in front: crest runs, cover lines (wall, sunken road, fence, embankment) and river lines in front, each within 30° of perpendicular to the threat axis and at least `MinLineMeters` = 200 m long; score (unitless) = line `TerrainStrength` − (distance from the objective, m) / 1,000 m. The best line becomes a terrain anchor in `TacticalDefensiveLineAnchorPlanner` (its "terrain-anchor-preserved" `None` return becomes an anchor): centre = the line's centre (a crest: its forward edge, the "military crest"; under `ReverseSlopeDefense`, `ReverseSlopeSetbackMeters` = 80 m behind the crest), facing along the threat axis, frontage = the line length clamped to 350-760 m, reason `terrain-line:<crest|wall|sunken|fence|embankment|river>`. The line planner's left and right frontage ends are marked anchored by vanilla's rule (river id 4 within 80 m sideways or a crest within 50 m, finding 2), reason suffix `:anchored-left` / `:anchored-right`, and `CommandDoctrineAssignment.ApplyScourgeSlotTask` gives no GuardFlank end slot on an anchored side (that slot keeps the line task).
7. **Intent inference reads terrain.** The dead `ArmyEvidence ownEvidence` parameter of `ArmyIntentInference.Build` / `BuildForFrontage` is deleted. `EnemyVisibleSector` gains `EnemyStrongGroundShare` = the share of the sector's Visible enemy strength that stands (no paths) on cells with `TerrainStrength >= 0.35`. Rule: share ≥ 0.6 and no recent fire from the sector → `Defend` with `EvidenceTag.EnemyOnStrongGround` (before the Probe/Refuse branches). `ArmyEvidence.Terrain` (playbook terrain fit) is computed from the picture over the same front rectangle (`ArmyEvidenceBuilder.BattlefieldTerrain`'s own 7 × 5 terrain and height sampling, `ArmyEvidenceBuilder.cs:284-319`, is deleted; `ClassifyTerrain` stays, fed by picture cells).
8. **Honest crest playbooks.** `ArmyEvidence.CrestLine` (the best crest run across the front, from decision 6's planner, or none). `ReverseSlopeDefense` is selectable only with a crest line (`TacticalPlaybook.RequiresCrest`, checked in `TacticalPlaybookCatalog` with the stance filter); its play projection puts the CenterHold and flank rects `ReverseSlopeSetbackMeters` behind the crest and the ForwardScreen on it (`PlayProjection` override for that template). `TacticalPlaybookLedger.BuildLineHold` labels `HighGroundDefense` only when at least half of the held sectors' own groups stand on crest cells (`TacticalPlaybookInput.OwnOnHighGroundShare`, from the picture in `BattleCommanderIntentObserverPatch`), else the new `TacticalPlaybookKind.LineHold`; smoke tooling has no rule on the label (checked: `tools/` greps).
9. **Dead-code rule.** Every terrain branch named in finding 3 now has a producer (Ridge, Ford, Bridge, Town, RoadJunction from decision 3; `TerrainStrength` from decision 4; ChokePoint as before). If the smoke (item 2.31) shows `Town` or `RoadJunction` never produced on the maps played, they stay (map-dependent, test-covered), not deleted; a branch with no producer at all after this slice is deleted in the same slice.
10. **Rollback.** `Tactical` / `Enable Battle Terrain Picture` (default true): decisions 3-6, the strong-ground rule of 7 and the crest playbooks of 8. Off: the picture still builds (playbook terrain, decision 7, reads it in place of the deleted sampler, so it is not a regression), but objectives keep today's types and zero terrain strength, the defensive anchor keeps its "terrain-anchor-preserved" behaviour, `ReverseSlopeDefense` keeps its crest precondition (so it is not selected without a crest line) and the intent rule gets no strong-ground evidence.

### Interfaces — Produces

```csharp
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Battle {
  public sealed class TerrainPicture {                                     // pure
    public const float CellMeters = 20f; public bool Ready { get; }
    public int Id(int cx, int cz); public float Height(int cx, int cz); public float CrestProminence(int cx, int cz); public int CrestAxis(int cx, int cz);
    public int Cover(int cx, int cz); public float CoverValue(int coverId); public bool Water(int cx, int cz);
    public bool TryNearestCrossing(float x, float z, float radius, out float cxW, out float czW, out int id);
    public bool RoadJunctionNear(float x, float z, float radius); public bool TownNear(float x, float z, float radius);
    public float TerrainStrength(float x, float z, float axisX, float axisZ); public float ApproachDifficulty(float x, float z, float axisX, float axisZ);
  }
  public sealed class TerrainPictureBuilder {                              // pure, stepwise
    public TerrainPictureBuilder(BattleTerrainGrid grid, float sizeX, float sizeZ, in TerrainShapeRules rules);
    public const float BuildStepBudgetMs = 4f; public const int CoverChunkObjects = 32, BuildingChunk = 64;
    public bool Step(IHeightSource heights, ICoverSource cover, IBuildingSource buildings, long deadlineTicks, Func<long> clock);   // clock: Stopwatch.GetTimestamp at runtime, a scripted counter in tests; true when Ready; every stage resumable
    public TerrainPicture Picture { get; }
  }
  public interface IHeightSource { int Rows { get; } int Columns { get; } bool TryReadRow(int row, float[] heights); }   // runtime: TerrainData.GetHeights strips; tests: a fixture
  public interface ICoverSource { bool Done { get; } int NextChunk(CoverSegment[] into); }        // runtime: one tag search or CoverChunkObjects objects per call
  public interface IBuildingSource { bool Done { get; } int NextChunk(float[] x, float[] z); }    // runtime: BuildingChunk Building positions per call
  public struct TerrainShapeRules { public float MinRelief, MinSteepness, PeakNearMeters, PeakFarMeters, AnchorRiverMeters, AnchorCrestMeters; }
  public struct CoverSegment { public float X0, Z0, X1, Z1; public int CoverId; }
}
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Operations {
  public struct TerrainLine { public bool Has; public float X0, Z0, X1, Z1, CenterX, CenterZ, Strength; public TerrainLineKind Kind; }
  public enum TerrainLineKind { None, Crest, Wall, SunkenRoad, Fence, Embankment, River }
  public static class TacticalTerrainLinePlanner {
    public const float LineSearchMeters = 400f, MinLineMeters = 200f, MaxForwardMeters = 100f, ReverseSlopeSetbackMeters = 80f;
    public static TerrainLine BestLine(TerrainPicture p, float objX, float objZ, float axisX, float axisZ, float frontage);
    public static bool FlankAnchored(TerrainPicture p, float endX, float endZ, float outwardX, float outwardZ);
  }
}
// ObjectiveObservationInput / BattlefieldObjectiveEstimate: TerrainStrength, ApproachDifficulty filled; TypeAnchorVerified set by the picture.
// EnemyVisibleSector.EnemyStrongGroundShare; EvidenceTag.EnemyOnStrongGround; ArmyEvidence.CrestLine; TacticalPlaybook.RequiresCrest;
// TacticalPlaybookKind.LineHold; ArmyIntentInference.Build(EnemyVisibleState) (ownEvidence deleted).
```

### Tests (`TerrainPictureTests`, red first)

- build: `PictureCellIdPrefersWaterAndCrossing`, `CrestNeedsReliefAndSteepnessLikeCheckTerrainPeak` (3 m pins), `CrestAxisPerpendicularToSlope`, `CoverSegmentRasterisedAlongDiagonalWall`, `RoadJunctionNeedsThreeRuns`, `TownNeedsThreeBuildingsWithin150m`, `BuilderHonoursDeadlineAcrossCoverAndCells`, `MissingTerrainDataNeverReady`, `PictureNotReadyMeansNoTerrain`, `PictureBuildAllocatesOnlyAtStart`;
- classification: `ObjectiveOnBridgeIsBridgeAndVerified`, `FordBeatsTownBeatsJunctionBeatsRidge`, `UnverifiedTypeStillDemoted`;
- strength: `TerrainStrengthWallOnCrestIsAnchor` (10 m crest, 0.57), `WallOnThreeMetreCrestIsNotAnchor` (0.42), `WallAloneIsNotAnchor` (0.35), `RiverInFrontAddsPoint4`, `ApproachDifficultyUsesSpeedCosts`;
- director: `AttackerAvoidsStrongEnemyHeldGround`, `DefenderPrefersStrongOwnGround`;
- lines: `BestLinePicksCrestAcrossAxis`, `LineRejectedOver30DegreesOff`, `ReverseSlopeLineSetBack80m`, `TerrainAnchorReplacesPreservedNone`, `FlankAnchoredByRiverWithin80m`, `AnchoredSideGetsNoGuardFlankSlot`;
- inference: `EnemyStandingOnStrongGroundReadsDefend`, `MovingEnemyOnStrongGroundNotDefend`, `BuildHasNoOwnEvidenceParameter` (compile);
- playbooks: `ReverseSlopeNeedsCrest`, `ReverseSlopeRectsBehindCrest`, `HighGroundLabelNeedsCrestShare`, `PlaybookTerrainFromPicture`.

### Steps

- [x] Tests first (above). Add the `UnityEngine.TerrainModule` reference (integrator decision 1: `<Private>false</Private>`, not shipped), its `refs/` symlink and the README refs-loop line in the slice's first commit.
- [x] Implement the builder and queries, then the consumers in this order: vision adapter and ledger (decisions 3-4), director (5), line and anchor planners (6), inference and playbook terrain (7), crest playbooks (8). Delete `ArmyEvidenceBuilder.BattlefieldTerrain`'s sampler and the `ownEvidence` parameter in the same commits as their replacements. Scopes: `tactical.terrain-picture.build` (5.0 ms per step), `tactical.terrain-line` (1.0 ms per ledger tick).
- [x] Docs: `docs/tactical-operations-ledger.md` (objective anchors, terrain lines, anchored flanks, director sign), `docs/tactical-orchestrator.md` (terrain picture, intent inference rule, playbook terrain), `docs/scourge-of-war-ai-anchors.md` (ReverseSlopeDefense now crest-based), `docs/vanilla-vision-and-movement.md` (cover tags and crest rule as the picture uses them), `docs/telemetry.md` (picture line, new reasons, `LineHold`), and the battle-core spec's architecture note (already names the picture).
- [x] **Acceptance:** tests pass; `rg -n "terrainStrength: 0f|approachDifficulty: 0f" src/WhiskeyRealism` finds nothing; `rg -n "TerrainKind.Open" src/WhiskeyRealism/Tactical/Orchestrator/ArmyIntentInference.cs` finds nothing; every `TacticalObjectiveType` value read by a planner branch has a producer (a source-pin test lists them); orphan-sweep and config-audit 0.

PROVE-MODE: harness for every pure rule (`TerrainPictureTests` plus the touched line-planner, director, inference and playbook tests; red first; mutation checks on the crest tests, the strength formula, the director sign and the anchored-side slot rule). Runtime proof is in-game (items 2.30-2.32): the picture-ready line with its build time, objective types and terrain-line reasons in the ledger rows, and a Defender holding a crest or wall line.

### Smoke watch (BC7 checklist, Session A items 2.30-2.32)

See `### BC7 smoke checklist`, items 2.30-2.32.

**As built (BC12):**

Branch `feat/battle-next`, base `0a312ef`. Commits: `69aeea5` (picture core, stepped builder, build tests, TerrainModule reference), `bee6872` (runtime builder, flag, classification, strength, director sign, line planner), `c747697` (anchor planner terrain branch, anchored-end slots, strong-ground inference, crest plays, `LineHold`, picture terrain; sampler and `ownEvidence` deleted), `1d49404` (scope, first-fire, crest axis use, `AnchorStrength`), then test and doc commits. Harness 2621 PASS (base 2574, +47 after verify round 1), build 0 warnings, config-audit 0, orphan-sweep 0 and `--include-test-only` 29, `tools/test_smoke_check.py` OK, `NavMeshQueriesSitAtTerrainHeight` still sees exactly three NavMesh query sites (BC12 adds none). 27 mutation plants, all killed (list in the build record).

What changed against the plan (each a finding or a deliberate deviation, with anchors):

- **Additive API beyond the interface block.** `TerrainPicture.Rules`, `CrestNear`, a private `RoadLayer` (a cell is road when at least 2 of its 16 id samples are road; the junction test reads it, because `Id` is the dominant id and a thin road is rarely dominant), `TryNearestCrossing(..., wantedId)` (the classifier needs bridge before ford), the constants `AnchorStrength` (0.55; the planners' literals now read it) and `StrongGroundStrength` (0.35); `TerrainLine.Length`; `TacticalTerrainLinePlanner.BestCrest` and `AnchorPoint`; `TacticalDefensiveLineAnchor.OnTerrainLine` / `AnchoredLeft` / `AnchoredRight`; `TacticalDefensiveLineAnchorPlanner.Plan(..., TerrainPicture, bool reverseSlope)` (the three-argument call reads `TerrainPicture.CurrentForPlanning()`); `CommandDoctrineAssignment.ApplyScourgeSlotTask` is `internal` with the two anchored arguments; `TacticalBattleLinePlanner.PlanNode` has an overload over a precomputed anchor (the doctrine build plans the anchor once per assignment).
- **Crest axis by slope, and crest runs by seed axis.** Four axes drop equally per cell on a ridge along an axis, so the axis is the one with the largest prominence per metre of its profile length (`CrestAxisPerpendicularToSlope`). The flood fill keeps a crest run to its seed's profile axis within 45 degrees (`CrestRunsDoNotJoinAcrossAxes`: a T of two ridges is two runs; chained neighbour-to-neighbour joining did not separate them because the diagonal cell at the meeting bridges both). This also gives `CrestAxis` its consumer.
- **Cover segment choice.** A collider's segment is the AABB diagonal whose quarter points are closer to the collider's surface by `Collider.ClosestPoint` (the plan said "the pair of opposite corners closer to its surface"); diagonal offsets use the same cells. Unverified in game: smoke item 2.31 shows whether a diagonal wall is found.
- **Flag off keeps the picture.** `TerrainPicture.Current()` (ready picture, used by playbook terrain) versus `CurrentForPlanning()` (the same, only with `Enable Battle Terrain Picture`): classification, terrain anchors, strong-ground evidence and the crest line read the second. The playbook terrain is not cached per battle any more (the deleted sampler was): `ClassifyFromPicture` is 35 array reads into a static scratch array.
- **Reverse slope reaches the line planner through the ledger.** The planners are plan-unaware, so `ArmyOrchestrator` passes `plan == ReverseSlopeDefense` through `StoreDoctrineForCommandStates(..., reverseSlope)` to `CommandDoctrineAssignment.Build`.
- **A held terrain line is stood on its own side.** `FrontageGeometry` treats a terrain-line anchor like a corridor intercept for placement (`:defensive-side-terrain-line`, positions behind the line's centre), skips the 700 m terrain-anchor minimum width (the line's own frontage stands) and skips the `:terrain-anchor` suffix. Crest-line ends are only anchored by a river (`FlankAnchored(..., includeCrest: false)`), since a crest's own ends are crest by construction.
- **Strong-ground share is of visible strength and facing the own centroid.** `FrontUnitSample` gains `Visible` / `OnStrongGround`; the per-enemy threat axis points at the own units' centroid; a remembered enemy is never flagged and not in the share's denominator (`MovingEnemyOnStrongGroundNotDefend`). `1 - (1 - 0.35)` rounds a hair under 0.35 in float, so the check carries `1e-4` slack. The intent rule precedes the Refuse branch but follows Attack, as written; `BuildForFrontage` also lost its `ownStrengthBucket` parameter (it only fed the dead evidence).
- **`OwnOnHighGroundShare`** counts every command group (`unittyp > 13`) in the observer's `unitsused`, not only held sectors' groups: the observer has no sector-to-group map, and the label is telemetry only.
- **Crest requirement after the jitter draw.** `TacticalPlaybookCatalog` checks `RequiresCrest` after the play's jitter draw so the other plays' jitter stream does not depend on whether a crest exists (a filter before the draw shifted every later play's jitter and flipped two tuned historical selections).
- **Scope and first-fire.** `tactical.terrain-line` wraps the whole `CommandDoctrineAssignment.Build` call in the ledger (the line planner is inside it), and `[once:tactical-battle-core:terrain-line]` marks the first terrain-line anchor; the picture-ready line also reports `maxStepMs`.
- **Test helpers.** `BattlePerceptionTests.ConsumerSnapshot`, `OwnUnit` and `Seen` became `internal` (the latter gained `standing`) for the BC12 sample tests; two source pins (`FlankResponseTests.ManeuverPlumbingIsWired`) follow the new ledger call text.
- **Verify round 1 (revise), fixes.** *F1:* a terrain-line anchor is a line to stand on, not a corridor intercept: its FormUp line slots stand on the anchor (`FrontageGeometry` assembly stand-off 0 for `OnTerrainLine`; corridor intercepts keep `AssemblyDistance` 210 m; depth, flank-guard and reserve slots keep their distances). The anchor already carries the crest's forward edge, the reverse-slope setback (crest 80 m behind, the same `ReverseSlopeSetbackMeters` the play rect uses) or the wall / river-line point; a river line is held `RiverStandOffMeters` = 150 m behind the river (an addition to decision 6: the river itself is not defensible ground). `DefenderLineSlotsStandOnTheTerrainLine` pins the order targets of `CommandDoctrineAssignment.Build` for a crest, a wall and the reverse slope. *F2 (plan decisions 4 and 5 corrected above):* every objective has its own axis, from its position toward the own centroid, so water and approach difficulty describe the ground the own side crosses (`EnemyHeldObjectiveWaterIsOnOurSide`, `EnemyHeldObjectiveApproachIsOnOurSide`). *Line direction (round 1 follow-up):* a terrain line is laid along its own direction, not across the avenue perpendicular: the anchor carries the line's unit direction and half length (`LineDirX/Z`, `LineHalfLength`), the lanes of `FrontageGeometry` follow the line (clamped to the line planner's 30 degree limit, `LineDirectionIsClampedToTheAngleLimit`), and past a line's ends a lane falls back to the perpendicular through the nearest end, so a short wall is never extended into the enemy (`TiltedWallSlotsStayOnTheWall` with a 28 degree wall both ways, `ShortWallEndsFallBackToThePerpendicular`). *Landing list:* the reverse-slope plumbing, the planning-flag gate of the classification, the evidence builder's `Current()` / `CurrentForPlanning()` split and the river-only flank anchor are pinned (`PlumbingFromTheArmyAndEvidenceIsPinned`, `ClassificationFollowsThePlanningFlag`, `OnlyARiverAnchorsAFlank`); `IndexBuildings` is three chunked phases (count, prefix, place) so no unit grows with the building count (`BuildingIndexIsIncremental`); the dead local `mid0` is gone.
- **Evidence after a host reboot.** The scratchpad was wiped mid-slice, which took the first red runs and the BC10 / BC11 sections of the build record with it. The red proof was rebuilt from git: the BC12 core tests (`69aeea5`) against the pre-BC12 source (`0a312ef`) fail to build, and the final tests against the source at `bee6872` (before the consumers) fail with 154 compile errors; the mutation run was repeated against the final tree.

Answers to the planning round's four open questions (integrator note, quoted; the quotes keep that round's provisional ordinals: their #100 is now #105 and their #101 is #106):

Ordinals stay provisional and are assigned at merge to `main`.

## BC13 — battle AI level of detail

**Goal:** spend producer time where the battle is. Today every producer evaluates every eligible own group on every doctrine tick (one arbiter tick per AI side per vanilla side-stat cycle), whether the group is in a firefight or holding a hill 3 km from anything it has seen. BC13 gives each group a tick tier from perceived and own state only. A group far from every perceived enemy, settled, on a static task, with no pending order and nothing changed in what its producers read, re-evaluates every fourth doctrine tick; every other group evaluates every tick, and a group is promoted on the tick any of its producers' inputs changes. This is the per-entity update-rate scheme real-time strategy AIs use (full rate near the action, a slower cadence elsewhere, an immediate wake-up on events); here every wake-up rule is fog-honest and every interval is on the doctrine clock.

**Branch and order:** `feat/battle-next-bc13` from `feat/battle-next` after BC11 merges (it reads BC11's `UnitRecord.ReceivedFire` and BC11's maneuver state); it can be written in parallel with BC12 and merges after it (both edit `PostureIntentProducer.cs`). No Harmony patch, so no ordinal.

**Owned files:**
- Create (pure, test csproj): `Tactical/Battle/BattleLod.cs` (tier rules, due schedule, promotion), `Tactical/Battle/BattleLodState.cs` (per-side state per group id: tier, last evaluation hour, the input signatures of decision 2, the nav-deferred mark; BattleScope).
- Modify: `Tactical/Battle/BattleCoreContracts.cs` (`EnemyObservation.ChangedSinceLastTier`, `LodTier`, `LodPromotion`), `BattlePerception.cs` (sets the latch), `SlicePressureBuilder.cs` (closest Remembered at confidence ≥ 0.5, in its existing loop), `BattleSnapshot.cs` (`ClosestRemembered`), `OrderArbiter.cs` (carried claims), `OrderExecutor.cs` (`IsUnsettled`), `BattleCoreTick.cs` (tier pass and carry before the producers; `StandDown` resets the side's tiers; `TacticalLod` row), `Tactical/Operations/TacticalFriendlySpatialGrid.cs` (`NavPlanBudget`: stable cursor), the five tiered producers (`Producers/PostureIntentProducer.cs`: the posture and facing-pulse producers; `Producers/GroupStanceIntentProducer.cs`; `Producers/PlayIntentProducer.cs`: the division play; `Producers/ArtilleryIntentProducer.cs`: the non-point-blank instance), `Tactical/Orchestrator/TacticalBattleCoordinatorRuntime.cs` (`PrepareForNewBattleDay`, `:475`, resets the tiers), `Plugin.cs` (one bind), the test csproj, `docs/tactical-orchestrator.md`, `docs/telemetry.md`.
- Test file: `BattleLodTests.cs`.

### Findings (shipped code and vanilla, build 14703111)

1. **Cadence.** `BattleCoreTick.RunArbiter` (`BattleCoreTick.cs:128-221`) runs all 12 producers over the side's snapshot once per arbiter tick (the #68 Postfix on `AdjustGroupFormations`, once per side-stat cycle); each producer loops over every own record (posture `PostureIntentProducer.cs:197`, from a rotating start index; facing pulse `:1224`; fire control `:1367`; `GroupStanceIntentProducer.cs:36`, `ArtilleryIntentProducer.cs:64`, `WithdrawalIntentProducer.cs:42`, `ChargeDemotionIntentProducer.cs:55`, `ReconIntentProducer.cs:140`, `ChargeIntentProducer.Plan.cs:347`, the division play `PlayIntentProducer.cs:95`) and builds its intents for every eligible group.
2. **The tick length is vanilla data, and the spacing is uneven.** The side-stat cycle runs when `currenttimefromstart − lastsidestatupdate ≥ GamePrefs.sidestatupdatecycle` (`CalculateSideStatsAndUpdateAITasks` 84572-84575), unless a caller forces it with `overridelastupdate`. `sidestatupdatecycle` is parsed from battleprefs (55059; the shipped `Config/battleprefs.txt` line 1034 = 0.015 game-h), and `loadcontrols` prefers a battlefield's own `Battlefields/<level>/<sublevel>/battleprefs.txt` outside operations 3 and 8 (54325-54328). So the tick length differs per battlefield, and successive ticks are frame-quantised (and sometimes forced), not exactly one cycle apart.
3. **Claims are per tick.** `OrderArbiter.Resolve` publishes only this tick's winners (`OrderArbiter.cs:93-135`), and `BeginTick` clears the working table (`:67-71`). A group with no intent this tick has no published claim. Three readers depend on that table: D4 inline refusal (`TryGetClaim`, `:138-141`, read by #42 and #56), the tie hysteresis (a tie keeps last tick's source, `:110-115`) and `OrderExecutor.RetryStillClaimed` (`BattleCoreTick.cs:193`). `StandDown` clears every claim (`BattleCoreTick.cs:122-126`).
4. **What the tiered producers read for a group.** Besides the group's own record, task and executor state: its command parent's state and layout slot (`PostureIntentProducer.cs:275-293`: `LeafSlotTarget.TryResolve` from `ParentIndex`, the parent's last refuse, the parent moving or with a pending order); the doctrine order's target, not only its task type (`PostureLedger.TryResolveDoctrineOrder`, `:208`); the closest Visible enemy at any range (`TryThreat`, `:772-779`, no range limit; the facing reads it); and friendly positions (the friendly grid is filled from every standing own unit up to brigade level, `:126-133`, and read along candidate paths, `:1023`).
5. **Nav deferral is producer-local.** The posture producer's NavMesh planning is capped at 4 plans per side per tick (`PostureIntentProducer.cs:79-81`; `NavPlanBudget.TryConsume`, `TacticalFriendlySpatialGrid.cs:134-139`). A group needing a plan once the budget is spent sets `NavDeferred` and submits nothing that tick (`"nav-budget-deferred"`, `PostureIntentProducer.cs:542-545`, `:880`); no executor or arbiter state records it. The cursor advances by the plans consumed, modulo the number of records walked (`TacticalFriendlySpatialGrid.cs:141-144`), so over a changing walked set it gives no fairness bound.
6. **`combatstatussubordinates` is not perceived state.** Vanilla computes it in `SetInterruptedStatusOfSubordinates` (116831-116925) from `GetClosestEnemyUnit`, which reads `unitrange.closestenemyunitfar` (123760-123770) from range lists with no fog test for the AI side (122650). BC13 does not use it.
7. **Cost.** Numbers from the 2026-10-15 performance review (estimates on Mono, not measured; the review names `tactical.producer.*` as a first-smoke measurement): the side-stat-cycle frame for an engaged 500-regiment side is ≈ 20-50 ms today and ≈ 8-15 ms after the review's observer and slice-pressure fixes; within that, "the rest" (#44's sectors, #47, the arbiter and the 12 producers) is about 5 ms, and the arbiter alone measured 0.025-0.08 ms on net8 (800 and 2,400 intents). The producers are therefore estimated at ≈ 2-4 ms per side per cycle on Mono. The code these numbers rest on: the producer loops of finding 1, the scope `tactical.producer.posture` (`PostureIntentProducer.cs:185`), and the nav budget of finding 5. With ≈ 60-150 groups a side and 4 plans per tick, a full side re-plan takes 15-38 ticks to plan every group.

### Decisions

1. **Two tiers (`BattleLod.Classify`, pure).** `LodTier.Full` evaluates every doctrine tick. `LodTier.Distant` evaluates when `now − lastEvaluated ≥ DistantIntervalHours`, where `DistantIntervalHours = 4 × cycle − cycle / 2` and `cycle` = `GamePrefs.sidestatupdatecycle`, read once per battle (finding 2); the half-tick tolerance makes a jittered fourth tick due. A group (unit type ≥ brigade, `TacticalUnitType.BattleGroupBrigade`) is `Distant` only when **all** of these hold this tick, each read from own records and perceived observations, never `EnemyRecords`:
   - no Visible enemy within `DistantMeters` = 1,500 m of the group or of any unit under it (`BattleSnapshot.ClosestVisibleEnemy`, already computed per own record by `SlicePressureBuilder`, folded up the parent map once per tick);
   - no Remembered observation at confidence ≥ 0.5 within 1,500 m (`BattleSnapshot.ClosestRemembered`, new, the same loop);
   - not under fire and not in melee: `UnitRecord.ReceivedFire` false and `UnitFlags.InMelee` clear on the group and every unit under it;
   - settled: the posture producer's own test is false for the group, `PostureSense.ActiveMove(record, movingDescendants)` and `PostureSense.PendingOrder(record, activeMove, allowStaleQueuedBypass: false)` (`CommandPostureExecutor.cs:874-896`), over the snapshot's moving-descendant count the posture tick already builds (`PostureTick.State.Moving`, read at `PostureIntentProducer.cs:273`, `:285`), passed in through `LodSideInput.MovingDescendants`. Vanilla's `groupsubordinatesmoving` is not used: it counts only subordinates whose `groupaiobject` is the group or null (120510-120522) and is refreshed only inside `UpdateGroupStrength` (120197, 120677);
   - a static task: the doctrine task is `None`, `FormUp`, `HoldObjective`, `HoldChoke`, `GuardFlank`, `ReserveWait` or `Consolidate`, with no BC11 maneuver state (phase `None`) and no doctrine latch (the main-arrival hold, the BC9 probe hold);
   - no pending order: `OrderExecutor.IsUnsettled(groupId)` is false (no queued retry, no Move outcome `Issued` that has not yet resolved to `Moved` or `Failed`, and last tick's winners for the group were not skipped for `Cooldown`), and the group is not nav-deferred (decision 7);
   - not the W&L player's command or one of its command ancestors (`PlayerCommandIndex`, D5 ancestry: their dispatch is time-sensitive);
   - no promotion this tick (decision 2) and none within the last `MinFullHours` = 2 × `cycle`.
   *Residual, intended:* `combatstatussubordinates` is not an input (finding 6). A group with a hidden enemy inside vanilla's interruption distance that is not firing on it can be `Distant`; that is the fog-honest outcome, because the AI cannot know about that enemy. Fire received (`ReceivedFire`) and melee still promote it.
   Recon, withdrawal, charge, charge demotion, point-blank artillery, fire control and reserve release are not tiered: they act only in contact or on scouts, which are full-rate by definition, or they are side-level.
2. **Promotion (`BattleLod.Promote`): every input the tiered producers read.** The tier pass runs at the start of each arbiter tick on that tick's snapshot, before any producer. `BattleLodState` keeps, per group, the input signature from its last evaluation; a `Distant` group becomes `Full` in that same tick, whatever its schedule, when any of the following holds:
   - (a) **perception**: an observation within `PromoteMeters` = 3,000 m of the group has `ChangedSinceLastTier` (a latch `BattlePerception` sets when an observation appears, turns Visible, turns Remembered or is dropped, on every snapshot build including deployment, battle-start and gated-off builds; `Classify` clears it after reading, so a change seen between arbiter ticks is not lost);
   - (b) **command parent**: the group's D5 parent is active by the producer's own test (`PostureSense.ActiveMove(own[parent], t.Moving[parent])` or `PendingOrder`, the expression at `PostureIntentProducer.cs:285-287`), is unsettled (`IsUnsettled`), or the group's layout slot from `LeafSlotTarget.TryResolve` (parent position, rotation and last refuse) has moved more than `OrderArbiter.DedupeMeters` (60 m) or turned more than `OrderExecutor.FacingToleranceDegrees` (15°) since the last evaluation. A parent's promotion for its own reasons does not pass to its children: they read only the parent's activity and their slot, which this trigger checks;
   - (c) **order target**: the target the posture pipeline resolves for the group has moved more than 60 m, or the order's `ManeuverHeading` more than 15°, since the last evaluation, or the task type changed. `ITaskLookup.TryTarget` returns that resolved target, not only the order's field: `PrimaryTarget` (or `SupportTarget` for the support tasks), else the objective approach built from `ArmyOrchestrator.CurrentOperation.PrimaryObjectiveId` (`PostureIntentProducer.cs:735-757`, `:788`, `:862`), so an operation change under an unchanged order promotes the group;
   - (d) **distant enemy bearing**: the bearing from the group to the closest Visible enemy at any range (the one `TryThreat` reads) changed by at least 15° (`OrderExecutor.FacingToleranceDegrees`), or its distance by at least 250 m, since the last evaluation, or the group gained or lost a closest Visible enemy. Which observation is closest is not compared: in a perceived enemy line the closest unit alternates between near-equidistant units with no change the facing or threat target acts on;
   - (e) **friendly corridor**: the set of friendly-grid units (standing own units up to brigade level, finding 4) inside the group's path corridor changed since the last evaluation; the corridor is the segment from the group to its order target (or its layout slot), 250 m wide, and the set is compared by a count and an id hash;
   - (f) **plan**: the side's plan signature changed: `ArmyOrchestrator.HasPlan`, `CurrentPlan.PlanId`, `CurrentPlan.Phase` and `CurrentPlan.MainEffortSector` (`TacticalBattlePlan.cs:60-104`), hashed once per tick; every group of the side is promoted for that tick;
   - any condition of decision 1 stops holding (an enemy closes within 1,500 m, fire, melee, a move, a pending order, a nav deferral).
   Promotion is a pure function of this tick's snapshot, the group's stored signatures and its previous tier, so a contact is never delayed by a tier: the producer sees the group on the tick the contact enters perception.
3. **Stagger.** A group entering `Distant` takes as its last evaluation hour `now − (k mod 4) × cycle`, where `k` is its own index, so the distant groups spread over the four ticks of an interval instead of all evaluating on the same tick.
4. **Carried claims (`OrderArbiter.CarryForward`).** Order inside `RunArbiter`: `arbiter.BeginTick()` (clears the working table), then the tier pass, then `CarryForward` for every `Distant` group that is not due, then the producers, then `Resolve`. `CarryForward` copies the group's cells from the published table into the working table, marked `Carried`. `Resolve` treats a carried cell like a submitted intent of the same priority and source (an intent another producer submits for that group this tick wins or loses by the usual rule, and a tie keeps the carried source, as the hysteresis would have), publishes it as this tick's claim (D4 refusal and `RetryStillClaimed` see it), and does not write it to the winners list (it is not executed again). A carried claim lives until the group is due or promoted, when the group's producers run and resubmit; there is no carry count, so uneven tick spacing (finding 2) never leaves a non-due tick without its claim.
5. **Why decisions stay the same.** The tiered producers' decision for a group is a function of these inputs and nothing else: the group's own record and task (decision 1), its command parent's state and layout slot (2b), the target the posture pipeline resolves for it and the order's maneuver heading (2c), its slice and flank pressure and its closest Visible enemy at any range (decision 1 and 2a, 2d), the friendly units along its path corridor (2e), the side's plan (2f), and its own executor state (decision 1, `IsUnsettled`). Each input is either a `Distant` condition or a promotion trigger, so while a group stays `Distant` none of them has changed, and re-running the producers would submit the same intents as on its last evaluation. Those intents would change nothing: the arbiter publishes the same claims (decision 4), and the executor would plan each as `Skip(Duplicate)` (same target within 60 m; same stance, formation or facing), since a group with any other plan is `IsUnsettled` and stays `Full`. A time-driven rule inside a producer could fire up to three ticks later; the static-task list excludes every task that carries one today (the movement, probe, screen and delay tasks), and the two latches that live on static tasks (the main-arrival hold, the probe hold) keep their groups `Full`. Spec § 6 states exactly this input list, as amended by BC14 (BC14 decision 12 adds triggers (g)-(j) for the inputs BC14 gives these producers).
6. **Clock.** Every interval above is a multiple of `GamePrefs.sidestatupdatecycle`, compared against doctrine hours (`TacticalDoctrineTime`), so tiers freeze with the doctrine clock overnight and never read real time.
7. **Navigation budget and nav deferral.** A group the posture producer defers for budget (`NavDeferred`) is reported to `BattleLodState` (`LodPromotion.Pending`) and stays `Full` until a nav plan runs for it. `NavPlanBudget` walks the due groups in a stable order: ascending group instance id, starting after the last id it planned (a persisted id cursor, not an index modulo the walked count). Fairness bound: with `D` groups waiting for a plan and a budget of 4 plans per tick, every waiting group is planned within `ceil(D / 4)` ticks, the same as at full rate (100 groups: at most 25 ticks); a contact group never waits behind a `Distant` group, because `Distant` groups that are not due are not walked.
8. **Stand-down and new day.** `BattleCoreTick.StandDown` (any gate-off: `macroai` 3, `state` < 5, the master toggle) also resets the side's `BattleLodState`: every group `Full` for `MinFullHours`, no carried claims (the stand-down cleared them). `TacticalBattleCoordinatorRuntime.PrepareForNewBattleDay` (`:475`) does the same for both sides.
9. **Estimated saving (to be measured).** With the tiered producers at ≈ 2-4 ms per side per cycle on Mono (finding 7), a `Distant` group's share of that work falls to one evaluation in four. The `Distant` share is to be measured, given triggers (a)-(f) (smoke 2.34 reports it with the promotions by reason); for scale, if 50-70 % of groups were `Distant` during the approach and 20-40 % once engaged, the saving (≈ share × 0.75 of the tiered producers' cost) would be ≈ 0.75-2.1 ms and ≈ 0.3-1.2 ms per side per cycle (illustrative, not a prediction), doubled under AI-vs-AI and W&L, where both sides are AI-run. The tier pass costs O(own records) plus a 3,000 m bucket-grid walk over the latched observations and one corridor query per `Distant` group: estimated < 0.2 ms per side per tick (scope `tactical.lod`, threshold 0.5 ms). The observer and slice-pressure fixes of the performance review remain the first frame-time fixes; BC13 removes the per-group producer cost that grows with army size. Smoke item 2.35 measures it.
10. **Telemetry.** One `TacticalLod` Observation row per side per 0.1 doctrine-h: `full`, `distant`, `due`, `carried`, `navDeferred`, and promotions by reason (`observation`, `parent`, `target`, `bearing`, `corridor`, `plan`, `distance`, `fire`, `moving`, `pending`, `player-chain`). OnceLog `[once:tactical-battle-core:lod-first-fire] [BattleLod] first distant tier: alliance=<a> full=<n> distant=<n>`.
11. **Rollback.** `Tactical` / `Enable Battle AI Level Of Detail` (default true). Off: every group is `Full` every tick and no claim is carried, which is today's behaviour exactly.

### Interfaces — Produces

```csharp
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Battle {
  public enum LodTier : byte { Full = 0, Distant = 1 }
  [System.Flags] public enum LodPromotion { None = 0, Observation = 1, Parent = 2, Target = 4, Bearing = 8, Corridor = 16, Plan = 32,
    Distance = 64, Fire = 128, Moving = 256, Pending = 512, PlayerChain = 1024 }
  public static class BattleLod {                                          // pure
    public const float DistantMeters = 1500f, PromoteMeters = 3000f, RememberedMinConfidence = 0.5f,
      TargetMoveMeters = 60f, TurnDegrees = 15f, EnemyDistanceChangeMeters = 250f, CorridorWidthMeters = 250f;
    public const int IntervalTicks = 4, MinFullTicks = 2;
    public static double DistantIntervalHours(double cycleHours);        // 4 × cycle − cycle / 2
    public static void Classify(BattleSnapshot s, BattleLodState state, in LodSideInput side, double nowHours);   // tier, due, promotion reason per own group; clears the observation latches
    public static bool IsStaticTask(CommandTaskType task);
  }
  public struct LodSideInput { public double CycleHours; public int PlanSignature; public OrderExecutor Executor; public ITaskLookup Tasks; public IFriendlyCorridor Friendly;
    public int[] MovingDescendants; }                                    // PostureTick.State.Moving, per own index
  public interface ITaskLookup { CommandTaskType TaskFor(int groupId); bool TryTarget(int groupId, out float x, out float z, out float maneuverHeading);   // the posture pipeline's resolved target (order target, else the operation's objective approach)
    bool HasLatchOrManeuver(int groupId); bool TrySlot(int ownIndex, out float x, out float z, out float heading); }
  public interface IFriendlyCorridor { int Signature(int selfId, float x0, float z0, float x1, float z1, float widthMeters, out int count); }
  public sealed class BattleLodState {                                     // per side, BattleScope
    public bool IsDue(int ownIndex); public LodTier TierOf(int ownIndex); public LodPromotion LastPromotion(int ownIndex);
    public void MarkNavDeferred(int groupId); public void MarkNavPlanned(int groupId);
    public void ResetAllFull(double nowHours);                             // StandDown and a new battle day
    public int FullCount { get; } public int DistantCount { get; } public int DueCount { get; }
  }
}
// OrderArbiter.CarryForward(int groupId) (carried cells: published as claims, never emitted as winners; live until due or promoted);
// OrderExecutor.IsUnsettled(int groupId); BattleSnapshot.ClosestRemembered(int ownIndex, out float distance);
// NavPlanBudget: persisted last-planned instance id, ascending-id walk over the due groups;
// EnemyObservation.ChangedSinceLastTier (BattleCoreContracts.cs, additive).
```

### Tests (`BattleLodTests`, red first; the file does not compile before `BattleLod` exists)

- **The contact test:** `ContactEventNeverDelayedByTier`: a `Distant` group on a tick it is not due; a new Visible observation appears 1,200 m away (and, in a second case, 2,800 m away, latched; in a third, the group receives fire with no observation change; in a fourth, the observation appears on a deployment-time snapshot build between two arbiter ticks) → `IsDue` is true on that same arbiter tick and the posture producer submits the group's intent on that tick. Mutation checks: the promotion pass removed, the 3,000 m radius cut to 1,500 m, `ReceivedFire` ignored, the latch cleared per build instead of per tier pass — each must fail it.
- **Equivalence:** `SteadyDistantGroupExecutesSameOrdersAsFullRate`: a 40-tick fixture battle run twice, LOD on and off, comparing the executed vanilla calls per group. The steady run must match, and so must four scripted cases, each changing only one input: (1) the leaf brigade's parent division moves 300 m; (2) the group's order `PrimaryTarget` moves 200 m with the same task; (3) a Visible enemy column 2,000 m away crosses laterally (bearing change ≥ 15°, distance change < 250 m); (4) a friendly brigade walks across the group's path corridor; (5) the army's operation changes its primary objective while the group's order stays the same (no `PrimaryTarget`, so the target comes from the objective approach). In each case the group is promoted on the tick the input changes, and the executed calls are identical to full rate. A sixth case (a scripted contact at tick 17) keeps the original contact check. Mutation: removing any one of triggers (b)-(e), or the objective-approach fallback in `TryTarget`, must fail its case.
- tiers: `DistantNeedsEveryCondition` (one case per condition of decision 1), `MovingTaskNeverDistant`, `PendingOrderKeepsFull` (an `Issued` move not yet resolved; a queued retry; a `Cooldown` skip), `PlayerChainAlwaysFull`, `PlanChangePromotesWholeSide`, `PromotionRateBoundedUnderReSortingEnemyLine` (a fixture enemy line whose closest unit alternates every tick with no bearing or distance change past the thresholds: the group stays `Distant`), `ParentPromotionDoesNotCascade` (a division promoted by its own trigger (a) leaves a settled child brigade `Distant`), `RememberedBelowHalfConfidenceDoesNotBlockDistant`, `TierUsesOnlyPerceivedState` (a hidden enemy record 300 m away, with `combatstatussubordinates` set on the vanilla side of the fixture, does not promote), `SettledUsesProducerActiveMoveTest` (a pooled brigade whose regiments move under a higher AI group: vanilla's `groupsubordinatesmoving` is 0, the snapshot's moving-descendant count is not, and the group is not `Distant`);
- schedule: `DistantIntervalFromSideStatCycle` (cycles 0.015 and 0.03 game-h), `CarryCoversEveryNonDueTickUnderJitteredSpacing` (tick spacings 0.0149, 0.0151 and 0.02 game-h, plus a forced tick: the group has a published claim on every tick until it is due), `StaggerSpreadsDistantGroupsOverFourTicks`, `MinFullHoldsTwoTicks`, `TiersFreezeWithDoctrineClockOvernight` (night-freeze fixture);
- arbiter: `CarryForwardRunsAfterBeginTickBeforeProducers`, `CarriedClaimRefusesInlineIntentLikeSubmitted` (D4), `CarriedClaimKeepsTieHysteresis`, `RetryStillClaimedAcrossSkippedTick`, `CarriedClaimNeverExecuted`, `CarriedClaimLivesUntilDueOrPromoted`, `StandDownResetsTiersToFull`, `NewBattleDayResetsTiersToFull`;
- nav: `NavDeferredGroupStaysFullUntilPlanned`, `NavCursorStableAscendingId`, `WholeSideReplanPlansEveryContactGroupWithinBound` (100 groups, budget 4 per tick: every group, contact groups included, is planned within 25 ticks; the contact groups are never behind a non-due `Distant` group);
- rollback: `FlagOffIsEveryGroupEveryTick`.

### Steps

- [ ] Tests first (above); the contact test and the equivalence test (all six cases) are written before any tier code.
- [ ] Implement the snapshot fields, the observation latch and `ClosestRemembered`, then `BattleLod` and its state (signatures for triggers b-f), then the arbiter's carried claims and `IsUnsettled`, then the stable `NavPlanBudget` cursor and the nav-deferred report (and the correction of the comment at `PostureIntentProducer.cs:79-80`: the 4-plan budget is the posture producer's alone, its single `TryConsume` caller is `:878`), then the tier pass in `BattleCoreTick.RunArbiter` (after `EnsureSnapshot` and `arbiter.BeginTick()`, then `CarryForward`, before the first producer), the `StandDown` and new-day resets, then the five producers' `IsDue` checks at their group loop (a regiment-level decision uses its command group's tier). Scope `tactical.lod` (0.5 ms).
- [ ] Docs: `docs/tactical-orchestrator.md` § Battle Core (tiers, promotion triggers, carried claims, nav fairness), `docs/telemetry.md` (`TacticalLod`), and this plan's BC7 smoke checklist (items 2.34-2.35).
- [ ] **Acceptance:** tests pass; `rg -n "realtimeSinceStartup|Environment.TickCount|DateTime" src/WhiskeyRealism/Tactical/Battle/BattleLod*.cs` finds nothing; `rg -n "EnemyRecords|combatstatussubordinates|CombatStatusSubordinates" src/WhiskeyRealism/Tactical/Battle/BattleLod*.cs` finds nothing; `rg -n "0\.015" src/WhiskeyRealism/Tactical/Battle/BattleLod*.cs` finds nothing (the cycle comes from `GamePrefs`); the BC7 writer grep is unchanged (BC13 adds no writer); orphan-sweep and config-audit 0.

PROVE-MODE: harness for every pure rule (`BattleLodTests`, red first; mutation checks on each promotion trigger, the static-task list, the carried-claim lifetime, the latch and the doctrine-clock interval). Runtime proof is in-game: items 2.34-2.35, including the on/off measurement of the producer scopes.
