## BC14 — formations, slots and lanes

**Goal:** AI groups fight in the formations their echelon and task call for, with their best regiments in the first line, supporting lines at a real distance, batteries and cavalry where the ground and doctrine want them, one owner of each group's formation, and the W&L player told where they stand. Every gap comes from the 2026-10-15 formation-layout research, re-read on build 14703111 for this plan (findings 4-5 below correct the research note's effective table); the user chose to fix all ten (2026-10-15, "Fix all (Recommended)").

**Branch and order:** `feat/battle-next-bc14` from `feat/battle-next` after BC13 merges. It reads BC8's troop-quality fields, BC10's column-march target (the posture pipeline's `targetFormation = RoadRoutePlanner.ColumnGroupFormation` while routed) and terrain grid, BC11's refuse path (decision 2), BC12's terrain picture and BC13's tiers. **`main` (`0c2bd01` or later) is merged into `feat/battle-next` before BC14 step 2** (the integrator does it after BC10 lands): it brings `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/TranspilerLabelTests.cs` and its clone sweep over all of `src/WhiskeyRealism` (`TranspilerLabelTests.cs:66-83` on `main`, from `dce672e`), which the two Transpilers below must pass. The patches are named, not numbered: ordinals are assigned at merge to `main` (`main` `0c2bd01`'s next free ordinal is #109; BC10's and BC11's provisional #105/#106 collide with `main`'s armament patches #105-#108 and are renumbered at merge too). Provisional bug ids: BUG-TAC-024 (index bug), BUG-TAC-025 (cavalry side flip), BUG-TAC-026 (corps-level mirror).

**Owned files:**
- Create (pure, test csproj): `Tactical/Operations/GroupFormationDoctrine.cs` (formation target per task, echelon, unit mix and frontage), `Tactical/Battle/SlotFitness.cs` (regiment order key and the stable insertion pass), `Tactical/Battle/LayoutReplica.cs` (vanilla layouts 0/1/2 as pure code, with Whiskey's gaps, for the W&L slot and tests), `Tactical/Battle/SupportLineClamp.cs` (pushed-row terrain and zone clamp), `Tactical/Operations/LaneOrder.cs` (lateral lane ranking with hysteresis), `Tactical/Battle/BatterySiting.cs`, `Tactical/Battle/BugFixes/CavalryFlipIl.cs` and `CorpsMirrorIl.cs` (the two IL rewriters, the pattern of `Strategic/BugFixes/*Il.cs` on `main`).
- Create (runtime): `Patches/VanillaFormationPassPatch.cs` (A2: index fix and ownership), `Patches/SlotQualityOrderPatch.cs` (R1), `Patches/GroupLayoutPatch.cs` (R2 support lines, the battery slot of decision 7 and R3's player slot: one Postfix on `BuildGroupOffsetPositions` plus a non-skipping context Prefix/Finalizer on `SetGroupFormation(Regiment, …)`), `Patches/CavalryFlankFlipFixPatch.cs` (A3), `Patches/CorpsLayoutMirrorFixPatch.cs` (A4), `Patches/LayoutObserverPatch.cs` (record only).
- Modify: `Tactical/Operations/CommandFormationCorrection.cs` (`TargetFormationForTask` deleted; its callers use `GroupFormationDoctrine.Target`), `Tactical/Operations/TacticalFacingPulseDoctrine.cs` (the pulse's formation target), `Tactical/Battle/Producers/PostureIntentProducer.cs` (the posture pipeline's `targetFormation`), `Tactical/Battle/Producers/ArtilleryIntentProducer.cs` (battery siting), `Tactical/Battle/BattleLod.cs` and `BattleLodState.cs` (BC13 triggers (g)-(j), decision 12), `Tactical/Battle/BattleCoreContracts.cs` / `BattleSnapshotBuilder.cs` (`UnitRecord.InfantryRegiments`, vanilla `groupcountperunittype[0]`), `Tactical/Operations/TacticalBattleLinePlanner.cs` (artillery line on ground; lanes from `LaneOrder`), `Tactical/Operations/CommandDoctrineAssignment.cs` (lane index, flank-guard choice), `Tactical/Orchestrator/DirectChildAllocator.cs` (main-effort quality factor), `Tactical/Battle/TacticalPlayerDispatchRuntime.cs` and `OrderExecutorRuntime.cs` (slot text on the ancestor dispatch), `Plugin.cs` (ten binds), `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/TranspilerLabelTests.cs` (the two new rewriters; the file arrives with the `main` merge), the test csproj, `docs/bug-fixes/vanilla-tactical-battlefield.md` (BUG-TAC-024..026), `docs/patch-catalog.md`, `docs/tactical-orchestrator.md`, `docs/tactical-operations-ledger.md`, `docs/telemetry.md`, `docs/wl-player-model.md`, `AGENTS.md` (§ Patch hygiene: A2's argument rewrite named as a steer).
- Test file: `FormationSlotTests.cs`.

### Vanilla and Whiskey findings (build 14703111)

1. **Group formations and layouts.** Group formation ids are 0 Line, 1 Double Line, 2 Successive Lines, 3 Attack Column, 4 March Column (debug names, 5883). `SetGroupFormation` maps a group formation to a regiment formation (`newformation2`: 0/1/2 → line 0, 3 → attack column 2, 4 → march column 3; 91909-91917) and to a layout per level (`num2` for a brigade, unittyp 14; `num3` for higher groups; 91920-91954): Double Line lays a brigade in two rows and a division's brigades abreast; Successive Lines lays a division's (or corps') children one behind another; Attack Column lays a division in successive lines of brigades whose regiments are in attack column; Line and March Column are one row at every level. `BuildGroupOffsetPositions` (92203-92521) builds each layout bottom-up (the deepest trees first, the ordered group last, 91988-92030); row gaps are `distancefirstandsecondlinedoubleline` 10 m (Double Line), `distancesuccessivelines` 5 m (Successive Lines) and `groupunitdistance[0]` 2.5 m otherwise (92425-92433; unitprefs 1030-1043), measured from the back of one row to the front of the next (92467-92470, 92515); +z is backward and column 0 is the left flank at levels 1-2 (`GetAngleTerrainPosXYStatic` 26937-26950, placement 92086).
2. **Whiskey flattens tasked groups to Line.** `CommandFormationCorrection.TargetFormationForTask` (`CommandFormationCorrection.cs:146-176`) returns 0 for every attack, support, fix, form-up, assembly, hold, choke, flank-guard, fall-back, delay, consolidate, reserve-wait, release and recover task, and keeps the current formation (march column → 0) for screen, probe and scout. Both Formation sources use it: the posture pipeline's formation target and the facing pulse's (`CommandPostureExecutor.cs:1231-1246`, `TacticalFacingPulseDoctrine`). Vanilla deploys every AI group in Double Line (`SetGroupFormation(…, 1, …)` at 85750, 85859, 86255, which also sets `formationordered` = 1).
3. **Vanilla's own formation pass.** `AdjustGroupFormations` (5875-5935) runs in `UpdateAITasks` (5870), just before the #68 arbiter Postfix on the same method (`BattleCoreArbiterPatch.cs:35-40`, `Priority.Last`). For each group in `unitsused` with a settled stance and no path (5884-5887) it first refreshes the group's statistics, `UpdateGroupStrength(overridecalc: true, ignoreactivestate: true)` (5890; in battle this is the per-cycle refresh of an AI group's `groupsubordinatesmoving`, `combatstatussubordinates`, `covervaluesubordinates`, `groupstrength` and `flanksthreated`: `Regiment.Update`'s refresh runs only on the campaign map, 112203-112263, and `CalculateSideStatsAndUpdateAITasks` refreshes only the top battle-data groups, 84648), then, with `groupsubordinatesmoving` ≤ 0 and `combatstatussubordinates` ≤ 0 (5891), picks `num` by stance (5895-5923): screen 0; defend excluded (5925); attack 1 when the group's subtree holds 4 or more infantry regiments (`groupcountperunittype[0]`, counted per regiment in `UpdateGroupStrength`, 120310), else 0; assault: brigade 3, division and up 2, cavalry group 1. It calls `bunits.SetFormation(go, num, exitifsameformation: true)` when `formation != num && formationordered != num && covervaluesubordinates <= 0` (5925-5927).
4. **A battle group's own `formation` field is 3.** At battle init every unit, groups included, calls `ChangeRegimentFormation(SetFormationParam(formation))` (112757). A battle group has `blockobjects` = 1 (112688-112691), so the call does not take its `blockobjects <= 0` return (135422-135425); for `unittyp > 13` outside the campaign it forces `num = 3` (135462-135465) and then sets `formation = num` (135677). The only other writers are a save load (74101), the campaign branch (112753) and another struct (88404). Group orders never send `ChangeRegimentFormation` to the group itself: `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel(…, level 13, …)` (91955-91963, 92572-92628) reaches only trees whose `hierarchylevel` = `max(13, unittyp)` (92736) is 13, i.e. regiments. So vanilla's gate `formation != num` is false exactly for `num` = 3.
5. **Vanilla's effective formation pass, and its index bug.** `SetFormation` treats its argument as a UI button index and, for a group, remaps 0 → 3, 1 → 1, 2 → 1, 3 → 2, 4 → 4, 5 → 0 (91001-91024); `CheckFormationRestrictions` leaves group values alone (unit types 0-4 only); `exitifsameformation` returns when the mapped value equals `formationordered` (91026-91029), else it stores it (91030) and queues the courier order. `AdjustGroupFormations` passes group formation ids (finding 3). With `formation` = 3 (finding 4) and `formationordered` = 1 after deployment (finding 2), the effective result per stance is:

   | Stance and group | Vanilla's `num` (intent) | What happens |
   |---|---|---|
   | screen (1) | 0 Line | `SetFormation(0)` → **3 Attack Column** (bug) |
   | attack (3), < 4 infantry regiments | 0 Line | → **3 Attack Column** (bug) |
   | attack (3), ≥ 4 infantry regiments | 1 Double Line | already 1 after deployment: nothing; after Whiskey changed `formationordered`: → 1 Double Line |
   | assault (4), brigade | 3 Attack Column | gate false (`formation` = 3 = `num`): **no order ever** |
   | assault (4), division and up | 2 Successive Lines | `SetFormation(2)` → 1; exits while `formationordered` = 1, else **1 Double Line** (bug) |
   | assault (4), cavalry group | 1 Double Line | as attack ≥ 4 |
   | defend (2) | — | excluded |

   The research note's table (it assumed `formation != num` on every row) and this plan's first draft (it assumed `formation` = 0) are both superseded by this table. Whiskey's own mapping (`CommandFormationWritePolicy.VanillaSetFormationIndexForGroup`, `CommandPostureExecutor.cs:256-267`) is correct. This plan's BC9 "Formations" note states vanilla's intended screen formation; this table gives the effective one.
6. **The tug-of-war.** Whiskey's Formation write (`OrderExecutorRuntime.IssueFormation` → `SetFormation`) sets `formationordered` to its target; in the next side-stat cycle vanilla's pass (finding 5) orders its own value whenever the group's stance row gives a `num` that differs from Whiskey's and passes the gate; the #68 Postfix then sees `groupformation` (written only on courier delivery, 125396-125400) differ from its own target and orders again (its cooldown 0.05 / 0.1 doctrine-h, `CommandPostureExecutor.cs:210-251`). Each delivery is a full re-lay (`ClearTargets`, regiment formation, re-path of every slot; 91960-91964). Under Whiskey's stance mapping (`TacticalCommandTaskToStanceMapper.cs:51-76`: Screen/Probe/Scout → 1; AttackObjective/SupportAttack/FixEnemy → 3; every holding, reserve and movement task → 2; the #45 scorer view `:87-105` lifts HoldObjective/HoldChoke to 3 at a visible weak point), a Whiskey-owned group is re-ordered by vanilla's pass exactly when its stance is not 2 and Whiskey's target W is neither vanilla's `num` n nor `SetFormation`'s mapping of it (W ∉ {n, map(n)}, map 0 → 3, 1 → 1, 2 → 1; n = 3 never passes the gate, finding 4). Under Whiskey's stance mapping that is, at stance 3: the assault row for a brigade with ≥ 4 infantry regiments (3 against 1); the assault row for a division (2 against 1, or against 0 → 3 below 4 infantry regiments); a cavalry group on an attack task or the assault row (1 against 0 → 3 Attack Column: `groupcountperunittype[0]` is 0 for cavalry, 120310); an attacking division with fewer than 4 infantry regiments (1 against 0); a frontage-starved hold lifted to stance 3 with ≥ 4 infantry regiments (0 against 1). Screens and small attacking brigades do not conflict: Whiskey's 0 is vanilla's own `num`, so once Whiskey's first write sets `formationordered` = 0 the gate is false; only one race remains, at the first settled cycle, because vanilla's pass runs before #68's Postfix (one extra re-lay per stance change). Groups on stance-2 tasks (holds, reserves, movement) conflict only while vanilla's own cascade holds them at stance 1, 3 or 4 (the mapper's note, `:85-86`), by the same rule. Derived from the code; smoke counts the flips (item 2.37).
7. **Slot order.** `GetHierarchyTree` (92720-92746) sorts a group's direct children ascending by `GetTotalUnitStrength(child, 1000)` (92698-92712: summed headcount, artillery × 1000) when `sortunits` is true (false only for a group whose path was interrupted, 91984-91987), at every `SetGroupFormation` and inside `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel` (three passes per `SetGroupFormation`, 91961-91963, each walking up to four levels, 92579-92616). Double Line fills the front row with infantry in that order until half the total width (92316-92331), so the **weakest** regiments form the first line; Successive Lines puts the weakest in front (92376-92421); Line puts the weakest on the outside (92258-92273). Experience, morale and fatigue never enter. Casualties re-sort slots at the next group order.
8. **Supporting-line distance.** Second lines stand 10 m (Double Line) or 5 m (Successive Lines) behind the first (finding 1); the reserve link is 10 m (`unitlinkdistance`, unitprefs 1068-1069). These are global unitprefs (parsed 53302-53333) that also lay out the player's units. Vanilla runs no terrain validation on layout slots beyond `adjustbyterrainshape`'s bend (92054, 92089-92094).
9. **Refused flank.** Whiskey requests a refuse only for holding tasks with a visible enemy within 250 m or in melee (`CommandPostureExecutor.cs:810, 848-853, 908-926`) and the executor wheels only with no enemy in range (`:233-248`); BC11 decision 2 is the fix (one path: `PostureSense.RefuseFor` → the intent's `Refuse` → `CommandFormationWritePolicy.Decide` → `Wheel` or `RefuseInPlace`). `Wheel` keeps the group's own `groupformation` (`OrderExecutorRuntime.cs`, `Wheel(…, unit.groupformation)`). The vanilla skew is `SkewedPosition` (78109-78168), applied per child at each level (92097, 92137, 92184).
10. **Batteries and cavalry.** Double Line puts a brigade's batteries on both ends of the front row (92297-92314) and its cavalry in the current row (normally the second). `BuildGroupOffsetPositions` holds five flip tests: `array[1] / 2` (92251, cavalry in Line), `array[0] / 2` (92268, infantry in Line), and three `array[2] / 2` (92309 artillery in Double Line, **92351 cavalry in Double Line**, 92398 artillery in Successive Lines). The cavalry loop (92333-92357) resets `num5` to 0 (92335), counts only cavalry placed, and tests `num5 >= array[2] / 2` (the artillery count) on every child iteration, cavalry or not. With no artillery the test is `0 >= 0`, true at the first iteration, so the direction turns to −1 at once: every cavalry child fills from the right end of its row (or every one after the first, when the first child is cavalry). With artillery `a`, the flip comes after `a / 2` cavalry. The intended test, as in the other loops, is the loop's own type count, `array[1]`. Line puts all batteries in the centre (92275-92281). Whiskey moves only whole artillery groups (`TacticalBattleLinePlanner.ArtilleryLineDistance` 650 m, `TacticalBattleLinePlanner.cs:71`); attached batteries follow vanilla's slot.
11. **Corps-level mirror.** Levels 1 and 2 place a child at `−offset.x` (92086, 92126); level 3 at `+offset.x` (92176). The skew uses `+offset.x` at all three levels (92097, 92137, 92184). So when an order is given three levels above the regiments (a corps), each brigade's regiments are laid out mirror-image to a brigade- or division-level order, and a refuse bends the wrong regiments at that level. Derived from the code; step 0 confirms it in game.
12. **Depth limit.** `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel` walks at most three levels below the called group (92572-92628), so an army-level group order does not re-form regiments four levels down. Recorded; BC14 issues no army-level Formation write.
13. **W&L.** The player's command is `permanentlydetached` in battle (85321-85336), so it is dropped from its parent's hierarchy (119865) and skipped by the placement loops (92076, 92101-92108): the parent's line closes up without it. `CheckCurrentOrderUpdate` widens any zone to the parent group (8398-8428) and stores `DLC_WL.givenorder` (8648); the panel shows `destinationname` inside its tooltip text (8606-8636). Vanilla otherwise only logs `destinationname` (8313, 8390) and writes and reads it as one line of the W&L save (45117, 45470): it is never compared. Whiskey's dispatch passes a 100 × 50 m zone and no slot or formation (`TacticalPlayerDispatchRuntime.cs:61-68`).
14. **Lanes.** Whiskey's lane index is the node's position in the command list (`CommandDoctrineAssignment.cs:40-56`), which follows the command-tree sort, unit type descending then Unity instance id (`CommandTreeBuilder.cs:243-250`), not ground or quality; with 6 or more nodes the first and last become flank guards (`:84-109`). The main effort among direct children is `OwnStrengthBucket × max(1, 4 − FlankExposureBucket)` (`DirectChildAllocator.cs:446`), with no quality input.

### Decisions

1. **Doctrinal formation targets (`GroupFormationDoctrine.Target`, pure; gap 1).** One function replaces `TargetFormationForTask` for both Formation sources. Inputs: task, echelon (brigade = unittyp 14, division and up ≥ 15), the group's subtree infantry regiment count (`UnitRecord.InfantryRegiments`, vanilla's `groupcountperunittype[0]`, finding 3), cavalry and artillery child counts (snapshot D5 tree), the node's assigned frontage from `TacticalBattleLinePlan` and the group's own width (`UnitRecord.GroupWidth`), the closest Visible enemy's distance, the side's stance (BC9) and the BC10 route flag. Table:

   | Task | Brigade, ≥ 4 infantry regiments | Brigade, < 4 | Division and up | Cavalry group |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | AttackObjective, SupportAttack, FixEnemy | 1 Double Line | 0 Line | 1 Double Line (brigades abreast, each in two lines) | 1 Double Line |
   | AttackObjective with the closest Visible enemy within `AssaultRangeMeters` = 500 m (the assault, formed on the approach) | 3 Attack Column | 3 Attack Column | 2 Successive Lines | 1 Double Line |
   | HoldObjective, HoldChoke, GuardFlank, Consolidate, FallBackToLine, Delay, FormUp | 1 Double Line | 0 Line | 1 Double Line | 0 Line |
   | ReserveWait, ReleaseReserve | 3 Attack Column (closed, quick to move) | 3 Attack Column | 2 Successive Lines | 1 Double Line |
   | AdvanceToAssembly, RecoverStuckOrder | 1 Double Line (BC10's route sets 4 March Column while routed) | 0 Line | 1 Double Line | 4 March Column beyond 400 m, else 1 |
   | Screen, Probe, Scout | 0 Line (march column → 0, as today) | 0 Line | 0 Line | 0 Line |

   *Assault timing:* 500 m is outside rifle range, so the column forms on the approach; `CommandFormationWritePolicy.Decide` refuses a formation change for a group in combat (`combatstatussubordinates` > 0), so a group that meets the enemy before 500 m keeps its line (no re-lay under fire). *Frontage-starved holds:* a holding task whose assigned frontage is at least `FrontageLineRatio` = 1.4 × the group's estimated double-line width (half its single-line width plus the row gap) takes 0 Line, so the line covers its sector (reason `frontage-line`). *Defender:* the assault row never applies to a Defender (no AttackObjective, BC9). *BC10:* the route override (`ColumnGroupFormation` = 4 while routed or waiting for the column) stays ahead of this table. *Artillery groups* keep their current formation. Every Formation intent still passes `CommandFormationWritePolicy.Decide` (settled, out of contact and cover, cooldown). Where vanilla's pass acts and its stance row has an intent, the table agrees with that intent except finding 6's conflict rule, which decision 2 settles.
2. **One owner per group per tick, through vanilla's own exit (gap 2, part of A2).** A group is *Whiskey-owned* this tick when the posture pipeline holds a doctrine state for it (in the AI pool, `PostureLedger.TryResolveState`), Commander Mode is Active and `Enable Doctrinal Group Formations` is on; vanilla owns every other group. `VanillaFormationPassPatch` (A2; the user approved it 2026-10-15: "Approve (Recommended)") sets a call-depth flag around vanilla's `AdjustGroupFormations` (a non-skipping Prefix; cleared by a `Priority.First` Postfix, so it is already clear when #68's `Priority.Last` Postfix issues Whiskey's own `SetFormation` calls, and again by a Finalizer if vanilla threw). While the flag is set, a non-skipping Prefix on `BattleUnits.SetFormation(GameObject, int, bool)` rewrites `newformation` for a Whiskey-owned group to `VanillaSetFormationIndexForGroup(formationordered)`, the UI index that maps back to the group's current `formationordered`; vanilla's own `exitifsameformation` test (passed `true` at 5927; 91026-91029) then returns without an order. `unitsused` is never touched and every group still gets its `UpdateGroupStrength` refresh (5890 runs before the `SetFormation` call, in the same iteration), so the live fields `CommandFormationWritePolicy.Decide` reads stay current. Whiskey's arbiter (#68) issues the only formation order an owned group gets. A `TacticalFormationFlip` Observation row counts a group whose `groupformation` changes twice within 0.1 doctrine-h (the tug-of-war signature), for smoke 2.37. *While A2 is not active (before step 2 lands it, or with `Enable Vanilla Formation Pass Fix` off):* a Whiskey-owned group whose target conflicts (finding 6's rule, evaluated on its live `AiStance`) takes vanilla's intended `num` n instead, the one value that closes vanilla's gate without its index bug; every other row keeps its doctrinal target. At stance 3 this means: with ≥ 4 infantry regiments in the subtree, the assault row (brigade or division), a frontage-starved hold and a reserve held at 3 take 1 Double Line; an attacking or assaulting division below 4 infantry regiments takes 0 Line; a cavalry group on an attack task, on the assault row or held at 3 takes 0 Line; screens, small attacking brigades, a frontage-starved hold below 4 infantry regiments and a small brigade's assault column keep their doctrinal 0 Line or 3 Attack Column.
3. **Vanilla's index bug (gap 5, BUG-TAC-024, the other half of A2).** For a group vanilla owns, the same `SetFormation` Prefix, under the same flag, rewrites `newformation` from the group formation id vanilla meant to the UI index that maps back to it (`VanillaSetFormationIndexForGroup`: 0 → 5, 1 → 1, 2 → 3, 3 → 0, 4 → 4). Only groups (unittyp ≥ 14), values 0-4; every other caller of `SetFormation` (the UI, Whiskey's executor) is untouched. **Effect:** screens and small attacking groups get Line instead of Attack Column, and assaulting divisions get Successive Lines (from their first settled assault-stance cycle: `formationordered` is 1 after deployment, so the gate passes); assaulting brigades are unchanged, because vanilla's own gate blocks `num` 3 (finding 4). **Commander Modes:** the index fix is a vanilla bug fix and runs in every Commander Mode (Off, MonitorOnly, Active) whenever `Enable Vanilla Formation Pass Fix` is on, like the other vanilla bug fixes (A3, A4, the perk-id and terrain fixes); the ownership rewrite (decision 2) runs only in Active, because it serves Whiskey's own formation writes. The `AGENTS.md` § Release mode contract edit names this exception.
4. **Regiment order by fitness (gap 3).** `SlotQualityOrderPatch` (R1), a Postfix on `BattleUnits.GetHierarchyTree(Regiment, bool, bool, bool, bool)` (92720), acts only on a **brigade's regiment children** (the tree's group is unittyp 14 and its children are regiments): child groups at division and corps level keep vanilla's order, so no brigade is sent laterally across its neighbours (decision 10 orders brigades by ground). Conditions: `sortunits` true, at least 2 children, the brigade AI-controlled (not `PlayerOwned`, not the W&L player's command), the flag on. It re-orders the returned tree's three parallel arrays (`attachedunit`, `attachedunitreg`, `unittyp`) by `SlotFitness.Order`:
   - *Fitness* (own state only, from the side's snapshot): `(0.6 + 0.4 × experience01) × (max(morale, 0.8 × battle-start morale) / battle-start morale) × (1 − 0.5 × fatigue01) × (strength / the largest sibling's strength)`. The damped morale term moves only once morale falls below 80 % of its battle-start value. Weights are pinned by tests and tuned by smoke.
   - *Order:* one stable insertion pass over the brigade's previous order (BattleScope; vanilla's order the first time): walking from the front, a child moves ahead of its predecessor only when its fitness is at least `PromoteRatio` = 1.15 × the predecessor's. The pass is deterministic for any number of children, and with unchanged inputs it changes nothing.
   - *Freeze:* while the brigade or any of its regiments has `ReceivedFire`, is in melee or is close-engaged (`PostureSense.CloseEngaged`), the stored order is returned unchanged, so no line swaps under fire.
   - *Result per layout:* Double Line: the best regiments form the first line and the rest the second; Successive Lines: the best leads; Line: the best stand on the outer flanks (the first-placed child goes outermost, 92258-92273). Artillery and cavalry keep their type rules (finding 10).
   - *Cost:* the order is cached per (brigade id, snapshot tick id), so the repeated `GetHierarchyTree` calls inside `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel` (finding 7) cost a dictionary lookup; scope `tactical.patch.slot-quality-order` (2.0 ms). A regiment missing from the snapshot keeps vanilla's position. Other callers (the UI's count reads at 219039-220565) only read counts.
5. **Supporting lines at a real distance (gap 4).** `GroupLayoutPatch` (R2), a Postfix on the private `BattleUnits.BuildGroupOffsetPositions(HierarchyTree, float, int, int)` (92203), for an AI-controlled group whose overall formation is 1 (Double Line) or 2 (Successive Lines), moves every row `r` ≥ 1 back by `r × (gap − vanilla gap)` (`unitoffsetposition[k].z`, +z backward) and grows `groupdepth` by `(rows − 1) × (gap − vanilla gap)`. Gaps (back of one row to the front of the next, finding 1): `BrigadeSupportMeters` = 100 m for a brigade's second line, `DivisionSupportMeters` = 200 m between a division's rows, `CorpsSupportMeters` = 300 m between a corps' division rows (historically 100-300 m, outside the first line's beaten zone); each level's gap applies only to its own rows, so nesting does not double it. Attack Column (3) keeps vanilla's 2.5 m (a column is meant to be dense). *Terrain and zone clamp (`SupportLineClamp`, pure):* `SetGroupFormation`'s non-skipping context Prefix records the ordered group, its `targetpos` (or the front point vanilla derives when it is default, 92044) and `manualfinalrotation`; when the Postfix runs for the ordered group itself (the last layout of the call, 92030), the whole tree's offsets are known, so it composes each pushed row's world centre and both ends (levels 1-2 at `−x`, level 3 as finding 11 and A4 leave it) and shrinks that row's extra gap to the largest value at which the centre and both ends are passable on BC10's `BattleTerrainGrid` (river 4 impassable unless frozen; inside the map) and, during deployment (the group's side AI instance, `ArmyEvidenceBuilder.ResolveAiBattleForAlliance`, has `state` < 5), inside the own deployment zone (`frontline2.GetClosestPointInDeploymentZone`, 102819, within 5 m of the point); never below vanilla's gap. Applying at deployment too means no second line marches back after the first order. The player's units keep vanilla's global unitprefs.
6. **One refuse path (gap 6).** BC14 adds no refuse code. BC11 decision 2 is the only path. A source-pin test finds every `SetGroupFormation(` call in `src/WhiskeyRealism` and requires its 9th argument (`refuseflank`) to be `-1` (or omitted) everywhere except `OrderExecutorRuntime.Wheel`, and `RefuseFlank` values to be set only by `PostureSense.RefuseFor`. Its tests check that the wheel's refuse keeps the group's doctrinal formation (a Double Line refuses both rows, finding 9) and that A4 makes a corps-level refuse bend the threatened side.
7. **Batteries and cavalry (gap 7).**
   - *Cavalry side flip (BUG-TAC-025):* `CavalryFlankFlipFixPatch` (A3; the user approved it 2026-10-15: "Approve (Recommended)"), a Transpiler on `BuildGroupOffsetPositions` that changes the array index of the cavalry flip test (92351) from 2 to 1, so the cavalry splits half to each end of its row like the other loops.
   - *Batteries by ground:* the artillery doctrine producer computes, for each AI battery (unittyp 2) of a settled AI brigade on a holding task (HoldObjective, HoldChoke, GuardFlank, Consolidate, Delay), `BatterySiting.Best`: within `SiteRadiusMeters` = 150 m of the battery's vanilla slot and not ahead of the brigade's front line, the BC12 picture cell with the highest crest prominence (at least 3 m) whose 30° cone toward the threat axis is clear of own units for 200 m and which lies at least `IntervalMeters` = 40 m laterally from every own infantry regiment's frontage (an interval); with no such cell the battery keeps its slot. *The sited cell becomes the battery's slot:* the result is stored (BattleScope) in the brigade's frame, relative to the brigade's front centre and facing at siting time, and `GroupLayoutPatch` writes it into the battery's `unitoffsetposition` whenever the brigade is laid out again with a front centre within 60 m and a facing within 15° of the stored ones (the context Prefix gives the ordered group's target, decision 5). A re-lay (a facing wheel, a leaf-slot move, vanilla's pass) therefore puts the battery back on the crest instead of sending it back and forth; the per-battery Move (`IntentFlags.ArtilleryFirePosition | PerRegiment`, the executor) is used only for the first placement after a siting. A brigade moved or turned beyond those bounds drops the stored result, and the siting runs again once it settles. When a division or corps is ordered, the brigade's own front centre is not known when its layout is built, so the battery takes vanilla's slot and the per-battery Move re-sites it once the brigade settles. Whole artillery groups: `ArtilleryLineTarget` (650 m) moves to the best crest cell of BC12's `TacticalTerrainLinePlanner.BestLine(…crest…)` within 300 m of it, else stays.
   - *Cavalry on the flanks:* decision 10's lane order gives cavalry nodes the outermost lanes and the flank-guard duty first.
8. **Scope of each change.** Decisions 4, 5, 7 (siting) and 11 act only on AI-controlled groups (`TacticalWriteGuard.CanWrite` on the group; a tree with a `PlayerOwned` child is left as vanilla lays it), and none writes a player unit. A3 and A4 are vanilla bug fixes: like the perk-id and terrain fixes they apply to **every group, the human player's own layouts included** (a normal campaign's player groups and the W&L player's command), which the approval table states.
9. **Corps-level mirror (gap 8, BUG-TAC-026): verify, then fix.** *Step 0 (record only):* `LayoutObserverPatch`, a Postfix on `SetGroupFormation(Regiment, …)`, records for every brigade laid out by the call the left-to-right order of its regiments as placed against the order of its layout columns, with the ordered group's depth above the regiments. The placed position is the regiment's `GetPosition()` when the call's `immediateplacement` is true (the deployment calls at 85750, 85859 and 86255 place through `SetNewPosition`, 92101-92104, 92188-92191, 131303-131363, which does not write `lastsetwaypointposition`) and its `lastsetwaypointposition` otherwise (written by `RegimentSetPath`, 131254, 131258), projected on the brigade's right axis. One `TacticalLayoutMirror` Observation row per call (`depth`, `brigades`, `agree`, `reversed`, `immediate`). It also records once per battle each AI group's `formation`, `formationordered` and `groupformation` (confirming finding 4). *Rule (relative, so it does not depend on which way column 0 points):* A4 is built when depth-3 rows **disagree** with depth 1-2 rows on lateral order: at least 80 % of depth-1-2 brigades `agree` and at least 80 % of depth-3 brigades are `reversed`, or the other way round, with at least 10 rows at each depth. If depth 3 matches depths 1-2 by the same 80 % measure, step 1 is not built and BUG-TAC-026 is closed as not reproduced. With fewer than 10 rows at a depth after the session, or neither pattern at 80 %, the result is recorded as inconclusive: A4 stays unbuilt and item 2.42 stays open. *Step 1:* `CorpsLayoutMirrorFixPatch` (A4; the user approved it 2026-10-15: "Approve if confirmed (Recommended)", so it is built only if step 0 is conclusive at the 80 % rule), a Transpiler on `SetGroupFormation(Regiment, …)` that negates the level-3 placement's lateral argument (the `offset.x` passed to `GetAngleTerrainPosXY` at 92176), leaving the level-3 skew (92184) as it is; a Postfix cannot do this, because negating the stored offsets would also flip the skew. After A4, smoke checks that the order preview drawn with `showmovementoptions` agrees with where the regiments go.
10. **Lanes by ground and quality (gap 10).** `LaneOrder.Rank` (pure) orders a side's command nodes by their lateral position on the side's `TacticalFrontAxis` (`(x − OriginX) × RightX + (z − OriginZ) × RightZ`), left to right, and `CommandDoctrineAssignment` uses that rank as the lane index in place of the command-list position. Hysteresis: two nodes swap ranks only when their lateral positions have crossed by at least `LaneSwapMeters` = 100 m. Flank-guard duty (6 or more nodes) goes to the outermost node on each side, preferring a cavalry node within one rank of the end. The main-effort pick multiplies its score by `QualityFactor` = 0.75 + 0.5 × the node's mean regiment fitness (0-1, decision 4's formula), so between equal strength buckets the better division leads. `CommandTreeBuilder`'s sort stays (tree determinism). All inputs are own positions and own state; the front axis is BC2's perceived axis.
11. **The W&L player's slot (gap 9).** Never moving the player:
    - `LayoutReplica.Slot` (pure) reproduces vanilla's layouts 0/1/2 (findings 1, 7, 10) for the player's parent's direct children **including** the player's detached command, in vanilla's order for child groups (decision 4 re-orders only regiments) and the parent's target formation (decision 1), from snapshot widths (`GroupWidth`), with `LayoutSpacing` taken from decision 5's gaps when the parent is AI-controlled and the flag is on (vanilla's otherwise). It returns every child's row and offset, the player's row, column and neighbours. Including the player's width can move a sibling to another row (Double Line fills the front row up to half the total width, 92316-92331), so the replica's output is used whole.
    - `GroupLayoutPatch` (R3), for the player's parent only, writes **every** sibling's `unitoffsetposition` (and `row`, `column`, `groupwidth`, `groupdepth`) from the replica's output, so both the hole and the rows are the replica's. It writes no player unit.
    - The executor's ancestor dispatch (`TacticalPlayerDispatchRuntime.Emit`) appends the slot to `destinationName`: e.g. "Peach Orchard — first line, left of Barksdale's Brigade, double line" (row: first / second / third line; side: left of / right of the neighbour, by name from the snapshot; formation name from finding 1). The text has no line break (it is saved as one line, 45117). The zone and position stay as vanilla sets them (the parent's sector), so W&L order compliance is unchanged.
12. **BC13 promotion inputs (BC14 owns the change).** BC14 adds inputs to tiered producers (the posture and facing-pulse formation target, the non-point-blank artillery producer's siting), so `BattleLod` gains promotion triggers and BC13's decision 5 now reads "the list as amended by BC14":
    - (g) the group's composition signature changed (subtree infantry regiments, cavalry and artillery child counts);
    - (h) its assigned frontage or its `GroupWidth` changed by at least 10 %;
    - (i) the closest Visible enemy crossed `AssaultRangeMeters` (500 m) in either direction;
    - (j) a battery-siting input changed for one of its batteries: an own regiment entered or left the battery's 150 m siting radius, or the side's front axis turned by at least 15°.
    BC13's equivalence test gains a case per trigger, including a casualty that drops a brigade below 4 infantry regiments.
13. **Rollback flags** (`Tactical`, all default true): `Enable Doctrinal Group Formations` (decision 1, and decision 2's flip row), `Enable Vanilla Formation Pass Fix` (A2: ownership and index fix), `Enable Slot Quality Order` (decision 4), `Enable Supporting Line Distance` (decision 5), `Enable Cavalry Flank Flip Fix` (A3), `Enable Battery And Cavalry Siting` (decision 7's siting and battery slot), `Enable Corps Layout Mirror Fix` (A4, after step 0), `Enable W&L Slot Dispatch` (decision 11), `Enable Lane Order By Ground` (decision 10). The step-0 observer runs under `Enable Tactical Observer`.

namespace WhiskeyRealism.Tactical.Operations {
